### [1] [Co-designing a Social Robot for Newcomer Children's Cultural and Language Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26674)
*Neil Fernandes,Tehniyat Shahbaz,Emily Davies-Robinson,Yue Hu,Kerstin Dautenhahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过共同设计方法探索社交机器人Maple在新移民儿童语言文化学习中的应用，总结了领域挑战、文化归属问题、SAR角色认知及初步设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新移民儿童面临宿主国语言习得障碍，现有扫盲项目受限于人员不足、混合能力班级和有限接触时间。虽然社交辅助机器人(SARs)在教育领域显示出潜力，但在这种社会情感敏感环境中的应用仍未被充分探索。

Method: 采用共同设计研究方法，与项目导师和协调员合作，探索社交机器人Maple的设计空间。通过专家参与的方式收集见解。

Result: 贡献包括：(1)总结四个反复出现的领域挑战；(2)关于文化定位和社区归属与机器人关系的讨论；(3)专家基础的SAR在文化和语言学习中角色认知讨论；(4)将SAR整合到课堂的初步设计指南。

Conclusion: 这些专家基础的见解为与新移民儿童及其家庭的迭代设计和评估奠定了基础，为SAR在敏感教育环境中的应用提供了理论框架和实践指导。

Abstract: Newcomer children face barriers in acquiring the host country's language and literacy programs are often constrained by limited staffing, mixed-proficiency cohorts, and short contact time. While Socially Assistive Robots (SARs) show promise in education, their use in these socio-emotionally sensitive settings remains underexplored. This research presents a co-design study with program tutors and coordinators, to explore the design space for a social robot, Maple. We contribute (1) a domain summary outlining four recurring challenges, (2) a discussion on cultural orientation and community belonging with robots, (3) an expert-grounded discussion of the perceived role of an SAR in cultural and language learning, and (4) preliminary design guidelines for integrating an SAR into a classroom. These expert-grounded insights lay the foundation for iterative design and evaluation with newcomer children and their families.

</details>


### [2] [A Deep Reinforcement Learning Framework for Closed-loop Guidance of Fish Schools via Virtual Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28200)
*Takato Shibayama,Hiroaki Kawashima*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于深度强化学习的虚拟智能体引导鱼群集体运动框架，在仿真中训练后部署到真实鱼群实验中，评估了视觉参数和群体规模对引导效果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究生物群体集体运动的引导机制，既是理解社会互动规则的基础科学问题，也是开发动物管理自动化系统的实际需求。当前需要能够实时与活体个体交互的智能引导系统。

Method: 提出深度强化学习框架，使用近端策略优化（PPO）在仿真中训练虚拟智能体的控制策略，然后部署到真实物理实验中与红鼻剪刀鱼（Petitella bleheri）进行实时交互。设计了复合奖励函数来平衡方向引导与社会凝聚力，以应对活体个体的随机行为。系统评估了视觉参数（背景颜色、刺激大小）和群体规模（5条和8条鱼）对引导效果的影响。

Result: 系统评估表明：白色背景和较大的刺激尺寸能最大化物理实验中的引导效果。在群体规模方面，系统能有效引导5条鱼的群体，但当群体规模增加到8条时，引导能力显著下降。这突显了在较大群体中维持人工影响的挑战。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了深度强化学习在生物群体自动化引导方面的潜力，同时揭示了群体规模扩大时维持人工影响的挑战。为理解集体运动机制和开发智能动物管理系统提供了新方法。

Abstract: Guiding collective motion in biological groups is a fundamental challenge in understanding social interaction rules and developing automated systems for animal management. In this study, we propose a deep reinforcement learning (RL) framework for the closed-loop guidance of fish schools using virtual agents. These agents are controlled by policies trained via Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) in simulation and deployed in physical experiments with rummy-nose tetras (Petitella bleheri), enabling real-time interaction between artificial agents and live individuals. To cope with the stochastic behavior of live individuals, we design a composite reward function to balance directional guidance with social cohesion. Our systematic evaluation of visual parameters shows that a white background and larger stimulus sizes maximize guidance efficacy in physical trials. Furthermore, evaluation across group sizes revealed that while the system demonstrates effective guidance for groups of five individuals, this capability markedly degrades as group size increases to eight. This study highlights the potential of deep RL for automated guidance of biological collectives and identifies challenges in maintaining artificial influence in larger groups.

</details>


### [3] [Bridging the Awareness Gap: Socially Mediated State Externalization for Transparent Distributed Home Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26686)
*Wenzheng Zhao,Manideep Duggi,Fengpei Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 社交中介机器人实时外部化隐藏执行状态，在不影响任务性能的情况下显著提升用户关注度和体验


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式家庭机器人系统常在用户视线外操作，导致状态感知差距，降低信任度、透明度和控制感

Method: 开发了社交中介机器人系统，通过语音更新和视觉进度显示同步外部化隐藏执行状态；采用平衡组内设计研究比较自主隐藏执行与社交中介状态外部化两种条件

Result: 状态外部化显著提升用户任务关注度（15.8%到84.6%），改善感知清晰度、可靠性、刺激性和吸引力；83%参与者偏好外部化条件，且任务完成时间无显著增加

Conclusion: 社交中介状态外部化是设计更透明、可信赖分布式机器人系统的有效架构机制，可在不牺牲性能的情况下提升用户体验

Abstract: Distributed multi-robot systems for the home often require robots to operate out of the user's sight, creating a state awareness gap that can diminish trust and perceived transparency and control. This paper investigates whether real-time, socially mediated state externalization can bridge this gap without compromising task performance. We developed a system where a co-located social mediator robot (Pepper) externalizes the hidden execution states of an out-of-sight mobile manipulator (Stretch~3) for voice-driven object retrieval and delivery, where task-level states are synchronized and externalized through verbal updates and visual progress display. In a counterbalanced within-subject study (N=30), we compared a baseline of Autonomous Hidden Execution against Socially Mediated State Externalization. Our results show that externalization significantly increases user task-focused attention (from 15.8% to 84.6%, p<.001) and substantially improves perceived perspicuity, dependability, stimulation, and attractiveness (all p<.001). Furthermore, 83% of participants preferred the externalized condition, and this improvement in user experience was achieved without a statistically significant increase in end-to-end task completion time (p=.271). The results suggest that socially mediated state externalization is an effective architectural mechanism for designing more transparent and trustworthy distributed robot systems, ultimately enhancing user experience without sacrificing performance in distributed home robot deployments.

</details>


### [4] [Learning Energy-Efficient Air--Ground Actuation for Hybrid Robots on Stair-Like Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26687)
*Jiaxing Li,Wen Tian,Xinhang Xu,Junbin Yuan,Sebastian Scherer,Muqing Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种能量感知强化学习框架，训练单一连续策略协调螺旋桨、轮子和倾斜舵机，实现混合空中-地面机器人的高效越障


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合空中-地面机器人在楼梯状不连续地形上面临权衡：仅用轮子容易在边缘卡住，而飞行模式对小高度增益能耗过高。需要一种能协调多种执行器的高效控制方法

Method: 使用能量感知强化学习框架，在Isaac Lab并行环境中训练单一连续策略，基于本体感知和局部高度扫描，采用硬件校准的推力/功率模型，奖励函数惩罚真实电能消耗

Result: 学习策略发现了推力辅助驱动，融合空中推力和地面牵引力。仿真中能耗比仅用螺旋桨控制低约4倍。在DoubleBee原型机上8cm间隙攀爬任务中，比基于规则的解耦控制器平均功率降低38%

Conclusion: 高效混合驱动可以通过学习自然涌现并部署到硬件，为混合机器人提供了一种能效优化的控制方法

Abstract: Hybrid aerial--ground robots offer both traversability and endurance, but stair-like discontinuities create a trade-off: wheels alone often stall at edges, while flight is energy-hungry for small height gains. We propose an energy-aware reinforcement learning framework that trains a single continuous policy to coordinate propellers, wheels, and tilt servos without predefined aerial and ground modes. We train policies from proprioception and a local height scan in Isaac Lab with parallel environments, using hardware-calibrated thrust/power models so the reward penalizes true electrical energy. The learned policy discovers thrust-assisted driving that blends aerial thrust and ground traction. In simulation it achieves about 4 times lower energy than propeller-only control. We transfer the policy to a DoubleBee prototype on an 8cm gap-climbing task; it achieves 38% lower average power than a rule-based decoupled controller. These results show that efficient hybrid actuation can emerge from learning and deploy on hardware.

</details>


### [5] [SpatialPoint: Spatial-aware Point Prediction for Embodied Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26690)
*Qiming Zhu,Zhirui Fang,Tianming Zhang,Chuanxiu Liu,Xiaoke Jiang,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SpatialPoint框架，将结构化深度信息集成到视觉语言模型中，用于解决具身定位问题——预测可执行的3D点坐标，支持可触摸点和空中点两种目标类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能需要确定在3D空间中的执行位置，现有视觉语言系统主要依赖RGB输入，缺乏显式几何信息，限制了跨场景泛化能力，而RGB-D传感器在机器人中已广泛应用。

Method: 提出SpatialPoint空间感知视觉语言框架，将结构化深度信息集成到视觉语言模型中，生成相机坐标系下的3D坐标。构建了包含260万样本的RGB-D数据集，涵盖可触摸点和空中点的问答对。

Result: 实验表明，将深度信息集成到视觉语言模型中显著提高了具身定位性能。在三个代表性任务中验证了实际机器人部署效果：语言引导机械臂在指定位置抓取、物体放置到目标位置、移动机器人导航到目标位置。

Conclusion: SpatialPoint框架通过显式集成深度信息，有效解决了具身定位问题，提升了3D空间推理能力，在实际机器人任务中展示了良好的应用潜力。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence fundamentally requires a capability to determine where to act in 3D space. We formalize this requirement as embodied localization -- the problem of predicting executable 3D points conditioned on visual observations and language instructions. We instantiate embodied localization with two complementary target types: touchable points, surface-grounded 3D points enabling direct physical interaction, and air points, free-space 3D points specifying placement and navigation goals, directional constraints, or geometric relations. Embodied localization is inherently a problem of embodied 3D spatial reasoning -- yet most existing vision-language systems rely predominantly on RGB inputs, necessitating implicit geometric reconstruction that limits cross-scene generalization, despite the widespread adoption of RGB-D sensors in robotics. To address this gap, we propose SpatialPoint, a spatial-aware vision-language framework with careful design that integrates structured depth into a vision-language model (VLM) and generates camera-frame 3D coordinates. We construct a 2.6M-sample RGB-D dataset covering both touchable and air points QA pairs for training and evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that incorporating depth into VLMs significantly improves embodied localization performance. We further validate SpatialPoint through real-robot deployment across three representative tasks: language-guided robotic arm grasping at specified locations, object placement to target destinations, and mobile robot navigation to goal positions.

</details>


### [6] [Topological Motion Planning Diffusion: Generative Tangle-Free Path Planning for Tethered Robots in Obstacle-Rich Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26696)
*Yifu Tian,Xinhang Xu,Thien-Minh Nguyen,Muqing Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TMPD框架，通过扩散模型生成多模态轨迹候选，结合拓扑记忆解决系留机器人在复杂环境中的终身运动规划问题，避免电缆缠绕。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在水下探索和灾后救援等极端环境中，系留机器人需要连续导航同时避免电缆缠绕。传统规划器因缺乏拓扑感知而难以应对终身规划场景，而拓扑增强图搜索方法在障碍物丰富的环境中面临计算瓶颈，因为候选拓扑类别数量增加。

Method: 提出拓扑运动规划扩散(TMPD)框架：1) 前端使用扩散模型生成跨不同同伦类的运动学可行轨迹候选；2) 后端通过计算广义卷绕数评估拓扑能量，基于累积电缆配置过滤和优化候选轨迹，实现电缆感知的拓扑规划。

Result: 在障碍物丰富的模拟环境中，TMPD达到100%的无碰撞到达率和97.0%的无缠绕率，在几何平滑性和计算效率方面均优于传统拓扑搜索和纯运动学扩散基线方法。包含真实电缆动力学的仿真进一步验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: TMPD通过结合终身拓扑记忆的生成规划框架，有效解决了系留机器人在复杂环境中的连续导航和电缆防缠绕问题，在保持高成功率的同时显著提升了计算效率。

Abstract: In extreme environments such as underwater exploration and post-disaster rescue, tethered robots require continuous navigation while avoiding cable entanglement. Traditional planners struggle in these lifelong planning scenarios due to topological unawareness, while topology-augmented graph-search methods face computational bottlenecks in obstacle-rich environments where the number of candidate topological classes increases. To address these challenges, we propose Topological Motion Planning Diffusion (TMPD), a novel generative planning framework that integrates lifelong topological memory. Instead of relying on sequential graph search, TMPD leverages a diffusion model to propose a multimodal front-end of kinematically feasible trajectory candidates across various homotopy classes. A tether-aware topological back-end then filters and optimizes these candidates by computing generalized winding numbers to evaluate their topological energy against the accumulated tether configuration. Benchmarking in obstacle-rich simulated environments demonstrates that TMPD achieves a collision-free reach of 100% and a tangle-free rate of 97.0%, outperforming traditional topological search and purely kinematic diffusion baselines in both geometric smoothness and computational efficiency. Simulation with realistic cable dynamics further validates the practicality of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [7] [Surface-Constrained Offline Warping with Contact-Aware Online Pose Projection for Safe Robotic Trajectory Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26711)
*Farong Wang,Sai Swaminathan,Fei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两阶段框架，将离线几何嵌入与在线执行调节分离，实现曲面上的稳定重复轨迹执行


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人表面处理任务中，将平面设计的运动基元直接平铺到曲面上会导致几何不一致、穿透、方向不连续和累积漂移等问题

Method: 1. 离线表面约束变形算子：通过非对称微分同胚变形和轴一致方向补全，将标称周期性基元嵌入曲面；2. 在线接触感知投影算子：使用FSR驱动的扰动适应和圆锥方向安全约束，强制执行相对于参考轨迹的有界偏差

Result: 在多个解析曲面族和真实机器人正弦曲面验证中，相比直接平铺方法，改善了几何连续性、减少了大幅方向跳跃、实现了鲁棒的接触保持

Conclusion: 将离线几何重映射与轻量级在线投影解耦，能够在传感器有限反馈下实现稳定可重复的表面嵌入轨迹执行

Abstract: Robotic manipulation tasks that require repeated tool motion along curved surfaces frequently arise in surface finishing, inspection, and guided interaction. In practice, nominal motion primitives are often designed independently of the deployment surface and later reused across varying geometries. Directly tiling such primitives onto nonplanar surfaces introduces geometric inconsistencies, leading to interpenetration, orientation discontinuities, and cumulative drift over repeated cycles. We present a two-stage framework that separates geometric embedding from execution-level regulation. An offline surface-constrained warping operator embeds a nominal periodic primitive onto curved surfaces through asymmetric diffeomorphic deformation of dual-track waypoints and axis-consistent orientation completion, producing a surface-adapted reference trajectory. An online contact-aware projection operator then enforces bounded deviation relative to this reference using FSR-driven disturbance adaptation and a conic orientation safety constraint. Experiments across multiple analytic surface families and real-robot validation on a sinusoidal surface demonstrate improved geometric continuity, reduced large orientation jumps, and robust contact maintenance compared with direct tiling. These results show that decoupling offline geometric remapping from lightweight online projection enables stable and repeatable surface-embedded trajectory execution under sensor-lite feedbacks.

</details>


### [8] [SutureAgent: Learning Surgical Trajectories via Goal-conditioned Offline RL in Pixel Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26720)
*Huanrong Liu,Chunlin Tian,Tongyu Jia,Tailai Zhou,Qin Liu,Yu Gao,Yutong Ban,Yun Gu,Guy Rosman,Xin Ma,Qingbiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SutureAgent：基于目标条件离线强化学习的像素级手术针轨迹预测框架，将针尖视为像素空间中的智能体进行序列决策，利用稀疏标注生成密集奖励信号，在肾脏伤口缝合数据集上比最强基线减少58.6%的平均位移误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接从视觉观测学习运动分布时忽略了相邻运动步骤间的序列依赖性，且稀疏路点标注提供的监督不足，增加了监督学习或模仿学习的难度。需要一种能够捕捉针运动连续性并建模物理合理像素状态转移的方法。

Method: 将基于图像的针轨迹预测建模为序列决策问题，针尖被视为在像素空间中逐步移动的智能体。提出SutureAgent框架：1)通过三次样条插值将稀疏标注转化为密集奖励信号；2)使用观察编码器编码变长视频片段以捕捉局部空间线索和长程时间动态；3)自回归预测由离散方向和连续幅度组成的动作；4)采用带行为克隆正则化的保守Q学习进行离线策略优化。

Result: 在包含50名患者1,158条轨迹的新肾脏伤口缝合数据集上，SutureAgent将平均位移误差降低了58.6%，显著优于最强基线，证明了将针轨迹预测建模为像素级序列动作学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 将针轨迹预测建模为像素级序列决策问题能够有效捕捉运动连续性并建模物理合理的状态转移。SutureAgent通过离线强化学习框架利用稀疏标注生成密集奖励，在有限专家指导下探索合理未来运动路径，显著提升了预测精度。

Abstract: Predicting surgical needle trajectories from endoscopic video is critical for robot-assisted suturing, enabling anticipatory planning, real-time guidance, and safer motion execution. Existing methods that directly learn motion distributions from visual observations tend to overlook the sequential dependency among adjacent motion steps. Moreover, sparse waypoint annotations often fail to provide sufficient supervision, further increasing the difficulty of supervised or imitation learning methods. To address these challenges, we formulate image-based needle trajectory prediction as a sequential decision-making problem, in which the needle tip is treated as an agent that moves step by step in pixel space. This formulation naturally captures the continuity of needle motion and enables the explicit modeling of physically plausible pixel-wise state transitions over time. From this perspective, we propose SutureAgent, a goal-conditioned offline reinforcement learning framework that leverages sparse annotations to dense reward signals via cubic spline interpolation, encouraging the policy to exploit limited expert guidance while exploring plausible future motion paths. SutureAgent encodes variable-length clips using an observation encoder to capture both local spatial cues and long-range temporal dynamics, and autoregressively predicts future waypoints through actions composed of discrete directions and continuous magnitudes. To enable stable offline policy optimization from expert demonstrations, we adopt Conservative Q-Learning with Behavioral Cloning regularization. Experiments on a new kidney wound suturing dataset containing 1,158 trajectories from 50 patients show that SutureAgent reduces Average Displacement Error by 58.6% compared with the strongest baseline, demonstrating the effectiveness of modeling needle trajectory prediction as pixel-level sequential action learning.

</details>


### [9] [Motion as a Sensing Modality for Metric Scale in Monocular Visual-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26740)
*Hadush Hailu,Bruk Gebregziabher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过轨迹设计改善单目视觉惯性里程计（VIO）的尺度恢复：曲率产生的平移加速度是尺度与惯性状态耦合的关键来源，提出基于IMU数据的轻量级激励度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目VIO无法仅从视觉恢复度量尺度，必须通过惯性测量解决尺度问题。现有方法未充分理解轨迹运动如何影响尺度可观测性，需要建立轨迹设计与尺度恢复之间的理论联系。

Method: 1. 进行轨迹依赖的可观测性分析，证明平移加速度（由曲率产生）是尺度与惯性状态耦合的基本来源；2. 基于IMU模型中的重力-加速度不对称性形式化这一关系；3. 推导可观测性矩阵的秩条件；4. 提出可从原始IMU数据计算的轻量级激励度量；5. 在差速驱动机器人上进行控制实验验证。

Result: 实验验证了理论分析：直线运动产生9.2%的尺度误差，圆周运动6.4%，八字形运动4.8%，激励度量跨越四个数量级。轨迹激励越强，尺度恢复精度越高。

Conclusion: 轨迹设计是改善度量尺度恢复的实用机制。曲率产生的平移加速度是尺度可观测性的关键因素，提出的激励度量可用于评估和优化轨迹以提高VIO性能。

Abstract: Monocular visual-inertial odometry (VIO) cannot recover metric scale from vision alone; scale must be resolved through inertial measurements. We present a trajectory-dependent observability analysis showing that translational acceleration, produced by curvature, not constant-speed straight-line travel, is the fundamental source that couples scale to the inertial state. This relationship is formalized through the gravity-acceleration asymmetry in the IMU model, from which we derive rank conditions on the observability matrix and propose a lightweight excitation metric computable from raw IMU data. Controlled experiments on a differential-drive robot with a monocular camera and consumer-grade IMU validate the theory, with straight-line motion yielding 9.2% scale error, circular motion 6.4%, and figure-eight motion 4.8%, with excitation spanning four orders of magnitude. These results establish trajectory design as a practical mechanism for improving metric scale recovery.

</details>


### [10] [LARD 2.0: Enhanced Datasets and Benchmarking for Autonomous Landing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26748)
*Yassine Bougacha,Geoffrey Delhomme,Mélanie Ducoffe,Augustin Fuchs,Jean-Brice Ginestet,Jacques Girard,Sofiane Kraiem,Franck Mamalet,Vincent Mussot,Claire Pagetti,Thierry Sammour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对自主着陆系统中机器学习模型监督训练的数据集限制问题，提出通过增强数据集多样性、优化操作设计域和建立基准测试框架来解决


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主着陆系统中机器学习模型用于目标检测时面临数据集限制的挑战，现有数据集多样性不足，操作设计域存在不现实的着陆场景，缺乏多跑道机场覆盖，需要建立可靠的基准测试框架

Method: 1) 增强数据集多样性：引入BingMap航空图像和飞行模拟器作为新数据源，扩展现有LARD数据集生成器的生成范围；2) 优化操作设计域：解决不现实的着陆场景问题，扩展至多跑道机场覆盖；3) 建立基准测试框架：为复杂多实例环境中的目标检测子任务提供评估框架，并开源相关模型作为性能基准

Result: 提出了增强的数据集生成方法，改进了操作设计域的现实性和覆盖范围，建立了自主着陆系统ML模型的基准测试框架，并提供了开源模型作为性能基准

Conclusion: 通过增强数据集多样性、优化操作设计域和建立基准测试框架，有效解决了自主着陆系统中ML模型训练的数据集限制问题，为自主着陆系统的AI模型开发提供了更可靠的基础

Abstract: This paper addresses key challenges in the development of autonomous landing systems, focusing on dataset limitations for supervised training of Machine Learning (ML) models for object detection. Our main contributions include: (1) Enhancing dataset diversity, by advocating for the inclusion of new sources such as BingMap aerial images and Flight Simulator, to widen the generation scope of an existing dataset generator used to produce the dataset LARD; (2) Refining the Operational Design Domain (ODD), addressing issues like unrealistic landing scenarios and expanding coverage to multi-runway airports; (3) Benchmarking ML models for autonomous landing systems, introducing a framework for evaluating object detection subtask in a complex multi-instances setting, and providing associated open-source models as a baseline for AI models' performance.

</details>


### [11] [Reasoning Systems for Semantic Navigation in Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26753)
*Jonathan Crespo,Ramón Barber,O. M. Mozos,Daniel Beßler,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了两种基于本体模型的语义导航系统解决方案，分别使用关系数据库和KnowRob，并集成到语义导航器中进行了定性和定量比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义导航通过考虑环境语义概念及其关系来规划移动机器人路径，便于与人类交互和理解人类环境。需要建立环境语义表示和推理系统，本文专注于开发基于语义概念的环境模型。

Method: 提出了两种语义导航系统解决方案：1）使用关系数据库实现本体模型；2）基于KnowRob实现本体模型。两种系统都集成了语义导航器，并在移动机器人上进行了实现和验证。

Result: 在定性和定量层面比较了两种系统，展示了在移动机器人上的实现作为概念验证，评估了两种方法的性能和适用性。

Conclusion: 两种基于本体模型的语义导航系统都有效实现了语义导航范式，通过比较分析为语义导航系统的选择提供了参考依据，证明了语义导航在实际机器人应用中的可行性。

Abstract: Semantic navigation is the navigation paradigm in which environmental semantic concepts and their relationships are taken into account to plan the route of a mobile robot. This paradigm facilitates the interaction with humans and the understanding of human environments in terms of navigation goals and tasks. At the high level, a semantic navigation system requires two main components: a semantic representation of the environment, and a reasoner system. This paper is focused on develop a model of the environment using semantic concepts. This paper presents two solutions for the semantic navigation paradigm. Both systems implement an ontological model. Whilst the first one uses a relational database, the second one is based on KnowRob. Both systems have been integrated in a semantic navigator. We compare both systems at the qualitative and quantitative levels, and present an implementation on a mobile robot as a proof of concept.

</details>


### [12] [Beyond Viewpoint Generalization: What Multi-View Demonstrations Offer and How to Synthesize Them for Robot Manipulation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26757)
*Boyang Cai,Qiwei Liang,Jiawei Li,Shihang Weng,Zhaoxin Zhang,Tao Lin,Xiangyu Chen,Wenjie Zhang,Jiaqi Mao,Weisheng Xu,Bin Yang,Jiaming Liang,Junhao Cai,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 多视角演示确实能提升机器人操作性能，而不仅仅是增强跨视角鲁棒性。研究发现多视角数据能突破单视角数据集的缩放限制，并提出RoboNVS框架从单目输入合成新视角视频来弥补真实世界多视角数据稀缺的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多视角演示对机器人操作的实际价值，区分其是真正提升操作性能还是仅增强跨视角鲁棒性。同时解决大规模机器人数据集中多视角数据稀缺，以及真实环境中收集额外视角困难的问题。

Method: 进行系统化研究，通过控制实验量化多视角数据的性能增益、缩放行为和底层机制。在固定和随机背景下测试多视角演示对单视角策略的影响。提出RoboNVS框架，这是一个几何感知的自监督框架，能够从单目输入合成新视角视频。

Result: 多视角演示能持续提升单视角策略的成功率和泛化能力。性能随视角覆盖呈非单调变化，存在有效区间而非简单的"越多越好"。多视角数据能突破单视角数据集的缩放限制，在饱和后继续提升性能上限。RoboNVS生成的数据在仿真和真实环境中都能持续改善下游策略。

Conclusion: 多视角数据确实能改善机器人操作性能，通过促进操作相关的视觉表征、更好地对齐动作头与学习特征分布、减少过拟合。为解决多视角数据稀缺问题，RoboNVS框架提供了一种有效的解决方案，能够从单目输入合成高质量的新视角视频来增强机器人学习。

Abstract: Does multi-view demonstration truly improve robot manipulation, or merely enhance cross-view robustness? We present a systematic study quantifying the performance gains, scaling behavior, and underlying mechanisms of multi-view data for robot manipulation. Controlled experiments show that, under both fixed and randomized backgrounds, multi-view demonstrations consistently improve single-view policy success and generalization. Performance varies non-monotonically with view coverage, revealing effective regimes rather than a simple "more is better" trend. Notably, multi-view data breaks the scaling limitation of single-view datasets and continues to raise performance ceilings after saturation. Mechanistic analysis shows that multi-view learning promotes manipulation-relevant visual representations, better aligns the action head with the learned feature distribution, and reduces overfitting. Motivated by the importance of multi-view data and its scarcity in large-scale robotic datasets, as well as the difficulty of collecting additional viewpoints in real world settings, we propose RoboNVS, a geometry-aware self-supervised framework that synthesizes novel-view videos from monocular inputs. The generated data consistently improves downstream policies in both simulation and real-world environments.

</details>


### [13] [ReMemNav: A Rethinking and Memory-Augmented Framework for Zero-Shot Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26788)
*Feng Wu,Wei Zuo,Wenliang Yang,Jun Xiao,Yang Liu,Xinhua Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReMemNav：一种新型分层导航框架，通过整合全景语义先验和情景记忆来解决零样本物体导航中的空间幻觉、局部探索死锁和语义意图与低级控制脱节问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本物体导航要求智能体在陌生环境中定位未见过的目标物体，无需先验地图或任务特定训练，这仍是一个重大挑战。尽管视觉语言模型（VLMs）为此任务提供了有前景的常识推理能力，但这些模型仍存在空间幻觉、局部探索死锁以及高级语义意图与低级控制脱节的问题。

Method: 提出ReMemNav分层导航框架，整合全景语义先验和情景记忆与VLMs。引入Recognize Anything Model来锚定VLM的空间推理过程。设计基于情景语义缓冲队列的自适应双模态重新思考机制，主动验证目标可见性并使用历史记忆纠正决策以防止死锁。对于低级动作执行，使用深度掩码提取可行动作序列，让VLM选择最优动作映射到实际空间移动。

Result: 在HM3D和MP3D数据集上的广泛评估表明，ReMemNav在成功率和探索效率方面优于现有的免训练零样本基线。具体而言，在HM3D v0.1上SR和SPL分别提高1.7%和7.0%，在HM3D v0.2上分别提高18.2%和11.1%，在MP3D上分别提高8.7%和7.9%。

Conclusion: ReMemNav通过整合全景语义先验、情景记忆和自适应重新思考机制，有效解决了零样本物体导航中的关键挑战，显著提升了导航性能，为免训练导航系统提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Zero-shot object navigation requires agents to locate unseen target objects in unfamiliar environments without prior maps or task-specific training which remains a significant challenge. Although recent advancements in vision-language models(VLMs) provide promising commonsense reasoning capabilities for this task, these models still suffer from spatial hallucinations, local exploration deadlocks, and a disconnect between high-level semantic intent and low-level control. In this regard, we propose a novel hierarchical navigation framework named ReMemNav, which seamlessly integrates panoramic semantic priors and episodic memory with VLMs. We introduce the Recognize Anything Model to anchor the spatial reasoning process of the VLM. We also design an adaptive dual-modal rethinking mechanism based on an episodic semantic buffer queue. The proposed mechanism actively verifies target visibility and corrects decisions using historical memory to prevent deadlocks. For low-level action execution, ReMemNav extracts a sequence of feasible actions using depth masks, allowing the VLM to select the optimal action for mapping into actual spatial movement. Extensive evaluations on HM3D and MP3D demonstrate that ReMemNav outperforms existing training-free zero-shot baselines in both success rate and exploration efficiency. Specifically, we achieve significant absolute performance improvements, with SR and SPL increasing by 1.7% and 7.0% on HM3D v0.1, 18.2% and 11.1% on HM3D v0.2, and 8.7% and 7.9% on MP3D.

</details>


### [14] [SpatialAnt: Autonomous Zero-Shot Robot Navigation via Active Scene Reconstruction and Visual Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26837)
*Jiwen Zhang,Xiangyu Shi,Siyuan Wang,Zerui Li,Zhongyu Wei,Qi Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SpatialAnt是一个零样本视觉语言导航框架，通过物理接地策略和视觉预期机制，在噪声自建场景重建中实现鲁棒导航


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于探索的零样本导航方法依赖高质量人工场景重建，不适用于机器人实际部署。机器人自建重建不完整且噪声大，导致依赖高质量重建的方法性能严重下降

Method: 提出物理接地策略恢复单目重建的绝对度量尺度；引入视觉预期机制，利用噪声点云渲染未来观测，通过反事实推理剪枝与人类指令矛盾的路径

Result: 在R2R-CE上达到66%成功率，RxR-CE上达到50.8%成功率；在Hello Robot上实际部署，在挑战性真实世界环境中达到52%成功率

Conclusion: SpatialAnt有效弥合了不完美自建重建与鲁棒执行之间的差距，在模拟和真实环境中均显著优于现有零样本方法

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) has recently benefited from Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), enabling zero-shot navigation. While recent exploration-based zero-shot methods have shown promising results by leveraging global scene priors, they rely on high-quality human-crafted scene reconstructions, which are impractical for real-world robot deployment. When encountering an unseen environment, a robot should build its own priors through pre-exploration. However, these self-built reconstructions are inevitably incomplete and noisy, which severely degrade methods that depend on high-quality scene reconstructions. To address these issues, we propose SpatialAnt, a zero-shot navigation framework designed to bridge the gap between imperfect self-reconstructions and robust execution. SpatialAnt introduces a physical grounding strategy to recover the absolute metric scale for monocular-based reconstructions. Furthermore, rather than treating the noisy self-reconstructed scenes as absolute spatial references, we propose a novel visual anticipation mechanism. This mechanism leverages the noisy point clouds to render future observations, enabling the agent to perform counterfactual reasoning and prune paths that contradict human instructions. Extensive experiments in both simulated and real-world environments demonstrate that SpatialAnt significantly outperforms existing zero-shot methods. We achieve a 66% Success Rate (SR) on R2R-CE and 50.8% SR on RxR-CE benchmarks. Physical deployment on a Hello Robot further confirms the efficiency and efficacy of our framework, achieving a 52% SR in challenging real-world settings.

</details>


### [15] [SCRAMPPI: Efficient Contingency Planning for Mobile Robot Navigation via Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26995)
*Raj Harshit Srirangam,Leonard Jung,Rohith Poola,Michael Everett*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合哈密顿-雅可比可达性分析与采样规划的方法，为自主机器人提供名义轨迹与应急计划的双重保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人在执行名义行为时容易因意外情况失败，需要应急计划来避免任务完全失败。现有方法要么放松硬约束，要么使用昂贵的采样策略，需要更高效的解决方案。

Method: 将应急可行性要求形式化为可达-避让问题，利用哈密顿-雅可比可达性分析进行认证。在线计算安全集的后向可达集值函数，并通过重采样滚轮与MPPI采样规划器集成，保证硬约束满足同时提高采样效率。

Result: 算法能够在移动机器人对抗规避任务中实时生成名义轨迹和应急计划，模拟和硬件实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过结合可达性分析与采样规划，在保证应急可行性硬约束的同时显著提高了采样效率，为自主机器人提供了可靠的名义行为与应急计划双重保障。

Abstract: Autonomous robots commonly aim to complete a nominal behavior while minimizing a cost; this leaves them vulnerable to failure or unplanned scenarios, where a backup or contingency plan to a safe set is needed to avoid a total mission failure. This is formalized as a trajectory optimization problem over the nominal cost with a safety constraint: from any point along the nominal plan, a feasible trajectory to a designated safe set must exist. Previous methods either relax this hard constraint, or use an expensive sampling-based strategy to optimize for this constraint. Instead, we formalize this requirement as a reach-avoid problem and leverage Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) reachability analysis to certify contingency feasibility. By computing the value function of our safe-set's backward reachable set online as the environment is revealed and integrating it with a sampling based planner (MPPI) via resampling based rollouts, we guarantee satisfaction of the hard constraint while greatly increasing sampling efficiency. Finally, we present simulated and hardware experiments demonstrating our algorithm generating nominal and contingency plans in real time on a mobile robot in an adversarial evasion task.

</details>


### [16] [UMI-Underwater: Learning Underwater Manipulation without Underwater Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27012)
*Hao Li,Long Yin Chung,Jack Goler,Ryan Zhang,Xiaochi Xie,Huy Ha,Shuran Song,Mark Cutkosky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种水下机器人抓取系统，通过自监督数据收集管道自动收集成功的水下抓取演示，并利用基于深度的可供性表示将陆地人类演示知识迁移到水下，克服视觉退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人抓取面临两大挑战：水下图像质量退化且变化大，以及收集多样化水下演示数据成本高昂。需要一种能够利用陆地演示知识并适应水下环境的解决方案。

Method: 系统包含两个核心组件：(1) 自监督数据收集管道，自动收集成功的水下抓取演示；(2) 基于深度的可供性表示，通过几何对齐实现陆地到水下领域的零样本迁移，并训练可供性条件扩散策略生成控制动作。

Result: 在池实验中，该方法提高了抓取性能和背景变化的鲁棒性，能够泛化到仅在陆地数据中见过的物体，性能优于仅使用RGB的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过结合自监督水下数据收集和基于深度的可供性表示跨域迁移，实现了有效的水下机器人抓取系统，克服了水下视觉挑战并减少了数据收集成本。

Abstract: Underwater robotic grasping is difficult due to degraded, highly variable imagery and the expense of collecting diverse underwater demonstrations. We introduce a system that (i) autonomously collects successful underwater grasp demonstrations via a self-supervised data collection pipeline and (ii) transfers grasp knowledge from on-land human demonstrations through a depth-based affordance representation that bridges the on-land-to-underwater domain gap and is robust to lighting and color shift. An affordance model trained on on-land handheld demonstrations is deployed underwater zero-shot via geometric alignment, and an affordance-conditioned diffusion policy is then trained on underwater demonstrations to generate control actions. In pool experiments, our approach improves grasping performance and robustness to background shifts, and enables generalization to objects seen only in on-land data, outperforming RGB-only baselines. Code, videos, and additional results are available at https://umi-under-water.github.io.

</details>


### [17] [Autonomous overtaking trajectory optimization using reinforcement learning and opponent pose estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27207)
*Matej Rene Cihlar,Luka Šiktar,Branimir Ćaran,Marko Švaco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于强化学习的多智能体自主赛车超车轨迹优化方法，融合LiDAR和深度相机数据，使用UKF进行数据融合以提升对手姿态估计精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超车是自动驾驶中最复杂的驾驶操作之一，需要多传感器支持以实现安全轨迹优化和超车效率。现有系统需要更智能的轨迹优化方法来处理多智能体赛车环境中的超车场景。

Method: 采用强化学习机制，结合LiDAR（2D检测算法）和深度相机（YOLO目标检测）识别待超车辆及其姿态。使用UKF融合两种传感器数据以改进对手姿态估计，基于预生成的赛道线数据和传感器输入计算转向角和线速度进行轨迹优化。

Result: 算法在仿真和真实实验中均成功执行超车操作，姿态估计RMSE在(x,y)方向上分别为0.0816米和0.0531米，验证了系统的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的强化学习框架结合多传感器融合技术能够有效优化自主赛车超车轨迹，在姿态估计精度和超车性能方面表现良好，为自动驾驶复杂操作提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Vehicle overtaking is one of the most complex driving maneuvers for autonomous vehicles. To achieve optimal autonomous overtaking, driving systems rely on multiple sensors that enable safe trajectory optimization and overtaking efficiency. This paper presents a reinforcement learning mechanism for multi-agent autonomous racing environments, enabling overtaking trajectory optimization, based on LiDAR and depth image data. The developed reinforcement learning agent uses pre-generated raceline data and sensor inputs to compute the steering angle and linear velocity for optimal overtaking. The system uses LiDAR with a 2D detection algorithm and a depth camera with YOLO-based object detection to identify the vehicle to be overtaken and its pose. The LiDAR and the depth camera detection data are fused using a UKF for improved opponent pose estimation and trajectory optimization for overtaking in racing scenarios. The results show that the proposed algorithm successfully performs overtaking maneuvers in both simulation and real-world experiments, with pose estimation RMSE of (0.0816, 0.0531) m in (x, y).

</details>


### [18] [Design of an In-Pipe Robot with Contact-Angle-Guided Kinematic Decoupling for Crosstalk-Suppressed Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27245)
*Min Yang,Yang Tian,Longchuang Li,Jun Ma,Shugen Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种V形管道检测机器人，采用关节轴与轮分离布局，仅用两个电机实现全轮驱动推进和电动滚动重定向，通过几何传输分析抑制运动耦合，在垂直管道中验证了去耦效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统V形管道检测机器人在紧凑结构中常依赖共享接触或间接驱动，导致强烈的运动学耦合，性能对几何和摩擦变化敏感。需要一种能够提供独立驱动通道、实现可靠推进和快速姿态校正的解决方案。

Method: 采用关节轴与轮分离布局，仅用两个电机实现全轮驱动推进和电动滚动重定向。建立驱动传输矩阵，识别球形轮接触角作为关键几何变量。通过几何传输分析将安装参数映射到接触角、泄漏和效率，提供抑制串扰的结构指导。建立静态稳定性模型，在摩擦不确定性下选择扭簧刚度以确保垂直管道稳定性。

Result: 实验验证了去耦效果：在垂直管道中进行高动态滚动时，推进扭矩几乎保持不变。在包含平面外双弯头的多材料测试平台上，机器人在超过10次独立往返试验中实现了100%的成功率。

Conclusion: 提出的V形管道机器人通过关节轴与轮分离布局实现了物理独立的驱动通道，几何传输分析有效抑制了运动耦合，静态稳定性模型确保了垂直管道中的可靠操作，为紧凑型管道检测机器人提供了高性能解决方案。

Abstract: In-pipe inspection robots must traverse confined pipeline networks with elbows and three-dimensional fittings, requiring both reliable axial traction and rapid rolling reorientation for posture correction. In compact V-shaped platforms, these functions often rely on shared contacts or indirect actuation, which introduces strong kinematic coupling and makes performance sensitive to geometry and friction variations. This paper presents a V-shaped in-pipe robot with a joint-axis-and-wheel-separation layout that provides two physically independent actuation channels, with all-wheel-drive propulsion and motorized rolling reorientation while using only two motors. To make the decoupling mechanism explicit and designable, we formulate an actuation transmission matrix and identify the spherical-wheel contact angle as the key geometric variable governing the dominant roll-to-propulsion leakage and roll-channel efficiency. A geometric transmission analysis maps mounting parameters to the contact angle, leakage, and efficiency, yielding a structural guideline for suppressing crosstalk by driving the contact angle toward zero. A static stability model further provides a stability-domain map for selecting torsion-spring stiffness under friction uncertainty to ensure vertical-pipe stability with a margin. Experiments validate the decoupling effect, where during high-dynamic rolling in a vertical pipe, the propulsion torque remains nearly invariant. On a multi-material testbed including out-of-plane double elbows, the robot achieved a 100% success rate in more than 10 independent round-trip trials.

</details>


### [19] [Robust Global-Local Behavior Arbitration via Continuous Command Fusion Under LiDAR Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27273)
*Mohamed Elgouhary,Amr S. El-Wakeel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROS2仲裁模块融合全局路径跟踪与局部避障控制器，使用PPO训练的门控策略在感知受损条件下实现鲁棒控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化自动驾驶系统需要在全局进度目标与局部安全反应之间协调，特别是在不完美感知和实时约束下。现有系统缺乏在ROS2环境中对控制器级鲁棒性的系统评估。

Method: 提出ROS2原生仲裁模块，融合两个可解释控制器：基于Pure Pursuit的全局参考跟踪控制器和基于LiDAR的Gap Follow反应式控制器。使用PPO训练的策略从紧凑特征观测预测连续门控值，结合实际安全检查生成融合驱动命令。实现轻量级基于采样的预测基线作为对比。

Result: 在可重复的近距通过场景中，评估了随着感知压力增加的安全成功率、失败率以及每步端到端控制器运行时间。使用ROS2损伤协议注入LiDAR噪声、延迟、丢包和虚假短程异常值。

Conclusion: 该研究旨在模块化ROS2设置中进行命令级鲁棒性评估，而非替代规划级交互推理。展示了在感知受损条件下通过仲裁融合实现鲁棒控制的可行性。

Abstract: Modular autonomous driving systems must coordinate global progress objectives with local safety-driven reactions under imperfect sensing and strict real-time constraints. This paper presents a ROS2-native arbitration module that continuously fuses the outputs of two unchanged and interpretable controllers: a global reference-tracking controller based on Pure Pursuit and a reactive LiDAR-based Gap Follow controller. At each control step, both controllers propose Ackermann commands, and a PPO-trained policy predicts a continuous gate from a compact feature observation to produce a single fused drive command, augmented with practical safety checks. For comparison under identical ROS topic inputs and control rate, we implement a lightweight sampling-based predictive baseline. Robustness is evaluated using a ROS2 impairment protocol that injects LiDAR noise, delay, and dropout, and additionally sweeps forward-cone false short-range outliers. In a repeatable close-proximity passing scenario, we report safe success and failure rates together with per-step end-to-end controller runtime as sensing stress increases. The study is intended as a command-level robustness evaluation in a modular ROS2 setting, not as a replacement for planning-level interaction reasoning.

</details>


### [20] [HiFlow: Tokenization-Free Scale-Wise Autoregressive Policy Learning via Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27281)
*Daichi Yashima,Koki Seno,Shuhei Kurita,Yusuke Oda,Komei Sugiura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiFlow是一种无需标记化的粗到细自回归策略，直接处理原始连续动作，通过时间池化构建多尺度动作目标，单阶段端到端训练，在多个机器人任务中超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有粗到细自回归方法依赖离散动作标记器，将连续动作序列映射为码本索引，这种设计继承自图像生成领域，但对于低维连续机器人动作来说，标记化引入了不必要的量化误差和多阶段训练流程。

Method: 提出分层流策略（HiFlow），一种无需标记化的粗到细自回归策略，直接操作原始连续动作。通过简单的时间池化从每个动作块构建多尺度连续动作目标：平均连续动作窗口产生粗粒度摘要，在更细的时间分辨率上进行细化。整个模型单阶段端到端训练，无需单独标记器。

Result: 在MimicGen、RoboTwin 2.0和真实世界环境中的实验表明，HiFlow持续优于现有方法，包括基于扩散的策略和基于标记化的自回归策略。

Conclusion: HiFlow证明了对于低维连续机器人动作，无需标记化的粗到细自回归建模是可行且有效的，消除了量化误差和多阶段训练需求，在多个机器人任务中实现了最先进的性能。

Abstract: Coarse-to-fine autoregressive modeling has recently shown strong promise for visuomotor policy learning, combining the inference efficiency of autoregressive methods with the global trajectory coherence of diffusion-based policies. However, existing approaches rely on discrete action tokenizers that map continuous action sequences to codebook indices, a design inherited from image generation where learned compression is necessary for high-dimensional pixel data. We observe that robot actions are inherently low-dimensional continuous vectors, for which such tokenization introduces unnecessary quantization error and a multi-stage training pipeline. In this work, we propose Hierarchical Flow Policy (HiFlow), a tokenization-free coarse-to-fine autoregressive policy that operates directly on raw continuous actions. HiFlow constructs multi-scale continuous action targets from each action chunk via simple temporal pooling. Specifically, it averages contiguous action windows to produce coarse summaries that are refined at finer temporal resolutions. The entire model is trained end-to-end in a single stage, eliminating the need for a separate tokenizer. Experiments on MimicGen, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-world environments demonstrate that HiFlow consistently outperforms existing methods including diffusion-based and tokenization-based autoregressive policies.

</details>


### [21] [Uni-World VLA: Interleaved World Modeling and Planning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27287)
*Qiqi Liu,Huan Xu,Jingyu Li,Bin Sun,Zhihui Hao,Dangen She,Xiatian Zhu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Uni-World VLA是一个统一的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过交替预测未来帧和规划轨迹实现世界建模与控制的闭环交互，提升自动驾驶在动态交通场景中的适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于世界模型的方法通常先预测未来场景再规划动作，导致开环想象可能偏离实际决策过程。需要更紧密地耦合世界预测与规划，实现更自适应的决策。

Method: 提出统一VLA模型，交替进行未来帧预测和自车动作规划，形成世界建模与控制的闭环交互。同时将单目深度信息融入帧预测，为世界建模提供更强的几何线索。

Result: 在NAVSIM基准测试中，该方法实现了有竞争力的闭环规划性能，同时生成高保真的未来帧预测。结果表明紧密耦合世界预测与规划是扩展VLA驾驶系统的有前景方向。

Conclusion: 通过交替预测和规划的紧密耦合，Uni-World VLA实现了世界建模与控制的闭环交互，为动态交通场景中的自适应决策提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires reasoning about how the environment evolves and planning actions accordingly. Existing world-model-based approaches typically predict future scenes first and plan afterwards, resulting in open-loop imagination that may drift from the actual decision process. In this paper, we present Uni-World VLA, a unified vision-language-action (VLA) model that tightly interleaves future frame prediction and trajectory planning. Instead of generating a full world rollout before planning, our model alternates between predicting future frames and ego actions step by step, allowing planning decisions to be continuously conditioned on the imagined future observations. This interleaved generation forms a closed-loop interaction between world modeling and control, enabling more adaptive decision-making in dynamic traffic scenarios. In addition, we incorporate monocular depth information into frames to provide stronger geometric cues for world modeling, improving long-horizon scene prediction. Experiments on the NAVSIM benchmark show that our approach achieves competitive closed-loop planning performance while producing high-fidelity future frame predictions. These results demonstrate that tightly coupling world prediction and planning is a promising direction for scalable VLA driving systems.

</details>


### [22] [MetaTune: Adjoint-based Meta-tuning via Robotic Differentiable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27313)
*Xiexin Peng,Bingheng Wang,Tao Zhang,Ying Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MetaTune：基于可微分闭环元学习的反馈控制器与扰动观测器联合自动调参框架，通过伴随方法高效计算元梯度，在四旋翼控制中显著提升性能并减少计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扰动观测器的控制方法在增强机器人系统鲁棒性方面表现出潜力，但控制器增益与观测器参数之间的强耦合使得调参过程具有挑战性。现有方法难以实现两者的联合优化。

Method: 提出MetaTune框架，将便携式神经策略与基于可微分系统动力学的物理信息梯度相结合，实现跨任务和工况的自适应增益调整。开发伴随方法，在时间上反向高效计算关于自适应增益的元梯度，直接最小化代价函数，将计算复杂度降至数据时域线性。

Result: 在四旋翼控制实验中，MetaTune相比现有可微分调参方法获得一致性能提升，梯度计算时间减少50%以上。在高保真PX4-Gazebo硬件在环仿真中，学习到的自适应策略在激进飞行速度下平均跟踪误差降低15-20%，在强扰动下提升达40%，且无需微调即可实现零样本仿真到仿真迁移。

Conclusion: MetaTune为控制器和扰动观测器的联合自动调参提供了高效统一的框架，通过可微分闭环元学习和伴随方法实现了性能提升与计算效率的平衡，展示了在复杂机器人系统中的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Disturbance observer-based control has shown promise in robustifying robotic systems against uncertainties. However, tuning such systems remains challenging due to the strong coupling between controller gains and observer parameters. In this work, we propose MetaTune, a unified framework for joint auto-tuning of feedback controllers and disturbance observers through differentiable closed-loop meta-learning. MetaTune integrates a portable neural policy with physics-informed gradients derived from differentiable system dynamics, enabling adaptive gain across tasks and operating conditions. We develop an adjoint method that efficiently computes the meta-gradients with respect to adaptive gains backward in time to directly minimize the cost-to-go. Compared to existing forward methods, our approach reduces the computational complexity to be linear in the data horizon. Experimental results on quadrotor control show that MetaTune achieves consistent improvements over state-of-the-art differentiable tuning methods while reducing gradient computation time by more than 50 percent. In high-fidelity PX4-Gazebo hardware-in-the-loop simulation, the learned adaptive policy yields 15-20 percent average tracking error reduction at aggressive flight speeds and up to 40 percent improvement under strong disturbances, while demonstrating zero-shot sim-to-sim transfer without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [23] [Where-to-Learn: Analytical Policy Gradient Directed Exploration for On-Policy Robotic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27317)
*Leixin Chang,Xinchen Yao,Ben Liu,Liangjing Yang,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于可微分动力学模型分析策略梯度的定向探索方法，通过任务感知、物理引导的指导将智能体导向高奖励区域，加速策略学习


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人控制中，有效探索对于高效高质量的策略学习至关重要。现有方法主要通过最大化策略熵或鼓励访问新状态来激励探索，而不考虑状态的潜在价值，如何引导智能体高效探索更好的轨迹仍然是一个挑战。

Method: 提出一种新的定向探索形式，利用可微分动力学模型的分析策略梯度，注入任务感知、物理引导的指导，从而将智能体导向高奖励区域。

Result: 该方法能够加速和更有效地进行策略学习，通过物理引导的定向探索提高学习效率。

Conclusion: 通过可微分动力学模型的分析策略梯度实现任务感知的定向探索，相比传统基于熵或状态新颖性的探索方法，能更有效地引导智能体探索高价值区域，提升强化学习在机器人控制中的性能。

Abstract: On-policy reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms have demonstrated great potential in robotic control, where effective exploration is crucial for efficient and high-quality policy learning. However, how to encourage the agent to explore the better trajectories efficiently remains a challenge. Most existing methods incentivize exploration by maximizing the policy entropy or encouraging novel state visiting regardless of the potential state value. We propose a new form of directed exploration that uses analytical policy gradients from a differentiable dynamics model to inject task-aware, physics-guided guidance, thereby steering the agent towards high-reward regions for accelerated and more effective policy learning.

</details>


### [24] [D-SPEAR: Dual-Stream Prioritized Experience Adaptive Replay for Stable Reinforcement Learninging Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27346)
*Yu Zhang,Karl Mason*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: D-SPEAR：一种解耦actor和critic采样的双流优先经验回放框架，通过自适应锚机制和Huber损失提升机器人操作任务的强化学习性能与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作任务中，传统的off-policy actor-critic算法（如SAC、TD3）常因经验回放策略忽略actor和critic的不同数据需求而导致策略振荡和性能崩溃。现有方法使用单一回放策略无法同时满足价值学习和策略优化的需求。

Method: 提出D-SPEAR框架：1）解耦actor和critic采样，使用共享回放缓冲区；2）critic采用优先经验回放以高效学习价值函数；3）actor使用低误差转移进行更新以稳定策略优化；4）引入自适应锚机制，基于TD误差变异系数平衡均匀采样和优先采样；5）使用Huber损失函数提升不同奖励尺度下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在robosuite基准测试的Block-Lifting和Door-Opening等挑战性机器人操作任务上，D-SPEAR在最终性能和训练稳定性方面均优于SAC、TD3、DDPG等强基线方法。消融研究证实了actor侧和critic侧回放流的互补作用。

Conclusion: D-SPEAR通过解耦actor和critic的经验回放需求，提供了一种更有效的强化学习训练框架，显著提升了机器人操作任务的性能和稳定性，为解决接触丰富动态、长时程任务中的训练不稳定问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation remains challenging for reinforcement learning due to contact-rich dynamics, long horizons, and training instability. Although off-policy actor-critic algorithms such as SAC and TD3 perform well in simulation, they often suffer from policy oscillations and performance collapse in realistic settings, partly due to experience replay strategies that ignore the differing data requirements of the actor and the critic. We propose D-SPEAR: Dual-Stream Prioritized Experience Adaptive Replay, a replay framework that decouples actor and critic sampling while maintaining a shared replay buffer. The critic leverages prioritized replay for efficient value learning, whereas the actor is updated using low-error transitions to stabilize policy optimization. An adaptive anchor mechanism balances uniform and prioritized sampling based on the coefficient of variation of TD errors, and a Huber-based critic objective further improves robustness under heterogeneous reward scales. We evaluate D-SPEAR on challenging robotic manipulation tasks from the robosuite benchmark, including Block-Lifting and Door-Opening. Results demonstrate that D-SPEAR consistently outperforms strong off-policy baselines, including SAC, TD3, and DDPG, in both final performance and training stability, with ablation studies confirming the complementary roles of the actorside and critic-side replay streams.

</details>


### [25] [Rainbow-DemoRL: Combining Improvements in Demonstration-Augmented Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27400)
*Dwait Bhatt,Shih-Chieh Chou,Nikolay Atanasov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文系统比较了三种利用离线演示数据提升在线强化学习样本效率的方法，发现直接重用离线数据和行为克隆初始化比复杂的离线RL预训练方法更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多种利用离线演示数据提升在线RL样本效率的方法，但缺乏对这些方法的系统性比较，不清楚哪种方法最有效、能否组合使用以及是否有累积效益。

Method: 将现有演示增强RL方法分为三类，进行广泛的实证研究，分析各自的优缺点和组合效果，以分离每种策略的贡献并确定有效的混合组合。

Result: 分析表明，直接重用离线数据和通过行为克隆初始化策略，在提升在线样本效率方面始终优于更复杂的离线RL预训练方法。

Conclusion: 对于提升在线RL样本效率，简单直接的离线数据重用和行为克隆初始化方法比复杂的离线RL预训练更有效，这为实际应用提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Several approaches have been proposed to improve the sample efficiency of online reinforcement learning (RL) by leveraging demonstrations collected offline. The offline data can be used directly as transitions to optimize RL objectives, or offline policy and value functions can first be learned from the data and then used for online finetuning or to provide reference actions. While each of these strategies has shown compelling results, it is unclear which method has the most impact on sample efficiency, whether these approaches can be combined, and if there are cumulative benefits. We classify existing demonstration-augmented RL approaches into three categories and perform an extensive empirical study of their strengths, weaknesses, and combinations to isolate the contribution of each strategy and determine effective hybrid combinations for sample-efficient online RL. Our analysis reveals that directly reusing offline data and initializing with behavior cloning consistently outperform more complex offline RL pretraining methods for improving online sample efficiency.

</details>


### [26] [Agent-Driven Autonomous Reinforcement Learning Research: Iterative Policy Improvement for Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27416)
*Nimesh Khandelwal,Shakti S. Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文通过案例研究展示了智能体在四足机器人强化学习研究中的自主执行能力，从早期粗糙地形实验到最终实现97%成功率，期间智能体执行了代码诊断、奖励函数配置、实验监控等任务，但人类仍提供高层指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索智能体在机器人强化学习研究中的自主执行能力，特别是在四足机器人运动控制这一复杂领域，验证智能体能否在有限人类干预下执行完整的强化学习研究循环。

Method: 采用智能体驱动的自主强化学习方法，在Isaac Lab环境中对12自由度四足机器人进行实验。智能体负责代码阅读、故障诊断、奖励函数和地形配置编辑、任务启动监控、中间指标分析以及实验设计等任务，人类仅提供高层指导。

Result: 通过14轮70多次实验，智能体从早期平均奖励约7的粗糙地形实验，发展到第12轮实验exp063，实现了速度误差0.263和97%的超时成功率（2000次迭代），并在不同GPU上独立复现5次。智能体还自主完成了多项研究决策。

Conclusion: 研究表明智能体能够在机器人强化学习领域实质性执行迭代研究循环，但人类方向指导仍然重要。该案例为自主研究范式在复杂机器人环境中的应用提供了实证和文档记录。

Abstract: This paper documents a case study in agent-driven autonomous reinforcement learning research for quadruped locomotion. The setting was not a fully self-starting research system. A human provided high-level directives through an agentic coding environment, while an agent carried out most of the execution loop: reading code, diagnosing failures, editing reward and terrain configurations, launching and monitoring jobs, analyzing intermediate metrics, and proposing the next wave of experiments. Across more than 70 experiments organized into fourteen waves on a DHAV1 12-DoF quadruped in Isaac Lab, the agent progressed from early rough-terrain runs with mean reward around 7 to a best logged Wave 12 run, exp063, with velocity error 0.263 and 97\% timeout over 2000 iterations, independently reproduced five times across different GPUs. The archive also records several concrete autonomous research decisions: isolating PhysX deadlocks to terrain sets containing boxes and stair-like primitives, porting four reward terms from openly available reference implementations \cite{deeprobotics, rlsar}, correcting Isaac Sim import and bootstrapping issues, reducing environment count for diagnosis, terminating hung runs, and pivoting effort away from HIM after repeated terrain=0.0 outcomes. Relative to the AutoResearch paradigm \cite{autoresearch}, this case study operates in a more failure-prone robotics RL setting with multi-GPU experiment management and simulator-specific engineering constraints. The contribution is empirical and documentary: it shows that an agent can materially execute the iterative RL research loop in this domain with limited human intervention, while also making clear where human direction still shaped the agenda.

</details>


### [27] [Robotic Dexterous Manipulation via Anisotropic Friction Modulation using Passive Rollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27452)
*Ethan Fisk,Taeyoon Lee,Shenli Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 设计带有可选择性制动或旋转的被动滚轮的机器人指尖，通过调制接触摩擦和约束方向来实现灵巧操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指尖摩擦控制对于灵巧操作至关重要，但在机器人手中难以实现。传统机器人夹持器在摩擦调制方面存在局限，需要更适应性强、鲁棒性强的解决方案。

Method: 设计配备被动滚轮的机器人指尖，滚轮可选择性地制动或旋转以调制接触摩擦和约束方向。未制动时滚轮允许接触点沿滚动方向无约束滑动；制动时则像传统指尖一样抵抗运动。滚轮安装在旋转机构上，可重新定向约束框架以适应不同操作任务。开发了基于约束的指尖模型，并将其集成到平行夹持器中。

Result: 实验表明，该设计能够实现传统机器人夹持器难以完成的多种灵巧动作，包括抓握内的滑动和旋转、对不确定接触的鲁棒适应、多物体或多部件操作，以及需要指尖间不对称摩擦的交互。被动滚轮指尖展示了广泛的适用性。

Conclusion: 被动滚轮指尖作为一种低复杂度、机械效率高的摩擦调制方法，能够实现更适应性强、鲁棒性强的机器人操作，推动了更灵活机器人操作的发展。

Abstract: Controlling friction at the fingertip is fundamental to dexterous manipulation, yet remains difficult to realize in robotic hands. We present the design and analysis of a robotic fingertip equipped with passive rollers that can be selectively braked or pivoted to modulate contact friction and constraint directions. When unbraked, the rollers permit unconstrained sliding of the contact point along the rolling direction; when braked, they resist motion like a conventional fingertip. The rollers are mounted on a pivoting mechanism, allowing reorientation of the constraint frame to accommodate different manipulation tasks. We develop a constraint-based model of the fingertip integrated into a parallel-jaw gripper and analyze its ability to support diverse manipulation strategies. Experiments show that the proposed design enables a wide range of dexterous actions that are conventionally challenging for robotic grippers, including sliding and pivoting within the grasp, robust adaptation to uncertain contacts, multi-object or multi-part manipulation, and interactions requiring asymmetric friction across fingers. These results demonstrate the versatility of passive roller fingertips as a low-complexity, mechanically efficient approach to friction modulation, advancing the development of more adaptable and robust robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [28] [Copilot-Assisted Second-Thought Framework for Brain-to-Robot Hand Motion Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27492)
*Yizhe Li,Shixiao Wang,Jian K. Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CNN-注意力混合模型解码EEG手部运动学，扩展到EEG-EMG多模态解码，并引入副驾驶框架提升轨迹保真度，用于机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于CNN或RNN的运动学预测方法在处理长序列EEG数据时存在局限，而Transformer模型虽能建模长序列但计算成本高。需要开发更高效的模型来解码EEG手部运动学，并探索多模态融合提升性能。

Method: 1. 提出CNN-注意力混合模型解码EEG手部运动学；2. 扩展到EEG-EMG多模态解码；3. 引入副驾驶框架，使用运动状态感知的评判器在有限状态机中过滤低置信度解码点；4. 在MuJoCo仿真中使用解码轨迹控制Franka Panda机械臂。

Result: 1. 被试内实验：X、Y、Z轴PCC值分别为0.9854、0.9946、0.9065（拇指和食指中点轨迹）；2. 跨被试实验：PCC值分别为0.9643、0.9795、0.5852；3. 副驾驶框架将纯EEG解码的被试内PCC提升至0.93，同时排除少于20%的数据点。

Conclusion: CNN-注意力混合模型能有效解码EEG手部运动学，EEG-EMG多模态融合显著提升性能。副驾驶框架通过后处理过滤低置信度点，在保持数据完整性的同时提升轨迹保真度，为运动相关脑机接口的实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Motor kinematics prediction (MKP) from electroencephalography (EEG) is an important research area for developing movement-related brain-computer interfaces (BCIs). While traditional methods often rely on convolutional neural networks (CNNs) or recurrent neural networks (RNNs), Transformer-based models have shown strong ability in modeling long sequential EEG data. In this study, we propose a CNN-attention hybrid model for decoding hand kinematics from EEG during grasp-and-lift tasks, achieving strong performance in within-subject experiments. We further extend this approach to EEG-EMG multimodal decoding, which yields substantially improved results. Within-subject tests achieve PCC values of 0.9854, 0.9946, and 0.9065 for the X, Y, and Z axes, respectively, computed on the midpoint trajectory between the thumb and index finger, while cross-subject tests result in 0.9643, 0.9795, and 0.5852. The decoded trajectories from both modalities are then used to control a Franka Panda robotic arm in a MuJoCo simulation. To enhance trajectory fidelity, we introduce a copilot framework that filters low-confidence decoded points using a motion-state-aware critic within a finite-state machine. This post-processing step improves the overall within-subject PCC of EEG-only decoding to 0.93 while excluding fewer than 20% of the data points.

</details>


### [29] [S3KF: Spherical State-Space Kalman Filtering for Panoramic 3D Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27534)
*Zhongyuan Liu,Shaonan Yu,Jianping Li,Pengfei Wan,Xinhang Xu,Pengfei Wang,Maggie Y. Gao,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出S³KF框架，利用旋转LiDAR和四鱼眼相机实现全景3D多目标跟踪，采用球面几何一致的状态表示和扩展球面卡尔曼滤波


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像平面跟踪器在全景图像中不可靠，传统欧几里得3D表示引入冗余方向参数且无法自然统一角度、尺度和深度估计，需要适用于工业安全监控、广域机器人感知和大工作空间的全景多目标跟踪解决方案

Method: 基于旋转LiDAR和四鱼眼相机平台，采用单位球面S²上的几何一致状态表示，使用两自由度切平面参数化建模目标方位，联合估计框尺度与深度动态，推导扩展球面卡尔曼滤波管道融合全景相机检测与LiDAR深度观测，建立基于地图的地面真值生成管道

Result: 在自采集真实世界序列上实现分米级平面跟踪精度，在动态场景中相比2D全景基线改进身份连续性，在Jetson AGX Orin平台上实现实时板载操作

Conclusion: S³KF框架是全景感知和工业规模多目标跟踪的实用解决方案，通过球面几何表示和传感器融合实现了可靠的全景3D跟踪

Abstract: Panoramic multi-object tracking is important for industrial safety monitoring, wide-area robotic perception, and infrastructure-light deployment in large workspaces. In these settings, the sensing system must provide full-surround coverage, metric geometric cues, and stable target association under wide field-of-view distortion and occlusion. Existing image-plane trackers are tightly coupled to the camera projection and become unreliable in panoramic imagery, while conventional Euclidean 3D formulations introduce redundant directional parameters and do not naturally unify angular, scale, and depth estimation. In this paper, we present $\mathbf{S^3KF}$, a panoramic 3D multi-object tracking framework built on a motorized rotating LiDAR and a quad-fisheye camera rig. The key idea is a geometry-consistent state representation on the unit sphere $\mathbb{S}^2$, where object bearing is modeled by a two-degree-of-freedom tangent-plane parameterization and jointly estimated with box scale and depth dynamics. Based on this state, we derive an extended spherical Kalman filtering pipeline that fuses panoramic camera detections with LiDAR depth observations for multimodal tracking. We further establish a map-based ground-truth generation pipeline using wearable localization devices registered to a shared global LiDAR map, enabling quantitative evaluation without motion-capture infrastructure. Experiments on self-collected real-world sequences show decimeter-level planar tracking accuracy, improved identity continuity over a 2D panoramic baseline in dynamic scenes, and real-time onboard operation on a Jetson AGX Orin platform. These results indicate that the proposed framework is a practical solution for panoramic perception and industrial-scale multi-object tracking.The project page can be found at https://kafeiyin00.github.io/S3KF/.

</details>


### [30] [Learning Smooth and Robust Space Robotic Manipulation of Dynamic Target via Inter-frame Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27537)
*Siyi Lang,Hongyi Gao,Yingxin Zhang,Zihao Liu,Hanlin Dong,Zhaoke Ning,Zhiqiang Ma,Panfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种数据驱动的空间机器人操控方法，通过整合历史时间信息和帧间相关性机制，实现对动态非合作目标的精确操控


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轨服务是未来航空航天工程的关键前沿，动态非合作目标的操控是关键技术。微重力环境下物体自由漂浮，缺乏地面支撑和摩擦约束，显著增加了空间机器人操控的复杂性。传统规划控制方法主要限于已知静态场景，缺乏实时响应能力。

Method: 提出数据驱动的空间机器人操控方法，整合历史时间信息和帧间相关性机制。通过利用历史帧与当前帧之间的时间相关性，系统能有效捕捉场景中的运动特征，从而为动态目标生成稳定平滑的操控轨迹。

Result: 开发了基于地面的实验平台，包括PIPER X机械臂和双轴线性平台，精确模拟2D平面中的微重力自由漂浮运动，用于验证所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够实现未知非结构化空间环境中动态目标的精确机器人操控，解决了传统方法在实时响应和动态场景适应方面的局限性。

Abstract: On-orbit servicing represents a critical frontier in future aerospace engineering, with the manipulation of dynamic non-cooperative targets serving as a key technology. In microgravity environments, objects are typically free-floating, lacking the support and frictional constraints found on Earth, which significantly escalates the complexity of tasks involving space robotic manipulation. Conventional planning and control-based methods are primarily limited to known, static scenarios and lack real-time responsiveness. To achieve precise robotic manipulation of dynamic targets in unknown and unstructured space environments, this letter proposes a data-driven space robotic manipulation approach that integrates historical temporal information and inter-frame correlation mechanisms. By exploiting the temporal correlation between historical and current frames, the system can effectively capture motion features within the scene, thereby producing stable and smooth manipulation trajectories for dynamic targets. To validate the effectiveness of the proposed method, we developed a ground-based experimental platform consisting of a PIPER X robotic arm and a dual-axis linear stage, which accurately simulates micro-gravity free-floating motion in a 2D plane.

</details>


### [31] [LLM-Enabled Low-Altitude UAV Natural Language Navigation via Signal Temporal Logic Specification Translation and Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27583)
*Yuqi Ping,Huahao Ding,Tianhao Liang,Longyu Zhou,Guangyu Lei,Xinglin Chen,Junwei Wu,Jieyu Zhou,Tingting Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个统一框架，将自然语言指令转换为信号时序逻辑规范，并通过混合整数线性规划合成无人机轨迹，实现城市环境中安全、可解释的导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言导航为低空无人机提供了智能便捷的解决方案，但在城市环境中需要将模糊指令精确转换为满足时空约束的安全关键运动规划，这是一个挑战。

Method: 开发了推理增强的大型语言模型，利用思维链监督和组相对策略优化生成可执行的STL公式；引入规范修复机制，结合MILP诊断和LLM引导的语义推理来选择性放松约束；通过MILP合成轨迹。

Result: 广泛的仿真和真实飞行实验表明，该闭环框架显著提高了NL到STL翻译的鲁棒性，实现了复杂场景下安全、可解释且适应性强的无人机导航。

Conclusion: 提出的统一框架成功解决了城市环境中自然语言导航的挑战，通过将NL指令转换为STL规范并合成轨迹，实现了安全、可解释和适应性强的无人机导航系统。

Abstract: Natural language (NL) navigation for low-altitude unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) offers an intelligent and convenient solution for low-altitude aerial services by enabling an intuitive interface for non-expert operators. However, deploying this capability in urban environments necessitates the precise grounding of underspecified instructions into safety-critical, dynamically feasible motion plans subject to spatiotemporal constraints. To address this challenge, we propose a unified framework that translates NL instructions into Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications and subsequently synthesizes trajectories via mixed-integer linear programming (MILP). Specifically, to generate executable STL formulas from free-form NL, we develop a reasoning-enhanced large language model (LLM) leveraging chain-of-thought (CoT) supervision and group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), which ensures high syntactic validity and semantic consistency. Furthermore, to resolve infeasibilities induced by stringent logical or spatial requirements, we introduce a specification repair mechanism. This module combines MILP-based diagnosis with LLM-guided semantic reasoning to selectively relax task constraints while strictly enforcing safety guarantees. Extensive simulations and real-world flight experiments demonstrate that the proposed closed-loop framework significantly improves NL-to-STL translation robustness, enabling safe, interpretable, and adaptable UAV navigation in complex scenarios.

</details>


### [32] [ProgressVLA: Progress-Guided Diffusion Policy for Vision-Language Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27670)
*Hongyu Yan,Qiwei Li,Jiaolong Yang,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VLA模型，通过进度估计和可微分进度指导改进机器人操作任务完成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型缺乏进度感知能力，依赖手工启发式规则进行任务终止判断，这在涉及级联子目标的长时程任务中尤为严重

Method: 1) 在大规模无监督视频-文本机器人数据集上预训练进度估计器；2) 引入逆动力学世界模型，将预测的动作标记映射到未来潜在视觉状态，通过进度估计器和最大进度正则化建立可微分管道，提供进度引导的动作标记优化

Result: 进度估计器在模拟中实现低预测残差(0.07，范围[0,1])，并在未见过的真实世界样本上展示零样本泛化能力；在CALVIN和LIBERO基准测试及真实机器人部署中，相比强基线模型在成功率和泛化能力上均有显著提升

Conclusion: 提出的VLA模型通过集成进度估计和可微分进度指导，有效解决了机器人操作任务中的进度感知问题，在模拟和真实世界环境中均表现出优越性能

Abstract: Most existing vision-language-action (VLA) models for robotic manipulation lack progress awareness, typically relying on hand-crafted heuristics for task termination. This limitation is particularly severe in long-horizon tasks involving cascaded sub-goals. In this work, we investigate the estimation and integration of task progress, proposing a novel model named {\textbf \vla}. Our technical contributions are twofold: (1) \emph{robust progress estimation}: We pre-train a progress estimator on large-scale, unsupervised video-text robotic datasets. This estimator achieves a low prediction residual (0.07 on a scale of $[0, 1]$) in simulation and demonstrates zero-shot generalization to unseen real-world samples, and (2) \emph{differentiable progress guidance}: We introduce an inverse dynamics world model that maps predicted action tokens into future latent visual states. These latents are then processed by the progress estimator; by applying a maximal progress regularization, we establish a differentiable pipeline that provides progress-piloted guidance to refine action tokens. Extensive experiments on the CALVIN and LIBERO benchmarks, alongside real-world robot deployment, consistently demonstrate substantial improvements in success rates and generalization over strong baselines.

</details>


### [33] [TerraSkipper: A Centimeter-Scale Robot for Multi-Terrain Skipping and Crawling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27725)
*Shashwat Singh,Sheri Zhang,Spencer Matonis,Zeynep Temel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受弹涂鱼启发，开发了一种厘米级多地形跳跃爬行机器人，结合旋转弹簧尾实现跳跃和侧鳍实现爬行，在粘性和颗粒介质上跳跃比爬行速度更快


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹涂鱼能够在陆地、水域和粘性泥浆等多种环境中运动，其强大的尾部能够储存并快速释放能量实现跳跃。受此生物机制启发，需要开发能够在多种地形上运动的机器人，特别是在传统爬行方式失效的粘性和颗粒介质上

Method: 主要采用3D打印制造，集成板载传感、计算和电源系统。机器人配备两个带霍尔效应传感器的侧鳍用于爬行步态控制，以及一个由10mm行星齿轮电机驱动的旋转弹簧尾实现连续跳跃。对尾部进行了建模和实验表征，确定了25mm的最佳长度以最大化推进力

Result: 尾部优化后产生4N平均推进力（峰值达6N）。在均匀沙土和膨润土粉末中测试，跳跃始终比爬行产生更高的平均速度，特别是在粘性和颗粒介质上。户外测试证实，在草地、松散沙地和硬质地面上，跳跃与爬行结合扩展了机器人在真实环境中的操作范围

Conclusion: 受弹涂鱼启发的跳跃爬行机器人设计成功实现了多地形运动能力。跳跃在粘性和颗粒介质上比纯爬行更有效，两种运动模式的结合显著扩展了机器人在复杂真实环境中的操作范围

Abstract: Mudskippers are unique amphibious fish capable of locomotion in diverse environments, including terrestrial surfaces, aquatic habitats, and highly viscous substrates such as mud. This versatile locomotion is largely enabled by their powerful tail, which stores and rapidly releases energy to produce impulsive jumps. Inspired by this biological mechanism, we present the design and development of a multi-terrain centimeter-scale skipping and crawling robot. The robot is predominantly 3D printed and features onboard sensing, computation, and power. It is equipped with two side fins for crawling, each integrated with a hall effect sensor for gait control, while a rotary springtail driven by a 10mm planetary gear motor enables continuous impulsive skipping across a range of substrates to achieve multi-terrain locomotion. We modeled and experimentally characterized the tail, identifying an optimal length of 25mm that maximizes the mean propulsive force (4N, peaks up to 6N) for forward motion. In addition, we evaluated skipping on substrates where fin based crawling alone fails, and varied the moisture content of uniform sand and bentonite clay powder to compare skipping with crawling. Skipping consistently produced higher mean velocities than crawling, particularly on viscous and granular media. Finally, outdoor tests on grass, loose sand, and hard ground confirmed that combining skipping on entangling and granular terrain with crawling on firm ground extends the operational range of the robot in real-world environments.

</details>


### [34] [Heracles: Bridging Precise Tracking and Generative Synthesis for General Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27756)
*Zelin Tao,Zeran Su,Peiran Liu,Jingkai Sun,Wenqiang Que,Jiahao Ma,Jialin Yu,Jiahang Cao,Pihai Sun,Hao Liang,Gang Han,Wen Zhao,Zhiyuan Xu,Yijie Guo,Jian Tang,Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Heracles框架，一种基于状态条件扩散模型的中间件，用于桥接精确运动跟踪与生成式合成，提升人形机器人控制的鲁棒性和适应性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前通用控制器将运动控制视为刚性参考跟踪问题，在名义条件下有效但在严重扰动下表现出脆性、非拟人化的失效模式，缺乏人类运动控制固有的生成适应性

Method: 提出Heracles状态条件扩散中间件，作为高层参考运动与低层物理跟踪器之间的中介层。通过条件化机器人实时状态，扩散模型隐式调整行为：状态与参考对齐时近似恒等映射保持零样本跟踪保真度；遇到显著状态偏差时无缝转换为生成合成器产生自然拟人恢复轨迹

Result: 框架证明将生成先验整合到控制回路中不仅能显著增强对极端扰动的鲁棒性，还能将人形控制从刚性跟踪范式提升为开放式的生成通用架构

Conclusion: Heracles通过生成式中间件成功桥接了精确跟踪与适应性恢复，为人形机器人控制提供了兼具高保真跟踪和自然恢复能力的通用解决方案

Abstract: Achieving general-purpose humanoid control requires a delicate balance between the precise execution of commanded motions and the flexible, anthropomorphic adaptability needed to recover from unpredictable environmental perturbations. Current general controllers predominantly formulate motion control as a rigid reference-tracking problem. While effective in nominal conditions, these trackers often exhibit brittle, non-anthropomorphic failure modes under severe disturbances, lacking the generative adaptability inherent to human motor control. To overcome this limitation, we propose Heracles, a novel state-conditioned diffusion middleware that bridges precise motion tracking and generative synthesis. Rather than relying on rigid tracking paradigms or complex explicit mode-switching, Heracles operates as an intermediary layer between high-level reference motions and low-level physics trackers. By conditioning on the robot's real-time state, the diffusion model implicitly adapts its behavior: it approximates an identity map when the state closely aligns with the reference, preserving zero-shot tracking fidelity. Conversely, when encountering significant state deviations, it seamlessly transitions into a generative synthesizer to produce natural, anthropomorphic recovery trajectories. Our framework demonstrates that integrating generative priors into the control loop not only significantly enhances robustness against extreme perturbations but also elevates humanoid control from a rigid tracking paradigm to an open-ended, generative general-purpose architecture.

</details>


### [35] [Transferability Through Cooperative Competitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27770)
*Rodrigo Serra,Carlos Azevedo,André Silva,Kevin Alcedo,Quentin Rouxel,Peter So,Alejandro Suarez,Alin Albu-Schäeffer,Pedro U. Lima*

Main category: cs.RO

Abstract: This paper presents a novel framework for cooperative robotics competitions (coopetitions) that promote the transferability and composability of robotics modules, including software, hardware, and data, across heterogeneous robotic systems. The framework is designed to incentivize collaboration between teams through structured task design, shared infrastructure, and a royalty-based scoring system. As a case study, the paper details the implementation and outcomes of the first euROBIN Coopetition, held under the European Robotics and AI Network (euROBIN), which featured fifteen robotic platforms competing across Industrial, Service, and Outdoor domains. The study highlights the practical challenges of achieving module reuse in real-world scenarios, particularly in terms of integration complexity and system compatibility. It also examines participant performance, integration behavior, and team feedback to assess the effectiveness of the framework. The paper concludes with lessons learned and recommendations for future coopetitions, including improveme

### [36] [Safety Guardrails in the Sky: Realizing Control Barrier Functions on the VISTA F-16 Jet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27912)
*Andrew W. Singletary,Max H. Cohen,Tamas G. Molnar,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Guardrails是一种基于控制屏障函数的运行时安全保障机制，用于确保自主系统在操作域边缘执行高性能动态运动时的安全性，已在F-16战斗机上成功测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主系统（从腿式机器人到自动驾驶车辆和飞机）的发展，需要在执行高性能动态运动时确保系统及其环境的安全，避免造成伤害。

Method: 基于控制屏障函数理论，Guardrails提出了一种控制策略，将人类或AI操作员的命令与安全控制动作谨慎混合，以保证安全行为。

Result: 在F-16战斗机上进行飞行测试，Guardrails成功监督人类飞行员执行g限值、高度边界、地理围栏约束及其组合，确保安全的同时在安全时保持飞行员控制，仅在必要时最小化修改不安全输入。

Conclusion: Guardrails作为一种运行时安全保障机制，能够有效确保自主系统的动态安全，使其在操作域边缘安全运行，已在真实飞行测试中得到验证。

Abstract: The advancement of autonomous systems -- from legged robots to self-driving vehicles and aircraft -- necessitates executing increasingly high-performance and dynamic motions without ever putting the system or its environment in harm's way. In this paper, we introduce Guardrails -- a novel runtime assurance mechanism that guarantees dynamic safety for autonomous systems, allowing them to safely evolve on the edge of their operational domains. Rooted in the theory of control barrier functions, Guardrails offers a control strategy that carefully blends commands from a human or AI operator with safe control actions to guarantee safe behavior. To demonstrate its capabilities, we implemented Guardrails on an F-16 fighter jet and conducted flight tests where Guardrails supervised a human pilot to enforce g-limits, altitude bounds, geofence constraints, and combinations thereof. Throughout extensive flight testing, Guardrails successfully ensured safety, keeping the pilot in control when safe to do so and minimally modifying unsafe pilot inputs otherwise.

</details>

*Jeonghwan Kim,Shamel Fahmi,Seungeun Rho,Sehoon Ha,Gabriel Nelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过强化学习实现自行车机器人的前空翻，提出迭代运动模仿方法，从不可行的参考运动开始，逐步训练出可行的敏捷行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
