Abstract: We classify all irreducible highest-weight unitary modules over the non-compact real form $\mathfrak{u}(p,q|n)$ of the general linear Lie superalgebra $\mathfrak{gl}_{p+q|n}$. The classification is given by explicit necessary and sufficient conditions on the highest weights. Our approach combines the Howe duality for $\mathfrak{gl}_{p+q|n}$ with a quadratic invariant of the maximal compact subalgebra. As consequences, we classify all irreducible lowest-weight unitary modules over $\mathfrak{u}(p,q|n)$ via duality, and all irreducible unitary modules over $\mathfrak{u}(n|q,p)$ via an isomorphism of Lie superalgebras.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce a one-skeleton path model for Mirkovic-Vilonen polytopes in type A_n. We show that MV polytopes induced by fundamental one-skeleton paths are Harder-Narasimhan polytopes. The paths given by an orientation of the fundamental alcove parameterize precisely the cluster variables in the initial seed of the coordinate ring C[N].

Result: 建立了多辛PDE的全面分类：结构不一致、无条件不稳定、条件稳定。特别指出KdV方程与菱形更新本质不兼容，而非线性Dirac方程和"好"Boussinesq方程在温和时间步长缩放下具有稳定性区域。大量数值实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 该分析框架阐明了菱形格式的基本限制，为将其可靠应用于新PDE模型提供了实用指南。结果表明某些基准方程与菱形格式本质不兼容，而其他系统在适当条件下可稳定求解。

Abstract: Multi-symplectic diamond schemes proposed by McLachlan and Wilkins (2015) provide a framework for the numerical integration of Hamiltonian partial differential equations, combining local implicitness with high-order accuracy and discrete multi-symplectic conservation laws. Despite these advantages, their behavior beyond a limited class of model equations remains poorly understood, and numerical difficulties may arise depending on the underlying multi-symplectic formulation. In this paper, we present a systematic stability analysis framework for diamond schemes applied to general multi-symplectic PDEs. The approach consists of three stages. First, we identify structural inconsistency of the local diamond update using Dulmage--Mendelsohn decomposition, revealing cases in which the scheme is intrinsically unsolvable. Second, we introduce a graph-based error-propagation analysis that yields a necessary stability condition by detecting negative cycles in a weighted directed graph. Third, for equations that pass the preliminary tests, we derive eigenvalue-based timestep restrictions providing sufficient conditions for stability. The analysis leads to a comprehensive classification of multi-symplectic PDEs according to whether diamond schemes are structurally inconsistent, unconditionally unstable, or conditionally stable. In particular, we show that benchmark equations such as the Korteweg--de Vries equation are intrinsically incompatible with the diamond update, while systems including the nonlinear Dirac and ``good'' Boussinesq equations admit stability regimes under mild timestep scaling. Extensive numerical experiments confirm the theoretical predictions and demonstrate the practical implications of the proposed framework. Our results clarify fundamental limitations of diamond schemes and provide practical guidelines for their reliable application to new PDE models.

Result: 在全局Lipschitz和线性增长条件下，随机theta方法在时间变换Lévy噪声驱动的SDEs中具有弱收敛阶为1。该结果将目前可用的弱收敛分析从Euler-Maruyama方法推广到更一般的随机theta方法类，并建立了从基础非时间变换Lévy方程的弱分析到相应时间变换问题的可行路径。

Conclusion: 该论文成功建立了随机theta方法在时间变换Lévy噪声驱动的SDEs中的弱收敛理论，数值实验进一步支持了理论发现。这项工作为处理具有随机时间变化的更复杂随机系统的数值分析提供了理论框架和方法。

Abstract: This paper studies the weak convergence order of the stochastic theta method for stochastic differential equations (SDEs) driven by time-changed Lévy noise under global Lipschitz and linear growth conditions. In contrast to classical Lévy-driven SDEs, the presence of a random time change makes the weak error analysis involve both the discretization error of the underlying equation and the approximation error of the random clock. Moreover, compared with explicit Euler--Maruyama method, the implicit drift correction in the stochastic theta method makes the associated weak error analysis substantially more delicate. To address these difficulties, we first establish a global weak convergence estimate of order one for the stochastic theta method applied to the corresponding non-time-changed Lévy SDEs on the infinite time interval by means of the Kolmogorov backward partial integro differential equations. Incorporating the approximation of the inverse subordinator together with the duality principle, we derive the weak convergence order of the stochastic theta method with $θ\in [0,1]$ in the time-changed Lévy setting. The result advances the currently available weak convergence analysis beyond the Euler--Maruyama method to the more general class of stochastic theta method, and establishes a workable route from the weak analysis of the underlying non-time-changed Lévy equation to the corresponding time-changed problem. Finally, numerical experiments are presented to further support the theoretical findings.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越静态或启发式方法，为进化聚类提供理论基础，解决传统期望最大化算法在非参数聚类应用中的局限性。

Result: 在时间依赖高斯混合模型设置中，平均场博弈动态恢复了经典期望最大化算法的轨迹同时确保质量守恒，数值实验证明了方法的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架为更一般的非参数聚类应用提供了路径，其中传统期望最大化方法可能面临限制，展示了控制理论方法在进化聚类中的潜力。

Abstract: We propose a control-theoretic framework for evolutionary clustering based on Mean Field Games (MFG). Moving beyond static or heuristic approaches, we formulate the problem as a population dynamics game governed by a coupled Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman and Fokker-Planck system. Driven by a variational cost functional rather than predefined statistical shapes, this continuous-time formulation provides a flexible basis for non-parametric cluster evolution. To validate the framework, we analyze the setting of time-dependent Gaussian mixtures, showing that the MFG dynamics recover the trajectories of the classical Expectation-Maximization (EM) algorithm while ensuring mass conservation. Furthermore, we introduce time-averaged log-likelihood functionals to regularize temporal fluctuations. Numerical experiments illustrate the stability of our approach and suggest a path toward more general non-parametric clustering applications where traditional EM methods may face limitations.

Method: 采用分步求解策略：首先通过时间半离散近似求解简化问题得到速度场，然后利用inf-sup性质恢复压力场。同时引入多个半变分不等式来描述数学模型，并探讨它们的等价性。

Result: 成功放宽了[19]中对源项和初始速度的假设条件，去除了数据初始值的相容性条件，在更自然的假设下证明了非定常Stokes半变分不等式的适定性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了非定常Stokes半变分不等式更精细的适定性分析，通过改进的求解策略在更弱的假设条件下证明了问题的适定性，为处理具有非光滑摩擦型边界条件的不可压缩流体流动问题提供了更实用的理论框架。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to the well-posedness analysis of a nonstationary Stokes hemivariational inequality for an incompressible fluid flow described by the Stokes equations subject to a nonsmooth boundary condition of friction type described by the Clarke subdifferential. In a recent paper [19], well-posedness of the nonstationary Stokes hemivariational inequality is studied for both the velocity and pressure fields. The solution existence is shown through a limiting procedure based on temporally semi-discrete approximations for both the velocity and pressure fields. In this paper, a refined well-posedness analysis is provided on the nonstationary Stokes hemivariational inequality under more natural assumptions on the problem data. The solution existence is first shown for the velocity field through a limiting procedure based on temporally semi-discrete approximations of a reduced problem and then the pressure field is recovered with the help of an inf-sup property. In this way, assumptions on the source term and the initial velocity needed in [19] are weakened, and a compatibility condition on initial values of the data is dropped. Moreover, several hemivariational inequalities are introduced for the mathematical model and their equivalence is explored.

</details>

Abstract: We design an energy-stable and asymptotic-preserving finite volume scheme for the compressible Euler system. Using the relative energy framework, we establish rigorous error estimates that yield convergence of the numerical solutions in two distinct regimes. For a fixed Mach number $\varepsilon>0$, we derive error estimates between the numerical solutions and a strong solution of the compressible Euler system that are uniform with respect to the discretisation parameters, ensuring convergence as the underlying mesh is refined. In the low Mach number regime, we analyse the error between the numerical solutions and a strong solution of the incompressible Euler system and obtain asymptotic error estimates that are uniform in $\varepsilon$ and the discretisation parameters. These results imply convergence of the numerical solutions toward a strong solution of the incompressible Euler system as $\varepsilon$, and the discretisation parameters simultaneously tend to zero. Numerical experiments are presented to validate the theoretical analysis.

Abstract: This paper presents a class of novel high-order fully-discrete entropy stable (ES) discontinuous Galerkin (DG) schemes with explicit time discretization. The proposed methodology exploits a critical observation from [4] that the cell averages of classical DG solutions with forward Euler time stepping satisfy an ``entropy-stable-like'' property. Building on this result, fully-discrete entropy stability is rigorously enforced through a simple Zhang--Shu-type scaling limiter [45] applied as a post-processing step, without modifying the underlying spatial discretization. Furthermore, the proposed methodology can simultaneously enforce multiple cell entropy inequalities, a capability unavailable in existing ES DG schemes. High-order accuracy in time is achieved by using strong-stability-preserving (SSP) multistep methods. Theoretically, we prove that the proposed scheme indeed maintains high-order accuracy and establish a Lax--Wendroff-type theorem guaranteeing that the limit of the numerical solutions, if it exists, satisfies the desired entropy inequality. Extensive numerical tests for scalar equations and systems, including the nonconvex Buckley--Leverett problem and extreme examples of Euler equations, demonstrate optimal accuracy, enforcement of multiple entropy conditions, and strong robustness.

Abstract: Quasiperiodic elliptic operators (QEOs) serve as fundamental models in both mathematics and physics, as exemplified by their role in the numerical modeling of one-dimensional photonic quasicrystals. However, distinct from periodic elliptic operators, approximating eigenpairs for QEOs poses significant challenges, particularly in capturing the full spectral structure (notably the continuous spectrum) and deriving convergence guarantees in the absence of compactness. In this paper, we develop a high-accuracy numerical method to compute eigenpairs of QEOs based on the projection method, which embeds quasiperiodic operators into a higher-dimensional periodic torus. To address the non-compactness issue, we construct a directional-derivative Hilbert space along irrational manifolds of a high-dimensional torus and characterize operators equivalent to QEOs within this space. By integrating spectral theory for non-compact operators into the Babuška-Osborn eigenproblem framework, we establish rigorous convergence analysis and prove that our method achieves spectral accuracy. Numerical experiments validate the accuracy and efficiency of the proposed method, including a one-dimensional photonic quasicrystal and two- and three-dimensional QEOs.

Abstract: In this paper, we propose two monolithic fully discrete finite element methods for fluid-structure interaction (FSI) based on a novel Piola-type Arbitrary Lagrangian-Eulerian (ALE) mapping. For the spatial discretization, we adopt arbitrary order hybridizable discontinuous Galerkin (HDG) methods for the incompressible Navier-Stokes and linear elasticity equations, and a continuous Galerkin (CG) method for the fluid mesh movement.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对强磁场下的Vlasov-Poisson方程，需要开发能够保持能量守恒的数值方法。传统方法在强磁场条件下可能无法保持能量守恒特性，因此需要设计新的算法框架来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出显式松弛粒子网格方法家族，结合分裂框架和动态松弛参数更新。使用平均化技术建立误差界：Strang型ER-PIC方法具有二阶误差界，Lie-Trotter ER-PIC方案在位置上具有一致一阶精度。

Result: 数值实验在流体、有限拉莫尔半径和扩散三种物理区域验证了方法的准确性和能量守恒特性。理论分析建立了严格的误差界。

Conclusion: ER-PIC方法家族能够有效处理强磁场下的Vlasov-Poisson方程，保持精确能量守恒，并在多种物理区域中表现出良好的数值性能。

Abstract: In this work, we present a novel family of explicit relaxation Particle-in-Cell (ER-PIC) methods for the Vlasov-Poisson equation with a strong magnetic field. These schemes achieve exact energy conservation by combining a splitting framework with the dynamic updating of a relaxation parameter at each time step. Using an averaging technique, we rigorously establish second-order error bounds for the Strang-type ER-PIC method and uniform first order accuracy in position for the Lie-Trotter ER-PIC scheme. Numerical experiments across the fluid, finite Larmor radius, and diffusion regimes confirm the accuracy and energy conservation of our methods.

</details>


### [88] [Quasi-random splitting method for accurate and efficient multiphysics simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27654)
*Lei Li,Yunxiao Liu,Chenchen Wan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种基于低差异序列的拟随机算子分裂方法，用于多机制驱动的演化方程，相比经典Strang分裂显著减少计算量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多算子Strang分裂需要2p-2次子流评估，计算成本高；随机分裂方法需要多次运行取平均。需要一种既能减少计算量又能在单次运行中获得高精度的方法

Method: 使用低差异序列生成子流的排序顺序，每个时间步仅需应用每个子流一次。对于p个算子的分解，只需p次子流评估，而经典方法需要2p-2次

Result: 对于两个算子的有界线性问题，获得了O(τ²|log τ|)的全局误差界。数值实验验证了预测的收敛行为，表明该方法以显著降低的计算成本实现了接近Strang分裂的精度

Conclusion: 拟随机算子分裂方法通过低差异序列的确定性排序，在单次运行中实现了高精度，同时大幅减少了计算成本，特别适用于多算子演化方程

Abstract: We propose a quasi-random operator splitting method for evolution equations driven by multiple mechanisms. The method uses a low-discrepancy sequence to generate the ordering of the subflows, while requiring only one application of each subflow per time step. In particular, for a decomposition into \(p\) operators, the classical multi-operator Strang splitting requires essentially \(2p-2\) subflow evaluations per step, whereas the present method uses only \(p\). In contrast to randomized splitting, the quasi-random scheme is deterministic once the underlying sequence is fixed, so its improved accuracy is achieved in a single run rather than through averaging over many independent realizations. To analyze this method, we develop a convergence framework that exploits the discrepancy structure of the induced ordering sequence and translates it into cancellation in the accumulated local errors. For two operators, this yields an essentially second-order global error bound of order \(O(τ^{2}|\log τ|)\) for bounded linear problems. We further extend the analysis to the Allen--Cahn equation and present numerical experiments, including bounded linear systems and the Allen--Cahn equation, which confirm the predicted convergence behavior and demonstrate that the proposed method achieves near-Strang accuracy at a substantially lower computational cost.

</details>


### [89] [Auto-Stabilized Weak Galerkin Finite Element Methods for Biot's consolidation model on Non-Convex Polytopal Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27704)
*Chunmei Wang,Shangyou Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种用于Biot固结模型的自稳定弱伽辽金有限元方法，无需传统稳定器即可实现数值稳定性，适用于一般形状规则多面体网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统弱伽辽金方法需要稳定器来确保数值稳定性，本文旨在开发一种无需稳定器的自稳定方法，提高计算效率并增强对复杂几何网格的适应性。

Method: 采用位移-压力双场公式，空间离散使用弱伽辽金有限元，时间离散采用后向欧拉格式。通过气泡函数作为主要分析工具，实现无需专门处理的自稳定压力近似。

Result: 证明了每个时间步线性系统的适定性，进行了严格的误差分析并获得了最优阶收敛率。数值实验验证了理论收敛速率，展示了计算效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的自稳定弱伽辽金方法成功解决了Biot固结模型，无需传统稳定器即可实现数值稳定性，在一般形状规则多面体网格上表现出良好性能，为复杂几何问题提供了灵活高效的数值解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents an auto-stabilized weak Galerkin (WG) finite element method for the Biot's consolidation model within the classical displacement-pressure two-field formulation. Unlike traditional WG approaches, the proposed scheme achieves numerical stability without the requirement of traditional stabilizers. Spatial discretization is performed using weak Galerkin finite elements for both displacement and pressure approximations, while a backward Euler scheme is employed for temporal discretization to ensure a fully implicit and stable formulation. We establish the well-posedness of the resulting linear system at each time step and provide a rigorous error analysis, deriving optimal-order convergence. A significant merit of this WG scheme is its flexibility on general shape-regular polytopal meshes, including those with non-convex geometries. By utilizing bubble functions as a primary analytical tool, the method produces stable, oscillation-free pressure approximations without specialized treatment. Numerical experiments are presented to validate the theoretical convergence rates and demonstrate the computational efficiency and robustness of the auto-stabilized formulation.

</details>


### [90] [Releasing the pressure: High-order surface flow discretizations via discrete Helmholtz-Hodge decompositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27714)
*Tim Brüers,Christoph Lehrenfeld,Tim van Beeck,Max Wardetzky*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于三角剖分曲面上的H(div)-相容BDM有限元的离散Helmholtz-Hodge分解方法，将散度自由子空间正交分解为连续流函数空间的旋转梯度与离散调和场空间，简化了不可压缩流动的数值模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在任意拓扑的曲面上进行不可压缩流动模拟时，需要处理压力未知量、鞍点结构以及确保精确的切向性、点态散度自由性和压力鲁棒性。传统方法存在计算复杂度和数值稳定性问题。

Method: 基于H(div)-相容的Brezzi-Douglas-Marini有限元，提出离散Helmholtz-Hodge分解：将散度自由BDM子空间L2正交分解为连续流函数空间的旋转梯度与离散调和场空间（维度等于曲面第一Betti数）。采用随机化算法构造调和基，实现杂交化、高效处理调和未知量和压力重构。

Result: 该方法消除了压力未知量和鞍点结构，同时保证了精确的切向性、点态散度自由性和压力鲁棒性。数值实验在三叶结和多重连通雕塑曲面上验证了非定常表面Navier-Stokes方程的有效性，并阐明了调和速度分量的物理作用。

Conclusion: 提出的离散Helmholtz-Hodge分解为曲面上的不可压缩流动模拟提供了一种高效、鲁棒的数值框架，通过流函数和有限个调和系数简化了问题表述，在复杂拓扑曲面上具有良好应用前景。

Abstract: We present a discrete Helmholtz--Hodge decomposition for H(div)-conforming Brezzi--Douglas--Marini (BDM) finite elements on triangulated surfaces of arbitrary topology. The divergence-free BDM subspace is split L2-orthogonally into rotated gradients of a continuous streamfunction space and a finite-dimensional space of discrete harmonic fields whose dimension equals the first Betti number of the surface. Consequently, any incompressible flow discretized on this subspace can be reformulated with a scalar streamfunction and finitely many harmonic coefficients as the only unknowns. This eliminates the pressure and the saddle-point structure while ensuring exact tangentiality, pointwise divergence-freeness, and pressure-robustness. We present a randomized algorithm for constructing the harmonic basis and discuss implementation aspects including hybridization, efficient treatment of the harmonic unknowns, and pressure reconstruction. Numerical experiments for unsteady surface Navier--Stokes equations on a trefoil knot and a multiply-connected sculpture surface demonstrate the method and illustrate the physical role of the harmonic velocity component.

</details>


### [91] [Global Convergence and Uniqueness for an Inverse Problem Posed by Gelfand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27729)
*Michael V. Klibanov,Jingzhi Li,Tian Niu,Vladimir G. Romanov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 首次提出针对n维(n≥2)波动方程系数反问题的全局收敛数值方法，在初始条件包含δ函数且单点源的最具挑战性情况下，建立了近似数学模型并开发了凸化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Gelfand于1954年提出的n维波动方程系数反问题唯一性问题，这是Marchenko一维定理的n维推广。该问题在初始条件包含δ函数且单点源时最具挑战性，需要开发不依赖初始猜测的全局收敛数值方法。

Method: 1) 为最具挑战性的情况建立近似数学模型；2) 开发凸化数值方法的新版本；3) 基于Carleman估计进行收敛性分析；4) 在近似模型框架内证明唯一性定理；5) 进行包含噪声数据的计算实验验证。

Result: 1) 证明了近似数学模型框架内的唯一性定理；2) 收敛性分析确保了方法的全局收敛性，无需良好初始猜测；3) 噪声数据下的计算实验展示了复杂结构的高精度重建；4) 重建精度验证了近似数学模型的高度适定性。

Conclusion: 首次成功解决了n维波动方程在δ函数初始条件和单点源情况下的系数反问题，建立了全局收敛的凸化数值方法，验证了近似数学模型的有效性，为这一长期存在的数学问题提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: The first globally convergent numerical method is developed for a coefficient inverse problem (CIP) for the $n-$d, $n\geq 2$ wave equation with the unknown potential in the most challenging case when the $δ-$ function is present in the initial condition with a single location of the point source. In fact, an approximate mathematical model for that CIP is derived. That globally convergent numerical method is developed for this model. This is a new version of the so-called convexification numerical method. Uniqueness theorem is proven as well within the framework of that approximate mathematical model. The question about uniqueness of this CIP was first posed by a famous mathematician I. M. Gelfand in 1954 as an $n-$d ($n=2,3$) extension of the fundamental theorem of V.A. Marchenko in the 1-d case (1950). Based on a Carleman estimate, convergence analysis is carried out. This analysis ensures the global convergence of the proposed numerical method, i.e. it is not necessary to have a good first guess for the solution. Exhaustive computational experiments with noisy data demonstrate a high reconstruction accuracy of complicated structures. In particular, this accuracy points towards a high adequacy of that approximate mathematical model.

</details>


### [92] [Rigorous Eigenvalue Bounds for Schrödinger Operators with Confining Potentials on $\mathbb{R}^2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27823)
*Xuefeng Liu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种计算二维薛定谔算子特征值双边界的严格方法，首次为无界域上的薛定谔算子提供了严格特征值边界


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要为无界域上的薛定谔算子提供严格的特征值边界计算方法，填补该领域的研究空白

Method: 结合域截断到有限圆盘D(R)，在受限特征值问题上应用Liu特征值边界和复合增强Crouzeix-Raviart有限元方法

Result: 成功计算了径向对称环势V₁(x)=(|x|²-1)²和笛卡尔双阱势V₂(x)=(x₁²-1)²+x₂²的特征值严格边界

Conclusion: 该方法首次实现了无界域上薛定谔算子特征值的严格边界计算，为相关量子系统分析提供了可靠工具

Abstract: We propose a rigorous method for computing two-sided eigenvalue bounds of the Schrödinger operator $H=-Δ+V$ with a confining potential on $\mathbb{R}^2$. The method combines domain truncation to a finite disk $D(R)$ on which the restricted eigenvalue problem is solved with a rigrous eigenvalue bound, where Liu's eigenvalue bound along with the Composite Enriched Crouzeix--Raviart (CECR) finite element method proposed plays a central role. Two concrete potentials are studied: the radially symmetric ring potential $V_1(x)=(|x|^2-1)^2$ and the Cartesian double-well $V_2(x)=(x_1^2-1)^2+x_2^2$. To author's knowledge, this paper reports the first rigorous eigenvalue bounds for Schrödinger operators on an unbounded domain.

</details>


### [93] [Long-Time H1-Stability of the Cauchy One-Leg Theta-Method for the Navier-Stokes Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27861)
*Isabel Barrio Sanchez,Catalin Trenchea,Wenlong Pei*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 研究二维Navier-Stokes方程Cauchy单腿theta方法的长时稳定性，证明在H^1空间中具有一致耗散性，即半离散时间近似解在足够小时间步长下存在全局吸引子


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究数值方法（特别是Cauchy单腿theta方法）在求解二维Navier-Stokes方程时的长时稳定性，确保数值解在长时间模拟中保持物理上合理的耗散特性

Method: 使用离散Gronwall引理和离散一致Gronwall引理，建立半离散时间近似解在H^1空间中的一致耗散性，证明存在全局吸引子

Result: 证明了对于足够小的时间步长，Cauchy单腿theta方法求解二维Navier-Stokes方程时具有一致耗散性，半离散时间近似解存在全局吸引子

Conclusion: Cauchy单腿theta方法在求解二维Navier-Stokes方程时具有良好的长时稳定性，在足够小时间步长下能保持物理系统的耗散特性，为长时间数值模拟提供了理论保证

Abstract: In this paper we study the long-time stability of the Cauchy one-leg theta-methods for the two-dimensional NavierStokes equations. We establish the uniform dissipativity in H^1, in the sense that the semi-discrete-in-time approximations possess a global attractor for a small enough time step, using the discrete Gronwall lemma and the discrete uniform Gronwall lemma.

</details>


### [94] [Deflation-PINNs: Learning Multiple Solutions for PDEs and Landau-de Gennes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27936)
*Sean Disarò,Ruma Rani Maity,Aras Bacho*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出Deflation-PINNs框架，结合PINNs和DeepONets，通过引入收缩损失项来识别PDE的多个不同解分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINNs一次只能找到一个解，无法识别非线性PDE的多个不同解分支，而许多物理系统（如液晶模型）存在多个平衡态，需要能同时发现多个解的方法。

Method: 结合PINNs和DeepONets架构，在损失函数中引入收缩项，系统性地迫使模型寻找并收敛到有限多个不同的解分支。

Result: 提供了模型收敛的理论证据，并在液晶的Landau-de Gennes模型上进行了数值实验，成功识别并表征了多个不同的晶体结构。

Conclusion: Deflation-PINNs能有效解决传统PINNs无法识别多个解的问题，为具有复杂能量景观和多平衡态的系统提供了新的求解框架。

Abstract: Nonlinear Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) are ubiquitous in mathematical physics and engineering. Although Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) have emerged as a powerful tool for solving PDE problems, they typically struggle to identify multiple distinct solutions, since they are designed to find one solution at a time. To address this limitation, we introduce Deflation-PINNs, a novel framework that integrates a deflation loss with an architecture based on PINNs and Deep Operator Networks (DeepONets). By incorporating a deflation term into the loss function, our method systematically forces the Deflation-PINN to seek and converge upon distinct finitely many solution branches. We provide theoretical evidence on the convergence of our model and demonstrate the efficacy of Deflation-PINNs through numerical experiments on the Landau-de Gennes model of liquid crystals, a system renowned for its complex energy landscape and multiple equilibrium states. Our results show that Deflation-PINNs can successfully identify and characterize multiple distinct crystal structures.

</details>


### [95] [A Generalized Matrix-Valued Allen--Cahn Model and Its Numerical Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27988)
*Yaru Liu,Chaoyu Quan,Dong Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个广义矩阵值Allen-Cahn模型，覆盖了标量、向量和方阵值Allen-Cahn方程，证明了连续模型满足最大界原理和能量耗散律，并研究了无条件保持MBP的高阶ETDRK格式及其能量耗散性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的Allen-Cahn方程从标量、向量值推广到更一般的矩阵值情形，建立统一的数学框架来处理不同维度的相场模型，同时保持重要的物理性质如最大界原理和能量耗散。

Method: 提出广义矩阵值Allen-Cahn模型，未知矩阵场属于ℝ^{m₁×m₂}；在连续层面证明解的唯一性、最大界原理和能量耗散律；在离散层面研究任意高阶指数时间差分Runge-Kutta格式，无条件保持MBP，并分析各阶格式的能量耗散性质。

Result: 连续模型存在唯一解且满足MBP和能量耗散律；一阶和二阶ETDRK格式无条件保持离散能量耗散，三阶及以上格式在适当时间步长约束下保持能量耗散；提供了时间收敛阶的严格证明；数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 成功建立了广义矩阵值Allen-Cahn模型的理论框架，设计了保持重要物理性质的高阶数值格式，为处理不同维度的相场问题提供了统一且有效的数值方法。

Abstract: This paper introduces a generalized matrix-valued Allen--Cahn model, where the unknown matrix-valued field belongs to $\mathbb{R}^{m_1\times m_2}$ with dimension $m_1\geq m_2$. By taking different values of $m_1$ and $m_2$, this model covers the classical scalar-valued, vector-valued, and square-matrix-valued Allen--Cahn equations. At the continuous level, the proposed model is proven to admit a unique solution satisfying the maximum bound principle (MBP) and the energy dissipation law. At the discrete level, a class of arbitrarily high-order exponential time differencing Runge-Kutta (ETDRK) schemes is investigated that preserve the MBP unconditionally. Moreover, we prove that the first- and second-order ETDRK schemes satisfy the discrete energy dissipation unconditionally, while third- and higher-order schemes preserve the discrete energy dissipation under suitable time-step constraints. The proof of sharp convergence order in time is provided. Numerical experiments are carried out to confirm our theoretical results.

</details>


### [96] [RBF-Generated Finite Difference Method Coupled with Quadratic Programming for Solving PDEs on Surfaces with Derivative Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28035)
*Peng Chen,Shixiao Willing Jiang,Rongji Li,Qile Yan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种结合两步切空间RBF-FD与二次规划的网格无关方法，用于稳定处理曲面PDE中的导数边界条件


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导数边界条件给曲面上的无网格PDE离散化带来挑战，特别是当域由随机采样点云表示时。现有的两步切空间RBF-FD方法在闭曲面上精度高，但在处理导数边界条件时可能失去稳定性。

Method: 将两步切空间RBF-FD离散化与二次规划(QP)过程耦合：1) 对边界附近内点使用QP稳定算子近似；2) 对边界点构建偏向共法线方向的受限最近邻模板，并使用约束二次规划近似外向共法线导数。该方法避免使用虚点，且不要求准均匀节点分布。

Result: 数值实验验证了该方法在带边界曲面上的椭圆问题、特征值问题、时间相关扩散方程和椭圆界面问题中的有效性。结果表明该方法具有稳定性能和高阶精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的耦合RBF-FD与QP的无网格方法能够稳定处理曲面PDE中的导数边界条件，在各种曲面上均表现出稳定性和高精度，无需虚点或准均匀节点分布。

Abstract: Derivative boundary conditions introduce challenges for mesh-free discretizations of PDEs on surfaces, especially when the domain is represented by randomly sampled point clouds. The recently developed two-step tangent-space RBF-generated finite difference (RBF-FD) method provides high accuracy on closed surfaces. However, it may lose stability when applied directly to surface PDEs with derivative boundary conditions. To enhance numerical stability, we develop a mesh-free method that couples the two-step tangent-space RBF-FD discretization with a quadratic programming (QP) procedure to stabilize the operator approximation for interior points near boundaries. For boundary points, we construct restricted nearest-neighbor stencils biased in the co-normal direction and employ a constrained quadratic program to approximate outward co-normal derivatives. The resulting method avoids using ghost points and does not require quasi-uniform node distributions. We validate the approach on elliptic problems, eigenvalue problems, time-dependent diffusion equations, and elliptic interface problems on surfaces with boundary. Numerical experiments demonstrate stable performance and high-order accuracy across a variety of surfaces.

</details>


### [97] [A discretization for the nonlinear parabolic evolution equation of fractional order in space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28155)
*Chien-Hong Cho,Hisashi Okamoto*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了分数阶空间非线性抛物方程的数值离散方法，使用函数分析框架定义分数阶导数而非传统Riemann-Liouville定义，并进行了数值实验和猜想验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究分数阶空间非线性抛物方程的数值解法，传统分数阶导数定义（如Riemann-Liouville）在数值计算中可能存在局限性，需要基于函数分析框架建立更合适的数值离散方法。

Method: 采用函数分析框架定义分数阶导数，而非传统Riemann-Liouville定义，提出相应的数值离散方案，并进行数值实验验证。

Result: 报告了数值实验结果，验证了所提数值方法的有效性，并基于实验结果提出了一些理论猜想。

Conclusion: 基于函数分析框架的分数阶导数定义能够有效支持非线性抛物方程的数值离散，数值实验证实了方法的可行性，为进一步理论研究提供了数值依据。

Abstract: We consider a nonlinear parabolic equation of fractional order in space and propose its numerical discretization. The fractional derivative is defined through a functional analytic setting, rather than the traditional definition of fractional derivatives such as the Riemann-Liouville derivative. Numerical experiments are reported and some conjectures are presented.

</details>


### [98] [Temperature-driven turbulence in compressible fluid flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28158)
*Eduard Feireisl,Maria Lukacova-Medvidova,Bangwei She,Yuhuan Yuan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文研究了温度驱动可压缩流动的长时间行为，证明了结构保持有限体积法的数值解生成离散吸引子，并建立了离散吸引子向连续吸引子的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究温度驱动可压缩流动的长时间动力学行为，特别是数值方法能否保持物理系统的吸引子结构，并验证数值解与理论预测的一致性。

Method: 采用结构保持有限体积法进行数值模拟，理论分析离散吸引子的存在性和收敛性，并通过Rayleigh-Benard问题的广泛数值实验进行验证。

Result: 证明了数值解生成离散吸引子且收敛到连续吸引子；数值结果表明各态历经假设成立，雷诺应力长时间保持非零；发现不变测度为高斯型，与先前猜想相悖。

Conclusion: 结构保持有限体积法能准确捕捉温度驱动可压缩流动的长时间动力学特性，离散吸引子理论收敛，且数值结果揭示了与理论预测一致但与传统猜想不同的统计特性。

Abstract: We study the long-time behaviour of the temperature-driven compressible flows. We show that numerical solutions of a structure-preserving finite volume method generate a discrete attractor that consists of entire discrete trajectories. Further, we prove the convergence of discrete attractors to their continuous counterparts. Theoretical results are illustrated by extensive numerical simulations of the well-known Rayleigh-Benard problem. The numerical results also indicate the validity of the ergodic hypothesis and imply that a non-zero Reynolds stress persist for long time. Finally, we also observe that any invariant measure is of Gaussian type in sharp contrast with the conjecture proposed by [Glimm et al., SN Applied Sciences 2, 2160 (2020)].

</details>


### [99] [Structure and symmetry of the Gross-Pitaevskii ground-state manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28174)
*Zixu Feng,Patrick Henning,Qinglin Tang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Gross-Pitaevskii能量泛函基态流形的几何结构及其对优化算法收敛性的影响，建立了Morse-Bott条件作为线性与次线性收敛的精确阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gross-Pitaevskii能量泛函基态的结构和简并在分析和计算中具有核心作用，但在对称性存在时基态流形的表征仍然是一个基本挑战。需要理解基态流形的几何结构及其对优化算法性能的影响。

Method: 建立了Morse-Bott条件作为自然且充分的准则，证明在局部极小值非唯一时，基态集合可分解为有限个嵌入子流形，每个子流形与能量泛函的内在对称性（相位移动和空间旋转）生成的轨道重合。在此基础上分析了预处理黎曼梯度法(P-RG)的局部收敛行为。

Result: 在Morse-Bott条件下，获得了最优的局部Q-线性收敛率，并证明该条件成立当且仅当P-RG生成的能量序列局部Q-线性收敛。当条件不满足时，建立了局部次线性收敛率。Morse-Bott条件成为线性与次线性收敛的精确阈值。

Conclusion: 对于Gross-Pitaevskii最小化问题，Morse-Bott条件作为精确阈值分离了线性与次线性收敛，同时决定了基态流形的对称性诱导结构。该分析将几何结构、对称性和算法性能统一在一个框架中。

Abstract: The structure and degeneracy of ground states of the Gross-Pitaevskii energy functional play a central role in both analysis and computation, yet a characterization of the ground-state manifold in the presence of symmetries remains a fundamental challenge. In this paper, we establish sharp results describing the geometric structure of local minimizers and its implications for optimization algorithms. We show that when local minimizers are non-unique, the Morse-Bott condition provides a natural and sufficient criterion under which the ground-state set partitions into finitely many embedded submanifolds, each coinciding with an orbit generated by the intrinsic symmetries of the energy functional, namely phase shifts and spatial rotations. This yields a structural characterization of the ground-state manifold purely in terms of these natural symmetries. Building on this geometric insight, we analyze the local convergence behavior of the preconditioned Riemannian gradient method (P-RG). Under the Morse-Bott condition, we derive the optimal local $Q$-linear convergence rate and prove that the condition holds if and only if the energy sequence generated by P-RG converges locally $Q$-linearly. In particular, on the ground-state set, the Morse-Bott condition is satisfied if and only if the minimizers decompose into finitely many symmetry orbits and the P-RG exhibits local linear convergence in a neighborhood of this set. When the condition fails, we establish a local sublinear convergence rate. Taken together, these results provide a precise picture: for the Gross-Pitaevskii minimization problem, the Morse-Bott condition acts as the exact threshold separating linear from sublinear convergence, while simultaneously determining the symmetry-induced structure of the ground-state manifold. Our analysis thus connects geometric structure, symmetry, and algorithmic performance in a unified framework.

</details>


### [100] [Critical phase transitions in minimum-energy configurations for the exponential kernel family $e^{-|x-y|^q}$ on the unit interval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28179)
*Michael T. M. Emmerich*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 研究单位区间上k个有序点对于有界对势K_q(d)=e^{-d^q}的最优配置，分析从无碰撞最小化到端点聚集最小化的转变，计算临界指数q_k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有界对势族K_q(d)=e^{-d^q}在单位区间上的最优点配置问题，该族在0<q<1时呈现强尖峰状核，q=1时为阈值核，q>1时为平坦高斯型核。重点分析从无碰撞最小化到端点聚集最小化的转变机制。

Method: 将问题重新表述为间隙变量，记录凸性、对称性和KKT条件，给出0<q<1时碰撞不可能的简短证明。在q=1时恢复端点聚集规律，在q>1时识别临界指数q_k。对于奇数k推导精确通用值，对于偶数k=4,6,...,20计算数值过渡值。

Result: 证明0<q<1时碰撞不可能发生；在q=1时得到端点聚集规律；对于奇数k得到精确临界值q_{2m+1}≈1.396363475；对于偶数k计算了q_4到q_20的数值临界值；将有界族与奇异Riesz核d^{-s}联系起来，并将q→0^+极限识别为[0,1]上的Fekete/Chebyshev-Lobatto配置。

Conclusion: 有界对势族K_q(d)=e^{-d^q}在单位区间上的最优点配置表现出丰富的相变行为：从0<q<1的无碰撞配置，到q=1的端点聚集，再到q>1时存在临界指数q_k，超过该值后内部点不再最优。该研究建立了有界势与奇异Riesz势之间的联系，并揭示了配置行为随参数q变化的完整图景。

Abstract: We study the optimal placement of $k$ ordered points on the unit interval for the bounded pair potential \[ K_q(d)=e^{-d^q}, \qquad q>0. \] The family interpolates between strongly cusp-like kernels for $0<q<1$, the threshold kernel $e^{-d}$, and the flatter Gaussian-type regime $q>1$. Our emphasis is on the transition from collision-free minimizers to endpoint-collapsed minimizers. We reformulate the problem in gap variables, record convexity, symmetry, and the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker conditions, and give a short proof that collisions are impossible for $0<q<1$. At the threshold $q=1$ we recover the endpoint-clustering law for $e^{-d}$, while for $q>1$ we identify critical exponents $q_k$ beyond which interior points are no longer optimal. For odd $k$ we derive the exact universal value \[ q_{2m+1} = \frac{\log(1/(-\log((1+e^{-1})/2)))}{\log 2} \approx 1.396363475, \] and for even $k=4,6,\dots,20$ we compute the numerical transition values \[ \begin{aligned} &q_4\approx 1.062682507,\quad q_6\approx 1.155601329,\quad q_8\approx 1.206132611,\quad q_{10}\approx 1.238523533,\\ &q_{12}\approx 1.261308114,\quad q_{14}\approx 1.278305167,\quad q_{16}\approx 1.291510874,\quad q_{18}\approx 1.302082885,\\ &q_{20}\approx 1.310744185. \end{aligned} \] We also include comparison tables and diagrams for the kernels $e^{-\sqrt d}$, $e^{-d}$, and $e^{-d^2}$, briefly relate the bounded family to the singular Riesz kernel $d^{-s}$, and identify the $q\to 0^+$ limit with the Fekete/Chebyshev--Lobatto configuration on $[0,1]$.

</details>


### [101] [Energy dissipation rates of ensemble eddy viscosity models of turbulence: the periodic box](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28340)
*William Layton,Nanda Nechingal Raghunathan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文证明了对于周期盒中的湍流，基于NSE系综的涡粘性方法不会过度扩散解


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统涡粘性模型通常基于Kolmogorov-Prandtl参数化，需要求解复杂的非线性对流-扩散-反应方程组，这些模型常常过度扩散，需要额外修正。而通过求解带扰动数据的NSE系综可以直接计算湍流动能，但这种方法是否会过度扩散尚未明确。

Method: 采用系综涡粘性方法，通过求解Navier-Stokes方程系综（带扰动数据）直接计算湍流动能，避免了传统模型中复杂的参数化建模过程。论文对周期盒中的湍流进行了理论分析。

Result: 证明了对于周期盒中的湍流，基于NSE系综的涡粘性方法不会过度扩散解，为这种直接计算方法提供了理论保证。

Conclusion: 系综涡粘性方法避免了传统涡粘性模型的复杂建模过程和过度扩散问题，为湍流模拟提供了一种更直接且理论上可靠的计算途径。

Abstract: Classical eddy viscosity models of turbulence add an eddy viscosity term based on the Kolmogorov-Prandtl parameterization by a turbulent length scale $l$ and a turbulent kinetic energy $k^{\prime }$. Approximations of the unknowns $l,k^{\prime }$ are typically constructed by solving multi-parameter systems of nonlinear convection-diffusion-reaction equations. Often these over-diffuse so additional fixes are added. Alternately, one can solve an ensemble of NSE's with perturbed data and simply compute directly $k^{\prime }$(without modeling). The question then arises: Does this ensemble eddy viscosity approach over-diffuse solutions? We prove herein that for turbulence in a periodic box it does not.

</details>


### [102] [A Simple Trigonometric Classification of Quintic Roots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28352)
*Sawon Pratiher*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种简单的三角方法，无需解方程即可判断五次方程实根与复根的个数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于阿贝尔-鲁菲尼定理禁止五次方程的一般代数解，因此需要一个简单的三角准则来判断其根的性质，这具有特别吸引力

Method: 将简化五次方程 $t^5 + mt^3 + nt^2 + pt + q = 0$（其中 $m < 0$）通过切比雪夫恒等式 $16\cos^5\!θ- 20\cos^3\!θ+ 5\cosθ= \cos 5θ$ 转化为三角方程 $f(θ) = α\cos^2\!θ+ β\cosθ+ \cos 5θ+ γ= 0$

Result: 该方法计算量小且概念自然，将四次方程的情况扩展到五次方程

Conclusion: 提供了一种无需解方程即可确定五次方程实根与复根个数的简单三角方法，扩展了四次方程的类似方法

Abstract: This article provides a simple trigonometric method for determining how many roots of a quintic equation are real and how many are complex, without solving the equation. The approach transforms a depressed quintic $t^5 + mt^3 + nt^2 + pt + q = 0$ with $m < 0$ into the trigonometric equation $f(θ) = α\cos^2\!θ+ β\cosθ+ \cos 5θ+ γ= 0$ via the Chebyshev identity $16\cos^5\!θ- 20\cos^3\!θ+ 5\cosθ= \cos 5θ$. The derivation is computationally light and conceptually natural, extending the quartic case to fifth-degree equations. As the Abel--Ruffini theorem forbids a general algebraic solution for the quintic, having a simple trigonometric criterion for the nature of its roots is especially appealing.

</details>


### [103] [Structure-Preserving Dynamic Mode Decomposition for Highly Oscillatory Dynamics of Semiclassical Schrödinger Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28443)
*Yizhe Feng,Weiguo Gao,Jia Yin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出两种新型数据驱动的动态模态分解方法（Crank-Nicolson DMD和半隐式DMD），用于高效准确预测半经典薛定谔方程的高度振荡动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DMD方法直接建模波函数动力学，缺乏对物理守恒定律的显式考虑。需要开发能够保证物理保真度、具有内置模型降阶能力且无需额外降维预处理的方法。

Method: 基于学习薛定谔算子，显式结合质量和能量守恒定律。提出Crank-Nicolson DMD和半隐式DMD两种方法，具有内置模型降阶能力。

Result: 提供了训练和预测误差分析的理论保证。数值实验证明方法具有噪声鲁棒性、计算高效性，并能迁移到其他方程。

Conclusion: 提出的两种DMD方法通过显式结合物理守恒定律，实现了对半经典薛定谔方程高度振荡动力学的高效准确预测，具有物理保真度和模型降阶优势。

Abstract: We propose two novel data-driven dynamic mode decomposition (DMD)-type methods, the Crank--Nicolson DMD and the semi-implicit DMD, to predict the highly oscillatory dynamics of the semiclassical Schrödinger equations efficiently and accurately. Unlike many existing DMD-type methods which directly models the dynamics of the wave function, our approach is based on learning the Schrödinger operator while explicitly incorporating mass and energy conservation laws. This approach ensures physical fidelity and endows the resulting methods with built-in model order reduction capabilities, without the necessity for additional dimensionality-reduction preprocessing. An analysis of training and prediction errors are given for theoretical guarantees. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate the noise robustness, computational efficiency, and transferability to other equations of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [104] [Quantum Enhanced Numerical Homogenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28521)
*Loïc Balazi,Matthias Deiml,Daniel Peterseim*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种结合经典粗尺度求解器与量子细尺度修正的数值均匀化方法，用于求解具有粗糙系数的标量线性偏微分方程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有粗糙系数的标量线性偏微分方程的数值均匀化问题，传统方法在细尺度分辨率高时计算成本大，需要利用量子计算的优势来提高效率

Method: 基于局部正交分解思想，将经典粗尺度求解器与量子局部问题求解器结合，通过量子局部求解器高效捕获细尺度特征，仅需对量子代表性体积元进行选择性测量

Result: 量子局部求解器能够以足够精度获得解，其操作次数仅随细尺度分辨率的对数增长，由扩散系数中编码的最小长度尺度决定，不依赖问题的周期性

Conclusion: 该方法成功整合了经典粗尺度求解与量子细尺度修正，克服了量子接口的信息瓶颈，通过二维测试案例展示了其潜力，为多尺度问题的高效求解提供了新途径

Abstract: We propose a numerical homogenization method for scalar linear partial differential equations with rough coefficients, that integrates classical coarse-scale solvers with quantum subroutines for fine-scale corrections. Inspired by the Localized Orthogonal Decomposition, we employ quantum local problem solvers to capture fine-scale features efficiently. Crucially, the approach does not rely on the periodicity of the problem, and the integration of the quantum computation within a coarse model requires only selected measurements of the quantum representative volume elements, overcoming the information bottleneck of quantum interfaces that could eliminate the speed-up. We demonstrate that the local quantum solver can achieve solutions with sufficient accuracy, with a number of operations that scales only logarithmically with the fine-scale resolution, determined by the smallest length scale encoded in the diffusion coefficient. The potential of the approach is illustrated through two-dimensional test cases, using a classical simulation of the local quantum solver.

</details>


### [105] [Row-Splitting ILU Preconditioners for Sparse Least-Squares Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28642)
*Jennifer Scott,Miroslav Tůma*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种基于ILU和行分割策略的预处理方法，用于解决超定最小二乘问题，避免形成法方程，特别适用于法矩阵病态或相对稠密的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超定最小二乘问题的预处理研究相对较少，设计既有效又内存高效的方法具有挑战性。现有方法如基于法方程的不完全Cholesky分解在处理病态或稠密问题时效果有限。

Method: 提出基于ILU的行分割策略预处理方法：1) 通过不完全LU分解识别条件良好的方阵子矩阵；2) 将不完全因子与剩余行的代数修正相结合；3) 避免形成法方程，适用于法矩阵病态或相对稠密的问题。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法与Krylov子空间求解器结合使用时效果显著，能够超越基于法方程不完全Cholesky分解的预处理方法，包括稀疏-稠密问题，其中分割策略能自然隔离稠密行。

Conclusion: 提出的ILU基行分割预处理方法为超定最小二乘问题提供了一种有效且内存高效的解决方案，特别适用于法矩阵病态或包含稠密行的问题，在多种实际应用测试中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Preconditioning for overdetermined least-squares problems has received comparatively little attention, and designing methods that are both effective and memory-efficient remains challenging. We propose a class of ILU-based preconditioners built around a row-splitting strategy that identifies a well-conditioned square submatrix via an incomplete LU factorization and combines its incomplete factors with algebraic corrections from the remaining rows. This construction avoids forming the normal equations and is well suited to problems for which the normal matrix is ill-conditioned or relatively dense. Numerical experiments on test problems arising from practical applications illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach when used with a Krylov subspace solver and demonstrate it can outperform preconditioners based on incomplete Cholesky factorization of the normal equations, including for sparse-dense problems, where the splitting naturally isolates dense rows.

</details>


### [106] [Resonant solutions and (in)stability of the linear wave equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28661)
*Giancarlo Sangalli,Davide Terazzi,Pietro Zanotti*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 重新分析声波方程，探讨经典适定性是否意味着解空间与数据空间之间存在同构，这对离散化方法的设计与分析很重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究经典适定性是否隐含解空间与数据空间之间的同构关系，这对离散化方法的设计与分析具有重要意义。

Method: 使用特征函数展开将原始偏微分方程简化为常微分方程组，在inf-sup理论框架下分析，并为数据空间配备共振感知范数以建立同构。

Result: 指出经典Bochner空间的选择由于共振波的存在与同构存在不兼容，但通过为数据空间配备合适的共振感知范数，可以在每个方程上建立同构。

Conclusion: 经典适定性不自动保证解空间与数据空间之间的同构，但通过适当选择范数可以建立这种同构关系，这对其他时间依赖线性偏微分方程也适用。

Abstract: We revise the analysis of the acoustic wave equation, addressing the question whether the classical well-posedness implies the existence of an isomorphism between prescribed solution and data spaces. This question is of interest for the design and the analysis of discretization methods. Expanding on existing results, we point out that established choices of solution and data space in terms of classical Bochner spaces must be expected to be incompatible with the existence of such an isomorphism, because of resonant waves. We formulate this observation in the language of the so-called inf-sup theory, with the help of an eigenfunction expansion, which reduces the original partial differential equation to a system of ordinary differential equations. We further verify that an isomorphism can be established, for each equation in the system, upon equipping the data space with a suitable resonance-aware norm. In the appendix, we extend our results to other time-dependent linear PDEs.

</details>


### [107] [A Scalable Monolithic Modified Newton Multigrid Framework for Time-Dependent $p$-Navier-Stokes Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28706)
*Nils Margenberg,Carolin Mehlmann*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种可扩展的单调修正牛顿框架，用于处理(p,δ)-Navier-Stokes模型的张量积时空有限元离散化，通过使用替代雅可比矩阵改善剪切稀化区域的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在剪切稀化区域(1<p<2)，特别是当p→1和δ→0时，精确的本构切线矩阵条件数很差，这会损害牛顿全局化和线性系统的预处理效果，需要开发更稳健的求解方法。

Method: 开发了单调修正牛顿框架，用条件数更好的替代矩阵替换雅可比作用中的精确本构切线；采用矩阵自由算子评估、单调多重网格V循环预处理器、保阶降阶Gauss-Radau时间积分，以及具有单时间点系数冻结的不精确时空Vanka光滑器。

Result: 证明了在一致椭圆区域(ν_∞>0)中线性化粘性-Nitsche项的强制性，以及降阶时间积分的一致性；数值测试显示了对模型参数、非线性和线性迭代次数的鲁棒性，以及可扩展的并行性能。

Conclusion: 该框架为(p,δ)-Navier-Stokes模型的张量积时空有限元离散化提供了可扩展且稳健的求解方法，特别适用于剪切稀化区域，通过改进的线性化策略和高效预处理技术实现了良好的计算性能。

Abstract: Fully implicit tensor-product space-time discretizations of time-dependent $(p,δ)$-Navier-Stokes models yield, on each time step, large nonlinear monolithic saddle-point systems. In the shear-thinning regime $1<p<2$, especially as $p\downarrow 1$ and $δ\downarrow 0$, the decisive difficulty is the constitutive tangent: its ill-conditioning impairs Newton globalization and the preconditioning of the arising linear systems. We therefore develop a scalable monolithic modified Newton framework for tensor-product space-time finite elements in which the exact constitutive tangent in the Jacobian action is replaced by a better-conditioned surrogate. Picard and exact Newton serve as reference linearizations within the same algebraic framework. Scalability is achieved through matrix-free operator evaluation, a monolithic multigrid V-cycle preconditioner, order-preserving reduced Gauss-Radau time quadrature, and an inexact space-time Vanka smoother with single-time-point coefficient freezing in local patch matrices. We prove coercivity of the linearized viscous-Nitsche term in the uniformly elliptic regime $ν_\infty>0$ and consistency of the reduced time quadrature. Numerical tests demonstrate robustness with respect to model parameters, nonlinear and linear iteration counts, and scalable parallel performance.

</details>
