# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Meta-Contrastive Learning for Vision-Language Models via Task-Adaptive CLIP Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27091)
*Merham Fouladvand,Peuroly Batra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出Domain-Conditioned Meta-Contrastive Learning框架，通过域条件元学习提升视觉语言模型的跨域泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比学习模型（如CLIP）依赖全局目标，未显式处理域偏移问题，限制了跨域泛化能力

Method: 将多模态学习构建为双层元学习问题，引入域嵌入调制图像和文本表示，通过梯度内环更新优化模型对域特定分布的快速适应，并加入跨域对齐正则化鼓励域不变表示

Result: 预期提升模型在域偏移下的鲁棒性和少样本适应性能，兼容标准对比训练流程，适用于自然和医疗等异构数据集

Conclusion: 该框架为可扩展的多模态学习提供了有前景的方向，通过显式建模域条件元学习来增强跨域泛化能力

Abstract: We propose Domain-Conditioned Meta-Contrastive Learning, a framework for improving the cross-domain generalization of vision-language models. While contrastive models such as CLIP achieve strong performance through large-scale training, they rely on a global objective that does not explicitly account for domain shift. To address this limitation, we formulate multimodal learning as a bilevel meta-learning problem over domain-conditioned tasks. Specifically, we introduce domain embeddings that modulate image and text representations, and optimize the model for rapid adaptation to domain-specific distributions via gradient-based inner-loop updates. In addition, we incorporate a cross-domain alignment regularization to encourage domain-invariant representations. Our approach is compatible with standard contrastive training pipelines and can be applied to heterogeneous datasets spanning natural and medical domains. We expect improved robustness under domain shift and enhanced few-shot adaptation performance, highlighting a promising direction for scalable multimodal learning.

</details>


### [109] [On the Convergence Rate of the One-Hop Transfer Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27172)
*Ruichao Jiang,Long Wen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析了Jiang等人提出的链上一跳交换路由算法的收敛速率，证明了算法在O(Nκlog(1/ε))轮内终止，其中N是AMM数量，κ是流动性异质性参数，ε是容差参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jiang等人提出的转移算法解决了链上一跳交换路由问题，但只证明了算法的收敛性，未分析其收敛速率。本文旨在填补这一空白，为算法提供明确的时间复杂度界限。

Method: 通过理论分析，推导出转移算法的收敛速率上界。使用参数化分析，考虑AMM数量(N)、流动性异质性参数(κ)和容差参数(ε)对收敛时间的影响。

Result: 证明了算法在最多O(Nκlog(1/ε))轮内终止，为原算法的收敛速率提供了严格的理论保证。

Conclusion: 本文完善了链上一跳交换路由算法的理论分析，提供了明确的收敛速率界限，增强了算法的实用性和可预测性。

Abstract: The transfer algorithm~\cite{jiang} solves the on-chain one-hop swap routing problem. In \cite{jiang}, the convergence is proved but the convergence rate is left open. We prove that the algorithm terminates in at most $\mathcal{O}(Nκ\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon})$ rounds, where $N$ is the number of AMMs, $κ$ a liquidity heterogeneity parameter and $\varepsilon$ a tolerance parameter.

</details>


### [110] [Linear-quadratic mixed Stackelberg-zero-sum game for mean-field regime switching system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27208)
*Pengyan Huang,Na Li,Zuo Quan Xu,Harry Zheng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究具有马尔可夫切换的线性二次Stackelberg微分博弈，包含一个领导者和两个追随者，追随者进行零和博弈，系统包含条件均值场项，应用于产品定价问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文的动机源于产品定价问题，需要研究具有马尔可夫切换的Stackelberg微分博弈框架，其中包含一个领导者和两个追随者，且系统包含条件均值场项，以更好地建模现实世界中的动态竞争环境。

Method: 采用连续方法和归纳法建立条件均值场前向-后向随机微分方程的存在唯一性；基于此证明追随者和领导者哈密顿系统的唯一可解性；运用随机最大值原理、解耦方法和最优滤波技术获得最优反馈策略；最后将理论结果应用于产品定价问题并进行数值模拟。

Result: 成功建立了条件均值场前向-后向随机微分方程的存在唯一性定理；证明了追随者和领导者哈密顿系统的唯一可解性；获得了两个追随者和领导者的最优反馈策略；将理论应用于产品定价问题并通过数值模拟验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了具有马尔可夫切换的线性二次Stackelberg微分博弈问题，建立了完整的理论框架，获得了最优反馈策略，并将理论应用于产品定价问题，为实际经济决策提供了数学工具。

Abstract: Motivated by a product pricing problem, a linear-quadratic Stackelberg differential game for a regime switching system involving one leader and two followers is studied. The two followers engage in a zero-sum differential game, and both the state system and the cost functional incorporate a conditional mean-field term. Applying continuation method and induction method, we first establish the existence and uniqueness of a conditional mean-field forward-backward stochastic differential equation with Markovian switching. Based on it, we prove the unique solvability of Hamiltonian systems for the two followers and the leader. Moreover, utilizing stochastic maximum principle, decoupling approach and optimal filtering technique, the optimal feedback strategies of two followers and leader are obtained. Employing the theoretical results, we solve a product pricing problem with some numerical simulations.

</details>


### [111] [Size-Selective Threshold Harvesting under Nonlocal Crowding and Exogenous Recruitment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27368)
*Jiguang Yu,Louis Shuo Wang,Ye Liang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一个非线性、尺寸结构的鱼类种群的无限时域生物经济最优控制问题，采用McKendrick-von Foerster偏微分方程建模，证明了最优尺寸选择性捕捞策略是严格的bang-bang阈值策略，并通过大西洋鳕鱼案例验证了经济最优与生物可持续性的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的内生繁殖框架无法准确描述增强型渔业或高度补贴种群的生态压力，需要开发一个能够反映密度依赖个体生长和自然死亡率的非局部拥挤指数模型，以解决尺寸结构种群的最优管理问题。

Method: 使用McKendrick-von Foerster偏微分方程建模种群动态，特征为严格外生下边界补充和非局部拥挤指数；引入弱耦合近似和单交叉假设，通过Pontryagin型最大值原理推导一阶必要条件，证明bang-bang阈值策略的最优性。

Result: 建立了无捕捞稳态剖面的存在唯一性；提出了适用于外生强迫系统的内在替代指数；数学证明了最优尺寸选择性捕捞策略是严格的bang-bang阈值政策；大西洋鳕鱼案例显示最优最小捕捞尺寸阈值（66.45厘米）能维持内在替代指数大于1。

Conclusion: 精确靶向的尺寸结构捕捞能够无缝统一经济最大化与长期生物可持续性，为增强型渔业或高度补贴种群的管理提供了理论框架和实用工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we formulate and analyze an original infinite-horizon bioeconomic optimal control problem for a nonlinear, size-structured fish population. Departing from standard endogenous reproduction frameworks, we model population dynamics using a McKendrick--von Foerster partial differential equation characterized by strictly exogenous lower-boundary recruitment and a nonlocal crowding index. This nonlocal environment variable governs density-dependent individual growth and natural mortality, accurately reflecting the ecological pressures of enhancement fisheries or heavily subsidized stocks. We first establish the existence and uniqueness of the no-harvest stationary profile and introduce a novel intrinsic replacement index tailored to exogenously forced systems, which serves as a vital biological diagnostic rather than a classical persistence threshold. To maximize discounted economic revenue, we derive formal first-order necessary conditions via a Pontryagin-type maximum principle. By introducing a weak-coupling approximation to the adjoint system and applying a single-crossing assumption, we mathematically prove that the optimal size-selective harvesting strategy is a rigorous bang-bang threshold policy. A numerical case study calibrated to an Atlantic cod (\textit{Gadus morhua}) fishery bridges our theoretical framework with applied management. The simulations confirm that the economically optimal minimum harvest size threshold ($66.45$ cm) successfully maintains the intrinsic replacement index above unity, demonstrating that precisely targeted, size-structured harvesting can seamlessly align economic maximization with long-run biological viability.

</details>


### [112] [The Risk Quadrangle in Optimization: An Overview with Recent Results and Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27370)
*Bogdan Grechuk,Anton Malandii,Terry Rockafellar,Stan Uryasev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文回顾并扩展了Rockafellar和Uryasev于2013年提出的风险四边形框架，该框架统一了风险管理、优化和统计估计，并介绍了新的四边形类型和理论扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视和扩展风险四边形框架，以应对2013年以来该领域的发展，提供更全面的理论框架来统一风险管理、优化和统计估计方法。

Method: 采用风险四边形框架，包含风险、偏差、遗憾和误差四个随机函数及其关联统计量；引入"次正则性"公理以放宽原有正则性限制；扩展主要定理和连接关系；提出新的四边形类型（超分位数、超分位数范数、期望分位数、偏置均值、分位数对称平均并集和φ-散度四边形）。

Result: 建立了更广泛的风险四边形理论框架，能够处理更多应用场景；展示了新四边形在机器学习、统计学、金融和PDE约束优化等领域的应用潜力；通过投资组合优化、回归和分类示例证明了该框架的优势。

Conclusion: 扩展后的风险四边形框架提供了更灵活和强大的统一方案，特别在风险敏感决策中表现出色，其理论扩展和新的四边形类型为多学科应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: This paper revisits and extends the 2013 development by Rockafellar and Uryasev of the Risk Quadrangle (RQ) as a unified scheme for integrating risk management, optimization, and statistical estimation. The RQ features four stochastics-oriented functionals -- risk, deviation, regret, and error, along with an associated statistic, and articulates their revealing and in some ways surprising interrelationships and dualizations. Additions to the RQ framework that have come to light since 2013 are reviewed in a synthesis focused on both theoretical advancements and practical applications. New quadrangles -- superquantile, superquantile norm, expectile, biased mean, quantile symmetric average union, and $\varphi$-divergence-based quadrangles -- offer novel approaches to risk-sensitive decision-making across various fields such as machine learning, statistics, finance, and PDE-constrained optimization. The theoretical contribution comes in axioms for ``subregularity'' relaxing ``regularity'' of the quadrangle functionals, which is too restrictive for some applications. The main RQ theorems and connections are revisited and rigorously extended to this more ample framework. Examples are provided in portfolio optimization, regression, and classification, demonstrating the advantages and the role played by duality, especially in ties to robust optimization and generalized stochastic divergences.

</details>


### [113] [Multidimensional Gradient-MUSIC: A Global Nonconvex Optimization Framework for Optimal Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27379)
*Albert Fannjiang,Weilin Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出多维Gradient-MUSIC方法用于从噪声样本中估计非谐波信号的频率，建立了扰动MUSIC函数的结构理论，证明其构成可优化景观，并在立方体和球体采样几何下验证了理论，获得了最优误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统频率估计方法需要处理非凸优化问题，本文旨在开发基于信号子空间的频率恢复方法，通过理解扰动MUSIC函数的几何特性，建立可构造的全局优化框架。

Method: 提出多维Gradient-MUSIC方法，将MUSIC函数视为编码相关信息的非凸目标函数。建立结构理论证明扰动MUSIC函数构成可优化景观：可通过粗阈值化高效找到合适初始点，梯度下降收敛到相关局部极小值，且这些极小值满足定量误差界。

Result: 在立方体和球体两种典型采样几何下验证了理论条件。对于边长为4m的立方体上的格点采样，当真实频率分离至少β_d/m且噪声ℓ∞范数至多ε时，Gradient-MUSIC恢复频率的误差至多为C_d ε/m，该缩放关于m和ε是最小最大最优的。在高斯噪声下误差改进为C_d σ√log(m)/m^{1+d/2}，达到噪声超分辨率缩放。

Conclusion: 该理论不仅存在性证明，而且提供了多维最优分辨率的构造性全局优化框架。通过理解扰动MUSIC函数的几何特性，实现了在确定性和随机噪声下的均匀非渐近恢复保证，获得了最优误差缩放。

Abstract: We develop a multidimensional version of Gradient-MUSIC for estimating the frequencies of a nonharmonic signal from noisy samples. The guiding principle is that frequency recovery should be based only on the signal subspace determined by the data. From this viewpoint, the MUSIC functional is an economical nonconvex objective encoding the relevant information, and the problem becomes one of understanding the geometry of its perturbed landscape.
  Our main contribution is a general structural theory showing that, under explicit conditions on the measurement kernel and the perturbation of the signal subspace, the perturbed MUSIC function is an admissible optimization landscape: suitable initial points can be found efficiently by coarse thresholding, gradient descent converges to the relevant local minima, and these minima obey quantitative error bounds. Thus the theory is not merely existential; it provides a constructive global optimization framework for multidimensional optimal resolution.
  We verify the abstract conditions in detail for two canonical sampling geometries: discrete samples on a cube and continuous samples on a ball. In both cases we obtain uniform, nonasymptotic recovery guarantees under deterministic as well as stochastic noise. In particular, for lattice samples in a cube of side length $4m$, if the true frequencies are separated by at least $β_d/m$ and the noise has $\ell^\infty$ norm at most $\varepsilon$, then Gradient-MUSIC recovers the frequencies with error at most \[ C_d \frac{\varepsilon}{m}, \] where $C_d, β_d>0$ depend only on the dimension. This scaling is minimax optimal in $m$ and $\varepsilon$. Under stationary Gaussian noise, the error improves to \[ C_d\frac{σ\sqrt{\log(m)}}{m^{1+d/2}}. \] This is the noisy super-resolution scaling: (see paper for rest of abstract)

</details>


### [114] [Dynamic Constrained Stabilization on the $n$-sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27382)
*Mayur Sawant,Abdelhamid Tayebi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种带约束邻近动态阻尼机制的控制策略，用于解决n-球面上二阶系统的约束镇定问题，确保目标点的安全且几乎全局渐近稳定


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决n-球面上二阶系统在星形约束条件下的约束镇定问题，特别是在姿态控制等应用中需要确保系统状态在约束区域内收敛到目标点

Method: 提出基于约束邻近的动态阻尼控制策略，该机制能够适应约束边界，确保系统在星形约束下安全收敛到目标点

Result: 证明了该方法能够实现目标点的安全且几乎全局渐近稳定，并展示了该方法可应用于约束刚体姿态镇定问题，通过2-球面上的仿真验证了有效性

Conclusion: 所提出的带约束邻近动态阻尼的控制策略有效解决了n-球面上二阶系统的约束镇定问题，为姿态控制等应用提供了理论框架和实用方法

Abstract: We consider the constrained stabilization problem of second-order systems evolving on the n-sphere. We propose a control strategy with a constraint proximity-based dynamic damping mechanism that ensures safe and almost global asymptotic stabilization of the target point in the presence of star-shaped constraints on the n-sphere. It is also shown that the proposed approach can be used to deal with the constrained rigid-body attitude stabilization. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is demonstrated through simulation results on the 2-sphere in the presence of star-shaped constraint sets.

</details>


### [115] [Control Forward-Backward Consistency: Quantifying the Accuracy of Koopman Control Family Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27548)
*Masih Haseli,Jorge Cortés,Joel W. Burdick*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 将前向-后向一致性指数从无输入系统的Koopman建模扩展到控制系统，为Koopman控制族模型提供闭式可计算精度度量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为控制系统中的数据驱动Koopman模型提供严格的、闭式可计算的误差界限，解决现有方法缺乏理论保证的问题

Method: 基于前向-后向回归视角，引入控制前向-后向一致性矩阵，证明其良好性质，建立KCF函数预测器相对均方根误差的严格界限

Result: KCF函数预测器的相对均方根误差严格受控于一致性指数的平方根，该指数定义为一致性矩阵的最大特征值，为有限维KCF模型提供尖锐闭式误差界

Conclusion: 提出的控制一致性指数为Koopman控制模型提供了理论保证的精度度量，可融入基于优化的建模框架，并通过仿真验证了方法有效性

Abstract: This paper extends the forward-backward consistency index, originally introduced in Koopman modeling of systems without input, to the setting of control systems, providing a closed-form computable measure of accuracy for data-driven models associated with the Koopman Control Family (KCF). Building on a forward-backward regression perspective, we introduce the control forward-backward consistency matrix and demonstrate that it possesses several favorable properties. Our main result establishes that the relative root-mean-square error of KCF function predictors is strictly bounded by the square root of the control consistency index, defined as the maximum eigenvalue of the consistency matrix. This provides a sharp, closed-form computable error bound for finite-dimensional KCF models. We further specialize this bound to the widely used lifted linear and bilinear models. We also discuss how the control consistency index can be incorporated into optimization-based modeling and illustrate the methodology via simulations.

</details>


### [116] [Huber-based Robust System Identification with Near-Optimal Guarantees Across Independent and Adversarial Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27586)
*Jihun Kim,Javad Lavaei*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于Huber的估计器，通过阈值常数μ平衡均值和中位数估计，在线性参数化非线性系统的单轨迹识别中，对零均值噪声和非零均值对抗攻击均具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态系统面临两种极端扰动：持续零均值独立噪声和稀疏非零均值对抗攻击。传统方法中，最小二乘等均值估计器适合前者，ℓ₁范数等中位数估计器适合后者，但缺乏统一框架处理两种极端情况。

Method: 提出基于Huber的估计器，引入阈值常数μ，用于从长度为T的单轨迹中识别线性参数化非线性系统的控制矩阵。该方法在均值和基于中位数的估计之间架起桥梁，在温和假设下对两种极端扰动场景实现可证明的鲁棒误差。

Result: 对于在零点附近具有正概率密度的持续零均值噪声，若扰动对称或基函数线性，估计器达到O(1/√T)误差率；对于每次发生概率小于0.5的任意非零均值攻击，误差以O(μ)为界。实验验证了将该方法集成到SINDy等框架中可实现对离散时间系统的鲁棒识别。

Conclusion: Huber基估计器为动态系统识别提供了统一框架，能同时处理零均值噪声和非零均值对抗攻击两种极端扰动场景，在理论上保证鲁棒性，并通过实验验证了在实际识别框架中的有效性。

Abstract: Dynamical systems can confront one of two extreme types of disturbances: persistent zero-mean independent noise, and sparse nonzero-mean adversarial attacks, depending on the specific scenario being modeled. While mean-based estimators like least-squares are well-suited for the former, a median-based approach such as the $\ell_1$-norm estimator is required for the latter. In this paper, we propose a Huber-based estimator, characterized by a threshold constant $μ$, to identify the governing matrix of a linearly parameterized nonlinear system from a single trajectory of length $T$. This formulation bridges the gap between mean- and median-based estimation, achieving provably robust error in both extreme disturbance scenarios under mild assumptions. In particular, for persistent zero-mean noise with a positive probability density around zero, the proposed estimator achieves an $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$ error rate if the disturbance is symmetric or the basis functions are linear. For arbitrary nonzero-mean attacks that occur at each time with probability smaller than 0.5, the error is bounded by $\mathcal{O}(μ)$. We validate our theoretical results with experiments illustrating that integrating our approach into frameworks like SINDy yields robust identification of discrete-time systems.

</details>


### [117] [Optimal Switching in Networked Control Systems: Finite Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27833)
*Abdullah Y. Etcibasi,C. Emre Koksal,Eylem Ekici*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在独立同分布零均值对称扰动下，切换LQR问题的分离原理成立，并求解了动态规划问题，发现最优切换策略是基于最近更新后累积扰动的对称阈值规则，而最优控制器是与切换策略无关的折扣线性反馈律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究切换线性二次调节器（LQR）问题，特别是在存在随机扰动的情况下，旨在理解分离原理是否成立以及如何找到最优的切换和控制策略。

Method: 首先证明了在独立同分布零均值对称分布扰动下，切换LQR问题的分离原理成立。然后通过求解动态规划问题，推导出最优切换策略和最优控制器的具体形式。

Result: 证明了分离原理成立，最优切换策略是基于最近更新后累积扰动的对称阈值规则，最优控制器是与切换策略无关的折扣线性反馈律。

Conclusion: 在独立同分布零均值对称扰动下，切换LQR问题存在分离原理，最优策略具有简洁的解析形式：切换决策基于累积扰动的阈值规则，而控制采用折扣线性反馈。

Abstract: In this work, we first prove that the separation principle holds for switched LQR problems under i.i.d. zero-mean disturbances with a symmetric distribution. We then solve the dynamic programming problem and show that the optimal switching policy is a symmetric threshold rule on the accumulated disturbance since the most recent update, while the optimal controller is a discounted linear feedback law independent of the switching policy.

</details>


### [118] [Tightening CVaR Approximations via Scenario-Wise Scaling for Chance-Constrained Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27957)
*Rui Chen,Nan Jiang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出场景式缩放方法增强CVaR对机会约束规划的逼近，通过重新加权场景减少保守性，建立最优性条件并开发高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机会约束规划(CCP)在不确定性决策中建模能力强，但非凸可行域导致计算困难。现有CVaR凸内逼近方法过于保守且次优，需要更紧的逼近方法。

Method: 提出场景式缩放方法，在CVaR约束中引入缩放因子重新加权各场景，形成一族更紧的内逼近。建立缩放因子选择的最优性充分条件，开发启发式和序列凸逼近算法迭代更新缩放因子。

Result: 证明缩放因子优化是NP-hard问题，但在适当条件下缩放CVaR逼近能达到与原始CCP相同最优值。数值实验表明该方法显著优于标准CVaR和现有凸逼近方法。

Conclusion: 场景式缩放方法有效减少CVaR逼近的保守性，在保持可处理性的同时获得更优解，为机会约束规划提供了实用的计算框架。

Abstract: Chance-constrained programs (CCPs) provide a powerful modeling framework for decision-making under uncertainty, but their nonconvex feasible regions make them computationally challenging. A widely used convex inner approximation replaces chance constraints with Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) constraints; however, the resulting solutions can be overly conservative and suboptimal. We propose a scenario-wise scaling approach that strengthens CVaR approximations for CCPs with finitely supported uncertainty. The method introduces scaling factors that reweight individual scenarios within the CVaR constraint, yielding a family of potentially tighter inner approximations. We establish sufficient conditions under which, for a suitable choice of scaling factors, the scaled CVaR approximation attains the same optimal value as the original CCP and admits a (near-)optimal solution of the CCP. We show that these conditions are tight and further relax them in the convex setting. We also show that optimizing over scenario-wise scaling factors is NP-hard. To address this computational challenge, we develop efficient heuristic and sequential convex approximation algorithms that iteratively update the scaling factors and generate improved feasible solutions. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed methods consistently improve upon standard CVaR and state-of-the-art convex approximations, often reducing conservativeness while maintaining tractability.

</details>


### [119] [A Framework for Exploring Social Interactions in Multiagent Decision-Making for Two-Queue Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27972)
*Mallory E. Gaspard,Naomi Ehrich Leonard*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于非线性意见动力学的多智能体排队决策框架，用内部意见状态替代传统优化决策，通过社会交互影响排队选择行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统排队模型假设智能体基于优化和理性响应进行决策，忽略了社会交互对集体行为的影响。需要新框架来捕捉社会交互如何改变纯粹理性响应，并解决排队选择中的犹豫不决问题

Method: 引入内部意见状态，由非线性意见动力学驱动，表示智能体在两个可用队列间偏好的演化强度。意见状态受社会交互影响，决定行为决策（如加入或切换队列）。提出新的排队模型子类，证明参数条件保证马尔可夫链在有限期望时间内达到底层拥塞博弈的纳什均衡

Result: 证明了充分参数条件确保系统在有限期望时间内达到纳什均衡。通过数值模拟展示了社会交互和个体对系统信息访问如何塑造集体行为，验证了框架的丰富性

Conclusion: 非线性意见动力学为多智能体排队决策提供了灵活且鲁棒的框架，能够捕捉社会交互影响，打破决策犹豫，并保证系统收敛到纳什均衡，丰富了排队系统集体行为的建模能力

Abstract: We introduce a new framework for multiagent decision-making in queueing systems that leverages the agility and robustness of nonlinear opinion dynamics to break indecision during queue selection and to capture the influence of social interactions on collective behavior. Queueing models are central to understanding multiagent behavior in service settings. Many prior models assume that each agent's decision-making process is optimization-based and governed by rational responses to changes in the queueing system. Instead, we introduce an internal opinion state, driven by nonlinear opinion dynamics, that represents the evolving strength of the agent's preference between two available queues. The opinion state is influenced by social interactions, which can modify purely rational responses. We propose a new subclass of queueing models in which each agent's behavioral decisions (e.g., joining or switching queues) are determined by this evolving opinion state. We prove a sufficient parameter condition that guarantees the Markov chain describing the evolving opinion and queueing system states reaches the Nash equilibrium of an underlying congestion game in finite expected time. We then explore the richness of the new framework through numerical simulations that illustrate the role of social interactions and an individual's access to system information in shaping collective behavior.

</details>


### [120] [Efficient modeling of chemotherapy regimens using mixed-integer linear programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28218)
*Alain Billionnet*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何确定最小成本的治疗方案，以将癌症肿瘤大小维持在患者舒适生活的水平。通过混合整数线性规划方法，可以高效求解最优治疗方案，并分析预算增加对生活质量的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是确定最小成本的治疗方案，使癌症肿瘤大小维持在患者能够舒适生活的水平。需要在治疗过程中平衡治疗效果（肿瘤缩小）与治疗成本，同时考虑肿瘤的自然生长规律。

Method: 方法包括：1）证明简单启发式解能提供最优治疗方案；2）展示Gompertz函数和指数函数都可以用不显式依赖时间的简单递推关系描述；3）利用对数函数特性将问题转化为混合整数线性规划问题；4）使用现有求解器高效求解；5）扩展分析包括确定最相关的等效最优解和测量治疗预算边际增加对生活质量的影响。

Result: 研究结果表明：1）可以通过混合整数线性规划高效求解最优治疗方案；2）能够确定多个等效最优解中最相关的方案；3）可以量化治疗预算边际增加对患者生活质量的影响；4）通过大量计算实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 结论是该方法能够有效确定最小成本的治疗方案，维持肿瘤在舒适水平。将问题转化为混合整数线性规划使得求解高效且可扩展，能够处理多个实际问题扩展，为临床决策提供量化支持。

Abstract: In this article, we focus on determining a minimum-cost treatment program aimed at maintaining the size of a cancerous tumor at a level that allows the patient to live comfortably. At each predetermined point in a treatment horizon, the patient either receives drug treatment or does not. In the first case, the tumor shrinks and its size is multiplied by a constant factor lower than 1; in the second, it grows following an exponential or Gompertz growth law. We first demonstrate that a simple heuristic solution provides an optimal treatment program. We then show that the Gompertz function can be described, like the exponential function, by a simple recurrence relation that does not explicitly depend on time. Thanks to the characteristics of the logarithmic function, this property allows us to formulate the problem as a mixed-integer linear program. This result makes it possible to solve the problem very efficiently using one of the many solvers available to handle this type of program, and above all to consider and solve several extensions to the problem. In particular, we show how to determine which of the optimal equivalent solutions to the initial problem are the most relevant. We also show how to measure the effect of a marginal increase in the treatment budget on patient quality of life. Numerous computational experiments are presented to illustrate these issues.

</details>


### [121] [Bundle EXTRA for Decentralized Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28220)
*Haijuan Liu,Zhuoqing Zheng,Cong Li,Wenying Xu,Xuyang Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种完全去中心化的bundle EXTRA方法，通过用更精确的多切割bundle模型替代EXTRA中的一阶泰勒近似，提高了收敛速度和步长鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化原始-对偶方法广泛用于解决去中心化优化问题，但其更新通常依赖于目标函数的可能粗糙的一阶泰勒近似，这会限制收敛速度。需要更精确的近似模型来提升性能。

Method: 将EXTRA（可解释为原始-对偶方法）原始更新中的一阶泰勒近似替换为更精确的多切割bundle模型，构建完全去中心化的bundle EXTRA方法。bundle模型整合历史信息以提高近似精度。

Result: 在温和假设下，证明了KKT残差收敛到零。在去中心化最小二乘问题上的数值实验表明，与EXTRA相比，bundle EXTRA方法收敛更快，且对步长选择更具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过引入多切割bundle模型替代传统一阶泰勒近似，提出的bundle EXTRA方法显著提升了去中心化优化的收敛速度和步长鲁棒性，为去中心化优化算法提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Decentralized primal-dual methods are widely used for solving decentralized optimization problems, but their updates often rely on the potentially crude first-order Taylor approximations of the objective functions, which can limit convergence speed. To overcome this, we replace the first-order Taylor approximation in the primal update of EXTRA, which can be interpreted as a primal-dual method, with a more accurate multi-cut bundle model, resulting in a fully decentralized bundle EXTRA method. The bundle model incorporates historical information to improve the approximation accuracy, potentially leading to faster convergence. Under mild assumptions, we show that a KKT residual converges to zero. Numerical experiments on decentralized least-squares problems demonstrate that, compared to EXTRA, the bundle EXTRA method converges faster and is more robust to step-size choices.

</details>


### [122] [Optimal control with the shifted proper orthogonal decomposition via a first-reduce-then-optimize framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28442)
*Tobias Breiten,Shubhaditya Burela,Philipp Schulze*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对输运主导PDE的最优控制问题计算成本高的问题，提出使用移位POD降阶模型替代完整PDE系统，并在1D线性平流方程上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输运主导偏微分方程的最优控制问题在高维系统中计算成本高昂，需要开发有效的降阶模型来替代完整PDE系统以降低计算复杂度。

Method: 采用移位适当正交分解作为降阶模型，首先构建降阶模型，然后对降阶系统进行优化。在1D线性平流方程问题上验证方法，并与标准POD方法进行性能比较。

Result: 证明了降阶模型解的存在性和唯一性，以及最优控制的存在性。移位POD方法在计算性能上优于标准POD方法。

Conclusion: 移位POD是一种有效的降阶建模技术，特别适用于输运主导现象，能够显著降低最优控制问题的计算成本，为高维PDE系统的控制问题提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Solving optimal control problems for transport-dominated partial differential equations (PDEs) can become computationally expensive, especially when dealing with high-dimensional systems. To overcome this challenge, we focus on developing and deriving reduced-order models that can replace the full PDE system in solving the optimal control problem. Specifically, we explore the use of the shifted proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) as a reduced-order model, which is particularly effective for capturing low-dimensional representations of high-fidelity transport-dominated phenomena. In this work, a reduced-order model is constructed first, followed by the optimization of the reduced system. We consider a 1D linear advection equation problem and prove existence and uniqueness of solutions for the reduced-order model as well as the existence of an optimal control. Moreover, we compare the computational performance of the shifted POD method against the standard POD.

</details>


### [123] [Nonlinear Trajectory Optimization Models for Energy-Sharing UAV-UGV Systems with Multiple Task Locations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28447)
*Minsen Yuan,Amanuel Adane,James Humann,Yue Yu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种用于无人机-无人车能量共享系统的平滑非线性规划模型，通过平滑参数化地形约束和近似处理分离约束，避免整数规划，显著降低计算时间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机-无人车能量共享系统在基础设施稀疏环境中能延长无人机续航，但现有轨迹优化方法面临无人车地形访问约束的离散性和任务调度复杂性挑战，需要更高效的优化模型

Method: 提出平滑非线性规划模型，平滑参数化无人车地形访问约束，支持部分无人机充电，引入分离约束的平滑近似，避免整数规划，使用非线性规划算法高效求解

Result: 在单无人机-单无人车多任务场景中，相比混合整数非线性规划，计算时间减少数个数量级

Conclusion: 所提平滑非线性规划模型能有效处理无人机-无人车能量共享系统的轨迹优化问题，避免整数规划的计算负担，显著提升求解效率

Abstract: Energy-sharing UAV-UGV systems extend the endurance of Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) by leveraging Uncrewed Ground Vehicles (UGVs) as mobile charging stations, enabling persistent autonomy in infrastructure-sparse environments. Trajectory optimization for these systems is often challenging due to UGVs' terrain access constraints and the discrete nature of task scheduling. We propose a smooth nonlinear program model for the joint trajectory optimization for these systems. Unlike existing models, the proposed model allows smooth parameterization of UGVs' terrain access constraints and supports partial UAV recharging. Further, it introduces a smooth approximation of disjunctive constraints that eliminates the need for computationally expensive integer programming and enables efficient solutions via nonlinear programming algorithms. We demonstrate the proposed model on a one-UAV-one-UGV system with multiple task locations. Compared with mixed-integer nonlinear programs, this model reduces the computation time by orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [124] [Yau's Affine Normal Descent: Algorithmic Framework and Convergence Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28448)
*Yi-Shuai Niu,Artan Sheshmani,Shing-Tung Yau*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: YAND是一种基于仿射微分几何的优化框架，利用水平集超曲面的等仿射法线定义搜索方向，具有仿射不变性和自适应各向异性曲率的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统优化方法对坐标变换敏感，特别是在强各向异性缩放条件下性能下降。作者希望开发一种几何框架，能够保持仿射不变性并自适应地适应各向异性曲率。

Method: 提出YAND方法，从仿射微分几何中引入等仿射法线作为搜索方向。在严格凸二次目标下，该方法等价于牛顿方向；对于一般光滑目标，开发基于线搜索的YAND算法，并建立收敛性理论。

Result: 证明了在严格凸二次目标下，仿射法线方向与牛顿方向共线，在线搜索下可实现一步收敛。对于一般光滑目标，建立了全局收敛性、强凸性和Polyak-Lojasiewicz条件下的线性收敛性，以及非退化极小值点附近的二次局部收敛性。

Conclusion: YAND提供了一种几何优雅且理论完备的优化框架，具有仿射不变性、自适应各向异性曲率和鲁棒性，特别是在强各向异性缩放条件下表现出优越性能。

Abstract: We propose Yau's Affine Normal Descent (YAND), a geometric framework for smooth unconstrained optimization in which search directions are defined by the equi-affine normal of level-set hypersurfaces. The resulting directions are invariant under volume-preserving affine transformations and intrinsically adapt to anisotropic curvature. Using the analytic representation of the affine normal from affine differential geometry, we establish its equivalence with the classical slice-centroid construction under convexity. For strictly convex quadratic objectives, affine-normal directions are collinear with Newton directions, implying one-step convergence under exact line search. For general smooth (possibly nonconvex) objectives, we characterize precisely when affine-normal directions yield strict descent and develop a line-search-based YAND. We establish global convergence under standard smoothness assumptions, linear convergence under strong convexity and Polyak-Lojasiewicz conditions, and quadratic local convergence near nondegenerate minimizers. We further show that affine-normal directions are robust under affine scalings, remaining insensitive to arbitrarily ill-conditioned transformations. Numerical experiments illustrate the geometric behavior of the method and its robustness under strong anisotropic scaling.

</details>


### [125] [Tracking controllability on moving targets for parabolic equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28449)
*Jone Apraiz,Jon Asier Bárcena-Petisco,Judit Muñoz-Matute*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究一维抛物型方程在边界控制下的跟踪可控性，证明单边界点控制单目标点，双边界点控制双目标点，并通过数值验证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一维抛物型方程在边界控制下的跟踪可控性问题，探索如何通过边界控制近似控制方程在特定点上的解

Method: 1. 通过对偶结果最小化相应问题研究固定目标的控制性；2. 应用变换将移动点问题转化为固定目标问题；3. 数值求解控制问题并计算近似解和目标

Result: 证明单边界点控制可控制单个目标点，双边界点控制可控制最多两个目标点；数值计算结果验证了理论方法的有效性

Conclusion: 成功建立了一维抛物型方程边界控制下的跟踪可控性理论框架，为固定和移动目标点的控制问题提供了系统解决方案

Abstract: In this paper, we study the tracking controllability of a 1D parabolic type equation. Notably, with controls acting on the boundary, we seek to approximately control the solution of the equation on specific points of the domain. We prove that acting on one boundary point, we control the solution on one target point, whereas acting on two boundary points, we can control the solution on up to two target points. In order to do so, when the target is fixed, we study the controllability by minimizing the corresponding problem with duality results. Afterwards, we study the controllability on moving points by applying a transformation that takes the problem to a fixed target. Lastly, we also solve some of these control problems numerically and compute approximations of the solutions and the desired targets, which validates our theoretical methodology.

</details>


### [126] [Constrained Optimization on Matrix Lie Groups via Interior-Point Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28747)
*Aclécio J. Santos,Jean C. Pereira,Guilherme V. Raffo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于矩阵李群上约束优化的内点法框架（MLG-IPM），直接利用李群结构，避免冗余矩阵表示，在SO(n)和SL(n)问题上比黎曼内点法表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决定义在矩阵李群上的约束优化问题，现有黎曼内点法依赖黎曼度量和冗余表示，需要更直接利用群结构的方法来提高效率和数值稳定性。

Method: 提出MLG-IPM方法：1) 使用最小李代数参数化直接操作群结构；2) 建立原对偶形式，通过敏感性和曲率矩阵构建牛顿系统；3) 通过指数映射进行乘法更新，确保内在可行性；4) 采用障碍策略保持松弛变量和对偶变量的严格正性。

Result: 在SO(n)和SL(n)优化问题上，MLG-IPM相比黎曼内点法具有更高的成功率、更少的迭代次数和更优的数值精度，且在扰动下表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MLG-IPM为结构化流形优化提供了可靠替代方案，直接利用李群结构避免了冗余表示，在收敛性、效率和数值稳定性方面均优于传统黎曼内点法。

Abstract: This paper proposes an interior-point framework for constrained optimization problems whose decision variables evolve on matrix Lie groups. The proposed method, termed the Matrix Lie Group Interior-Point Method (MLG-IPM), operates directly on the group structure using a minimal Lie algebra parametrization, avoiding redundant matrix representations and eliminating explicit dependence on Riemannian metrics. A primal-dual formulation is developed in which the Newton system is constructed through sensitivity and curvature matrices. Also, multiplicative updates are performed via the exponential map, ensuring intrinsic feasibility with respect to the group structure while maintaining strict positivity of slack and dual variables through a barrier strategy. A local analysis establishes quadratic convergence under standard regularity assumptions and characterizes the behavior under inexact Newton steps. Statistical comparisons against Riemannian Interior-Point Methods, specifically for optimization problems defined over the Special Orthogonal Group SO(n) and Special Linear Group SL(n), demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves higher success rates, fewer iterations, and superior numerical accuracy. Furthermore, its robustness under perturbations suggests that this method serves as a consistent and reliable alternative for structured manifold optimization.

</details>

### [127] [Updates on the DEAP-3600 experiment and steps towards the ARGO experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27372)
*Susnata Seth*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: DEAP-3600是最大的单相液氩暗物质探测器，已设定了最严格的WIMP-nucleon自旋无关截面排除限，并正在升级以降低α背景，同时为下一代300吨ARGO探测器提供设计输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DEAP-3600作为世界上最大的单相液氩暗物质探测器，旨在探测WIMP暗物质和超重多散射暗物质候选者，通过改进液氩性质理解和位置重建技术，以及降低残余α背景，提高探测灵敏度。

Method: 利用DEAP-3600数据研究液氩性质和位置重建技术，进行硬件升级以缓解残余α背景，同时通过模拟研究放射性中子背景及其缓解策略，为下一代ARGO探测器设计提供输入。

Result: DEAP-3600已设定了WIMP-nucleon自旋无关截面的最严格排除限（质量>30 GeV/c²），在氩上提供了对超重多散射暗物质的领先灵敏度，并推进了液氩性质理解和背景抑制技术。

Conclusion: DEAP-3600作为领先的暗物质探测器，通过持续改进和背景抑制，为暗物质搜索提供了重要限制，同时为下一代300吨ARGO探测器的设计和开发奠定了基础，将显著提高对稀有暗物质相互作用的灵敏度。

Abstract: The DEAP-3600 experiment, with an approximately 3.3 tonne liquid argon (LAr) target, is currently the world's largest single-phase LAr dark matter detector. It is located 2 km underground at SNOLAB, Canada, one of the most radiopure underground laboratories. With excellent pulse-shape discrimination against low-energy beta decays and precise position reconstruction, DEAP-3600 has set the most stringent WIMP-nucleon spin-independent cross-section exclusion limits for masses above 30 GeV/c$^{2}$ on argon and provided leading sensitivity to superheavy, multi-scattering dark matter candidate. Here we report the recent advances in understanding LAr properties and position reconstruction techniques using DEAP-3600 data along with hardware upgrades to mitigate residual challenging $α$-backgrounds for WIMP search. As a part of Global Argon Dark Matter Collaboration (GADMC), the next-generation ARGO detector, featuring a 300-tonne fiducial LAr mass, is under development to significantly enhance sensitivity to rare dark matter interactions. Simulation-based studies of radiogenic neutron backgrounds and their mitigation strategies provide essential input to this design and will be described here.

</details>

Zheng,T. Zhou,X. Zhou,Y. Zhou,V. Zhovkovska,L. Z. Zhu,X. Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,Q. Zou,D. Zuliani,G. Zunica*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: LHCb实验测量了B介子衰变中的CP不对称性，发现B⁰→Ds⁻D⁺的CP不对称性接近零，而Bs⁰→Ds⁺D⁻的CP不对称性为0.103±0.053±0.010，两者均与CP对称性一致


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量B介子衰变中的CP不对称性对于检验标准模型和寻找新物理至关重要。B⁰和Bs⁰介子的衰变过程可以提供关于CP破坏机制的重要信息，特别是在强子衰变通道中

Method: 使用LHCb实验收集的质子-质子对撞数据，对应积分亮度为9fb⁻¹。测量了B⁰→Ds⁻D⁺和Bs⁰→Ds⁺D⁻衰变中的组合CP不对称性，采用统计和系统不确定性分析方法

Result: A_CP(B⁰→Ds⁻D⁺) = 0.0009±0.0053±0.0040，A_CP(Bs⁰→Ds⁺D⁻) = 0.103±0.053±0.010。这是Bs⁰衰变中该不对称性的首次测量，也是B⁰衰变中最精确的测量结果

Conclusion: 两个测量结果均与CP对称性一致，未发现显著的CP破坏效应。这些结果为标准模型的CP破坏机制提供了重要约束，并为未来更精确的测量奠定了基础

Abstract: Measurements of the combined CP asymmetries in $\kern 0.18em\overline{\kern -0.18em B}^0 \to D_s^- D^+$ and $\kern 0.18em\overline{\kern -0.18em B}_s^0 \to D_s^+ D^-$ decays are made using proton-proton collision data collected by the LHCb experiment, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 9fb$^{-1}$. The measurements are found to be \begin{aligned} A_{CP}(\kern 0.18em\overline{\kern -0.18em B}^0 \to D_s^- D^+) &= 0.0009 \pm 0.0053 \pm 0.0040, \\ A_{CP}(\kern 0.18em\overline{\kern -0.18em B}_s^0 \to D_s^+ D^-) &= 0.103\phantom{0} \pm 0.053\phantom{0} \pm 0.010, \end{aligned} where the first and second uncertainties are statistical and systematic, respectively. This is the first measurement of this asymmetry in $\kern 0.18em\overline{\kern -0.18em B}_s^0$ decays, and the most precise measurement to date for $\kern 0.18em\overline{\kern -0.18em B}^0$ decays. Both measurements are found to be consistent with CP symmetry.

</details>

R. Zhou,X. Y. Zhou,Y. X. Zhou,Y. Z. Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,J. H. Zou*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BESIII首次观测到Λc⁺→nπ⁺η衰变，测量了分支比并设定了na₀(980)⁺过程的限制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究粲重子Λc⁺的稀有衰变过程，探索其衰变机制和可能的中间共振态，特别是寻找Λc⁺→nπ⁺η衰变和可能的Λc⁺→na₀(980)⁺过程

Method: 使用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上收集的6.1 fb⁻¹数据（质心能量4.600-4.843 GeV），采用基于Transformer架构的深度学习方法来区分信号与强子背景，并进行系统误差量化

Result: 首次观测到Λc⁺→nπ⁺η衰变，统计显著性9.5σ；测量分支比比值B(Λc⁺→nπ⁺η)/B(Λc⁺→Λπ⁺η)=0.155±0.031(stat)±0.012(syst)；绝对分支比B(Λc⁺→nπ⁺η)=(2.94±0.59±0.23±0.13)×10⁻³；对Λc⁺→na₀(980)⁺过程设定上限B(Λc⁺→na₀(980)⁺)×B(a₀(980)⁺→π⁺η)<8.4×10⁻⁴（90%置信水平）

Conclusion: 成功观测到Λc⁺→nπ⁺η衰变，为粲重子衰变研究提供了重要数据；未发现显著的Λc⁺→na₀(980)⁺信号，设定了该过程的限制；基于Transformer的深度学习方法有效提升了信号识别能力

Abstract: By analysing 6.1 ${\rm fb}^{-1}$ of data collected at center-of-mass energies between $\sqrt{s}=4.600$ and 4.843 $\rm GeV$ with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider, we observe the decay $Λ_c^+\to nπ^+η$ for the first time with a statistical significance of $9.5σ$. The ratio of branching fractions $\mathcal{B}(Λ_c^+\to nπ^+η)/\mathcal{B}(Λ_c^+\to Λπ^+η)$ is measured to be $0.155\pm0.031_{\rm stat.}\pm0.012_{\rm syst.}$ Taking the world average of $\mathcal{B}(Λ_c^+\to Λπ^+η)$ as reference, the absolute branching fraction is calculated to be $\mathcal{B}(Λ_c^+\to nπ^+η)=(2.94\pm0.59_{\rm stat.}\pm0.23_{\rm syst.}\pm0.13_{\rm ref.})\times10^{-3}$. The intermediate process $Λ_c^+\to na_0(980)^+$ is also searched for in the $π^+η$ invariant mass spectrum. Since no significant signal is found, the upper limit on $\mathcal{B}(Λ_c^+\to na_0(980)^+)\times\mathcal{B}(a_0(980)^+\toπ^+η)$ is set to $8.4\times10^{-4}$ at 90\% confidence level. A sophisticated deep learning approach using a Transformer-based architecture is employed to distinguish signals from prevalent hadronic backgrounds, complemented by thorough validation and systematic uncertainty quantification.

</details>

Zamora Saa,M. Zavertyaev,M. Zdybal,F. Zenesini,C. Zeng,M. Zeng,S. H Zeng,C. Zhang,D. Zhang,D. Zhang,J. Zhang,L. Zhang,L. Zhang,R. Zhang,S. Zhang,S. L. Zhang,Y. Zhang,Z. Zhang,J. Zhao,Y. Zhao,A. Zhelezov,S. Z. Zheng,X. Z. Zheng,Y. Zheng,T. Zhou,X. Zhou,V. Zhovkovska,L. Z. Zhu,X. Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,D. Zuliani,G. Zunica*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: LHCb首次观测到双粲重子Ξ_{cc}^+，通过其衰变到Λ_c^+ K^- π^+末态，统计显著性超过7σ，这是LHCb Run 3探测器首次发现的新粒子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找和确认双粲重子Ξ_{cc}^+的存在，这是重子谱学中的重要粒子，有助于理解量子色动力学和重子结构。

Method: 使用LHCb Run 3探测器收集的质子-质子对撞数据，质心能量13.6 TeV，积分亮度6.9 fb^{-1}，通过Ξ_{cc}^+衰变到Λ_c^+ K^- π^+末态进行观测。

Result: 首次观测到Ξ_{cc}^+，统计显著性>7σ；测量其质量为3619.97±0.83(统计)±0.26(系统)^{+1.90}_{-1.30}(寿命) MeV/c^2；测得Ξ_{cc}^+与Ξ_{cc}^{++}质量差为-1.77±0.84±0.15^{+1.90}_{-1.30} MeV/c^2。

Conclusion: 成功观测到双粲重子Ξ_{cc}^+，这是LHCb Run 3探测器的首个新粒子发现，为研究重子结构和强相互作用提供了重要数据。

Abstract: The first observation of the doubly charmed baryon $\itΞ_{cc}^+$ is reported through its decay to the $\itΛ_c^+ K^-π^+$ final state, with a statistical significance exceeding seven standard deviations. The observation is made using proton-proton collision data collected in 2024 with the LHCb Run 3 detector at a center-of-mass energy of 13.6 TeV, corresponding to a total integrated luminosity of $6.9\,\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$. The $\itΞ_{cc}^+$ mass is measured to be $3619.97 \pm 0.83 \pm 0.26 \,^{+1.90}_{-1.30}\,\mathrm{MeV}/c^2$, where the first uncertainty is statistical, the second is systematic, and the third is due to the unknown $\itΞ_{cc}^+$ lifetime, which is assumed to lie in the range 15-160 fs with a baseline value of 45 fs. The difference between the masses of the $\itΞ_{cc}^+$ and $\itΞ_{cc}^{++}$ baryons is determined to be $-1.77 \pm 0.84 \pm 0.15 \,^{+1.90}_{-1.30}\,\mathrm{MeV}/c^2$. This is the first observation of a new particle made with the LHCb Run 3 detector.

</details>


### [131] [Searching for the Dark Photon with PADME](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28685)
*Kalina Dimitrova*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: PADME实验通过正负电子湮灭和丢失质量技术探测暗光子，本文介绍了数据分析技术、背景成分及抑制方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测暗光子这种假想的规范玻色子，它负责可见世界与隐藏世界之间的相互作用，对于理解暗物质和超出标准模型物理至关重要

Method: 采用正电子束与固定靶中电子湮灭过程，利用丢失质量技术：如果湮灭产生一个可见光子和一个暗光子，通过电磁量能器探测可见光子，结合已知束流能量重建暗光子质量

Result: 论文介绍了PADME实验正在采用的数据分析技术，包括背景成分分析和抑制程序，但未提供具体的实验结果或探测限

Conclusion: PADME实验建立了探测暗光子的完整分析框架，包括背景识别和抑制方法，为未来数据分析和暗光子搜索奠定了基础

Abstract: The PADME Experiment at Laboratori Nationali di Frascati is designed to search for the Dark Photon, a hypothetical gauge boson responsible for the interaction between the visible and the hidden sector. PADME explores the process of annihilation of beam positrons with the electrons in a fixed target, employing the missing mass technique: in case the annihilation results in the associate production of one visible and one Dark photon, the first can be registered by the experiment's electromagnetic calorimeter and the Dark Photon mass can be reconstructed knowing the beam energy. This paper presents the analysis techniques that are being employed for the PADME data, as well as the background composition and rejection procedure.

</details>


### [132] [Inclusive jet cross section in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 200$ and $510$ GeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28695)
*The STAR Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: STAR实验组在RHIC上测量了质子-质子碰撞中双微分喷注截面，作为喷注横动量和赝快度的函数，中心质心能量为200和510 GeV，用于约束质子中胶子的部分子分布函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RHIC的质子-质子碰撞中，喷注产生主要由胶子-胶子和夸克-胶子部分子过程主导，这为直接探测质子中的胶子部分子分布函数提供了独特机会，与深度非弹性散射形成互补。特别是在TeV尺度对撞机数据约束较差的x_T区域，RHIC数据能提供重要约束。

Method: 使用STAR探测器在RHIC上测量质子-质子碰撞中的喷注产生。通过离轴锥方法校正喷注横动量中的底层事件贡献。测量双微分喷注截面作为喷注横动量p_T和赝快度η的函数，中心质心能量为√s = 200和510 GeV。

Result: 在中间赝快度区域(|η| < 0.9)，数据覆盖了0.07 < x_T < 0.5 (200 GeV)和0.03 < x_T < 0.31 (510 GeV)的动力学范围，这些区域正是胶子部分子分布函数约束较差的范围。测量结果与使用多种最新部分子分布函数集的次次领头阶微扰量子色动力学计算进行了比较。

Conclusion: 这些结果将进一步约束胶子部分子分布函数，帮助调优蒙特卡洛生成器，并为研究夸克-胶子等离子体提供关键参考数据。RHIC数据在TeV尺度对撞机约束较差的x_T区域提供了独特的胶子部分子分布函数约束。

Abstract: Jets are collimated clusters of particles formed by the hadronization of partons following a hard interaction. In proton-proton ($pp$) collisions at the Relativistic Heavy Ion Collider (RHIC), jet production is dominated by $gg$ and $qg$ partonic processes, allowing us to directly probe the gluon parton distribution function (PDF) in the proton in a way complementary to deep inelastic scattering. In this paper, we report the double-differential inclusive-jet cross sections as a function of jet transverse momentum, $p_{\rm T}$, and pseudorapidity, $η$, at center-of-mass energies $\sqrt{s} = 200$ and $510$~GeV, from $pp$ collisions studied with the STAR detector. The jet $p_{\rm T}$ is corrected for underlying event contributions by applying an off-axis cone method. At mid-pseudorapidity, $|η| < 0.9$, the kinematic coverage of our data extends to $0.07 < x_{\rm T} \text{ (}= 2p_{\rm T}{} / \sqrt{s} \text{)} < 0.5$ and $0.03 < x_{\rm T} < 0.31$ at $\sqrt{s} = 200$~and 510 GeV, respectively, where the gluon PDF is poorly constrained by the TeV-scale $pp$~($p\bar{p}$) colliders. The inclusive jet cross sections are compared to the next-to-next-to-leading order perturbative quantum chromodynamics calculations using several recent PDF sets as inputs. These results will further constrain the gluon PDF, help tune Monte Carlo generators, and provide critical reference data needed to study the quark-gluon plasma.

</details>
