# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [ASAS-SN Rates IV: Constraints on the Kilonova Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26871)
*Dhvanil D. Desai,Benjamin J. Shappee,Christopher S. Kochanek,Krzysztof Z. Stanek,Katie Auchettl,John F. Beacom,Jeff Cooke,Subo Dong,Willem B. Hoogendam,Jose L. Prieto,Todd A. Thompson,Michael A. Tucker,Natasha Van Bemmel*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 使用ASAS-SN巡天11年数据寻找千新星，未发现任何事件，得到千新星本地体积率上限为4400 yr⁻¹ Gpc⁻³，比引力波估计高18倍


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 千新星是中子星并合的电磁对应体，也是宇宙r过程核合成的主要场所，但其本征发生率仍受限于观测事件稀少。需要利用现有巡天数据更好地约束千新星发生率。

Method: 使用ASAS-SN（全自动超新星全天巡天）2014-2024年共11年的数据，通过注入-恢复模拟评估巡天对千新星的完备性。采用基于唯一充分采样千新星SSS17a（AT 2017gfo）早期蓝光发射校准的激波冷却茧模型。

Result: 在11年巡天中未发现任何千新星事件。计算得到千新星本地体积率在2σ（约95%）置信水平上的上限为R_KN < 4400 yr⁻¹ Gpc⁻³。虽然ASAS-SN极限星等较浅，但其连续、高时间分辨率、全天监测使其约束能力与最强电磁巡天结果相当，但仍比LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA GWTC-4估计的双中子星并合率高18倍。

Conclusion: ASAS-SN巡天为千新星发生率提供了重要约束，表明明亮、邻近千新星事件稀少。结果与引力波估计存在差异，可能反映了千新星亮度分布或并合产物多样性。需要更多千新星探测来更好约束发生率及其与引力波事件的关系。

Abstract: Kilonovae (KNe) are the electromagnetic signatures of neutron star mergers and are likely the dominant site of cosmic $r$-process nucleosynthesis. However, their intrinsic rate remains poorly constrained due to a paucity of confirmed events. We use the All-Sky Automated Survey for Supernovae (ASAS-SN) to place limits on the rate of bright, nearby KNe over an 11-year baseline ranging from 2014 to 2024. To evaluate the survey's completeness for KNe, we employ an injection-recovery simulation using a shock-cooling cocoon model calibrated to the early blue emission of the only well-sampled KN, SSS17a (AT 2017gfo). Finding no KNe within the survey, we calculate a $2σ$ ($\sim95\%$) upper limit on the local volumetric KN rate of $R_{\mathrm{KN}} < 4400\,\mathrm{yr}^{-1}\,\mathrm{Gpc}^{-3}$. Despite ASAS-SN's shallower limiting magnitude compared to other time-domain searches, its continuous, high-cadence, all-sky monitoring yields a constraint that is competitive with the strongest results from electromagnetic surveys but remains a factor of 18 higher than the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA GWTC-4 estimate of the binary neutron star merger rate.

</details>


### [134] [Identifying heating processes in simulations with an entropy-based scheme: A single jet episode in a galaxy cluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26882)
*Moun Meenakshi,Rainer Weinberger,Christoph Pfrommer,Thomas Berlok*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究通过追踪MHD模拟中的被动熵标量，系统分析了AGN喷流在星系团中的加热机制，发现轻喷流主要通过湍流耗散加热，而较密喷流则通过激波加热主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解星系团中的加热过程对于预测辐射冷却和恒星形成的调控至关重要，同时需要阐明冷核星系团中AGN反馈的机制。研究旨在探究AGN喷流如何将热量沉积到星系团内介质中。

Method: 使用磁流体动力学模拟追踪被动熵标量，系统分离不同加热通道的贡献。通过理想化测试验证方法，包括湍流加热、各向异性Braginskii粘性加热、激波加热和宇宙射线通过阿尔芬波激发和阻尼的加热。模拟了不同密度的高功率单次喷发喷流在星系团环境中的演化。

Result: 轻喷流产生更宽的气泡，置换更多核心气体；较密喷流能更有效地传播到更大距离而不显著扰动中心区域。早期演化中激波加热主导；后期轻喷流主要通过湍流耗散加热，而较密喷流继续通过激波耗散能量。湍流和混合驱动加热在茧内占主导，激波和声学压缩在外部占主导。轻喷流的前向激波迅速减弱，而密喷流能维持强弓形激波到较大距离。

Conclusion: 该加热估计方法能够识别主导加热机制，有助于在未来自调节AGN喷流模拟中解决冷却流问题，为理解AGN反馈如何调节星系团热平衡提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Understanding heating processes in galaxy clusters is essential for predicting the regulation of radiative cooling and star formation, and for clarifying the mechanisms underlying active galactic nucleus (AGN) feedback in cool-core clusters. We investigate the processes through which AGN jets deposit heat into the intracluster medium (ICM) by tracking passive entropy scalars in magneto-hydrodynamic (MHD) simulations. This enables us to systematically disentangle the contributions from different heating channels. We successfully validate this method with several idealized tests, including turbulent heating, heating by anisotropic Braginskii viscosity, dissipative and adiabatic heating by shocks using in-situ shock-detection methods, and cosmic ray (CR) heating through the excitations and damping of Alfvén waves. Using this methodology, we simulate single-epoch outbursts of high-power jets with varying densities in a cluster environment. Light jets produce wider bubbles, displacing a larger fraction of the gas in the cluster's core, whereas comparatively denser jets propagate more efficiently to larger distances without significantly disturbing the central region. During early evolution, shock heating dominates for the jets irrespective of their densities. At later times, light jets primarily heat the ICM through turbulent dissipation, while the denser jets continue to dissipate most of their energy via shocks. Turbulent and/or mixing-driven heating prevails inside the cocoon, whereas shock and acoustic compressions dominate outside. In light jets, the forward shock weakens rapidly, whereas dense jets can sustain strong bow shocks to large distances. This heating estimator allows us to identify the dominant heating mechanism responsible for resolving the cooling flow problem in future self-regulated AGN jet simulations.

</details>


### [135] [Why Stellar Sequences Turn Over: Fixed Points, Instability, and Equation-of-State Universality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26973)
*Isaac Legred,Nicolas Yunes*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 将恒星结构方程重新表述为动力系统，发现恒星序列最大质量源于相对论区域存在不动点，该不动点直接关联质量-半径曲线的转折，并解释了多个状态方程不敏感关系


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究恒星序列最大质量的理论起源，解释质量-半径曲线转折的物理机制，探索状态方程不敏感关系的理论基础

Method: 将Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程重新表述为动力系统，识别相对论区域的不动点，分析弱相对论极限下的"可压缩极限"，推导牛顿和后牛顿区域的最大质量普适关系

Result: 发现最大质量对应动力系统的不动点，该不动点直接导致质量-半径曲线转折；识别弱相对论极限下的"可压缩极限"普适结构；推导出牛顿和后牛顿区域的最大质量普适关系；结合天体物理约束表明J0740+6620脉冲星不太可能位于TOV最大质量附近，除非状态方程在其中心密度之上存在强一级相变

Conclusion: 恒星序列最大质量源于相对论区域动力系统的不动点，这解释了质量-半径曲线转折和状态方程不敏感关系；理论预测与观测约束结合表明，除非存在强一级相变，否则J0740+6620脉冲星不太接近TOV最大质量

Abstract: We reformulate the stellar structure equations in the language of dynamical systems and show that the maximum mass of stellar sequences arises from the existence of a fixed point in the relativistic regime. In an appropriate representation of the Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equations, this fixed point becomes manifest and is directly associated with the turnover of the mass-radius curve. The existence of a fixed point implies an effective reduction in dimensionality near the onset of instability, which provides a simple explanation for several equation-of-state-insensitive relations and predicts new ones. In the weakly relativistic limit, we identify a complementary universal structure shared by stellar sequences at their maximum mass, which we term the "compressible limit," and derive distinct universal relations governing the maximum mass in the Newtonian and post-Newtonian regimes. Combining these theoretical results with current astrophysical constraints, we show that the J0740+6620 pulsar is unlikely to lie near the Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff maximum mass unless the equation of state exhibits a strong first-order phase transition at densities just above its central density.

</details>


### [136] [Unequal Mass Binary Evolution Driven by High Mach Circumbinary Disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27022)
*Madeline Clyburn,Jonathan Zrake*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 研究不同质量比黑洞双星在环双星气体盘中的轨道演化，发现质量比、马赫数和粘性共同决定扭矩方向和吸积偏好，影响双星轨道演化趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不同质量比黑洞双星在环双星气体盘中的气体驱动轨道演化机制，特别是中等质量比双星系统的演化特性，填补该参数空间的研究空白。

Method: 采用二维网格流体动力学模拟，固定双星间距运行上千轨道周期，研究质量比q=0.05-1.0范围，分析马赫数和粘性变化对CBD施加的扭矩和分量吸积率的影响。

Result: 等质量双星在低马赫数盘中受正扭矩，马赫数>25时转为负扭矩；质量比降低时转变点移向更高马赫数；q<0.1时马赫数~52以下无扭矩反转；低质量比双星中次级黑洞无法有效排斥CBD，反而吸积大部分流入气体；中等质量比双星在低粘性环境中出现偏向主黑洞的吸积。

Conclusion: 中等质量比双星在CBD中的轨道演化频率可能较低，但具体取决于质量比、盘温和粘性之间的复杂相互作用，挑战了传统吸积偏向次级黑洞的经验法则。

Abstract: We present a study of the gas-driven orbital evolution of unequal mass black hole binaries with circumbinary gas disks (CBDs), varying Mach number and viscosity (nu). Using two-dimensional grid-based hydrodynamics simulations spanning a thousand binary orbits at fixed separation, we explore low to moderate mass ratios (q = 0.05-1.0) and examine how variations in Mach and q affect the torques and component accretion rates exerted by the CBD and consequently the binary evolution. Equal mass binary systems receive positive torques in low-mach disks but transition to negative torques for Mach >25. As q decreases, the transition moves to higher Mach numbers. For q<0.1, we find no torque sign reversal below Mach~52, except in sufficiently low-viscosity disks. We find that the secondary black hole cannot effectively repel the CBD, it instead accretes most of the inflowing gas from the CBD; these low mass ratio binaries in high viscosity disks therefore tend to outspiral, although inspiral can occur in less viscous environments. We also find that binaries with mass ratios in the range of 0.25 - 0.5 can show preferential accretion favoring the primary when the gas viscosity is low, exemplifying an exception to the established rule of thumb that accretion favors the secondary. We discuss differences between our results and those reported in the literature on the orbital evolution and preferential accretion, and emphasize that our simulations extend into a regime that remains largely unexplored. Overall, our results suggest that intermediate mass ratio inspirals (IMRIs) in CBDs may be less frequent, but this depends sensitively on the interplay between mass ratio, disk temperature, and viscosity.

</details>


### [137] [Comparison between axisymmetric numerical magnetohydrodynamical simulations and self-similar solutions of jet-emitting disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27079)
*N. Zimniak,C. Zanni,J. Ferreira*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究通过2.5D数值模拟验证了喷流发射盘(JED)自相似解析解的正确性，解决了长期存在的数值模拟与解析解之间的质量损失率差异问题，并确认JED是动力学吸引子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，喷流发射盘(JED)的自相似解析解与数值模拟之间存在质量损失率差异，这引发了对解析方法、数值方法或两者的质疑。研究旨在弥合这两种互补方法之间的差距，解决这一长期存在的问题。

Method: 采用2.5D（轴对称）模拟，研究具有与解析JED解相同参数集的电阻性和粘性吸积盘。通过比较数值模拟结果与解析解，验证两者的一致性，并测试系统从不同初始条件向稳态解的收敛性。

Result: 数值模拟与解析解显示出几乎完美的一致性，解决了之前观察到的张力问题。模拟还证实JED表现为动力学吸引子：从不同初始条件出发，系统始终收敛到预期的稳态解。

Conclusion: 自相似解为吸积-喷射物理提供了有价值的见解，但2.5D模拟依赖于α参数化，强烈依赖于湍流项的假设。相比之下，3D模拟能捕捉湍流，但模拟大尺度天体物理系统时计算成本过高。作者主张使用全局3D模拟研究湍流，并为2.5D研究推导物理动机的参数化方案。

Abstract: Turbulent accretion disks threaded by a large-scale vertical field near equipartition can drive tenuous and fast self-confined jets. Self-similar solutions of these jet-emitting disks (JEDs) have been known for a long time and provide the distributions of all physical quantities, from the turbulent disk to the asymptotic regime of ideal magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) jets. However, a thorough comparison with time-dependent numerical simulations has never been achieved, mostly because mass-loss rates found in simulations were always larger than those found analytically. This tension may have cast doubt on the analytical approach, the numerical one, or both. Our goal is to bridge the gap between these two complementary approaches and settle this long-standing issue. We performed 2.5D (axisymmetric) simulations of resistive and viscous accretion disks described by the same parameter sets as analytical JED solutions. The results demonstrate an almost perfect agreement between the numerical and analytical solutions, thereby resolving the previously observed tension. The simulations also confirm that JEDs behave as dynamical attractors: starting from different initial conditions, the system consistently converges toward the expected steady-state solution. This work demonstrates that self-similar solutions provide valuable insights into accretion-ejection physics. However, as 2.5D numerical simulations which rely on alpha-prescriptions, they strongly depend on the assumptions made for turbulent terms. In contrast, 3D simulations capture the turbulence, but become prohibitively expensive when modeling large-scale astrophysical systems. We advocate for the use of global 3D simulations to investigate turbulence and to derive physically motivated prescriptions for use in 2.5D studies.

</details>


### [138] [The cold molecular gas regulates the activity of active galactic nuclei in massive galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27121)
*Yongyun Chen,Qiusheng Gu,Luis. C Ho,Junhui Fan,Feng Yuan,Tao Wang,Zhifu Chen,Dingrong Xiong,Xiaoling Yu,Xiaotong Guo,Nan Ding*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 研究发现分子气体质量与活动星系核（AGN）的射电和[OIII]光度相关性最强，表明分子气体可能是驱动AGN活动的主要因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGN反馈在星系演化中起关键作用，但调控AGN反馈的物理量仍不清楚。观测显示AGN周围的分子气体可能是AGN活动的燃料源，因此需要研究AGN活动与分子气体性质之间的关系。

Method: 分析一个大型的近邻AGN样本，这些AGN具有可用的分子气体质量、射电光度和[OIII]光度测量数据。研究这些参数之间的相关性，并控制变量分析。

Result: 射电光度和[OIII]光度与分子气体质量的相关性比与黑洞质量、恒星质量、核球质量等其他物理参数的相关性更强。控制分子气体质量的影响后，这些光度与其他关键物理参数的关系显著减弱或消失。

Conclusion: 在所考虑的所有性质中，分子气体质量与射电和[OIII]光度的相关性最紧密，因此分子气体可能是驱动核活动的最重要因素。

Abstract: The physical quantities that directly regulate AGN feedback in massive galaxies remain poorly understood. Observations of molecular gas surrounding AGNs suggest that this gas serves as a fuel source for AGN activity. Accordingly, we study the relationship between AGN activity and molecular gas properties. In this study, we analyze a large sample of nearby AGNs with available measurements of molecular gas mass, radio luminosity, and \oiii~luminosity. Our results show that radio luminosity and \oiii~luminosity exhibit stronger correlations with molecular gas mass than with other physical parameters such as black hole mass, stellar mass, and bulge mass. Moreover, when controlling for the correlations between radio luminosity, \oiii~luminosity, and molecular gas mass, the relationships between these luminosities and other key physical parameters become significantly weaker or disappear entirely. This suggests that, of all the properties we have considered, it is the molecular gas mass that is most tightly correlated with radio and \oiii~luminosity, and may thus be the most important driver of nuclear activity.

</details>


### [139] [Irregular Repeating Tidal Disruption Events due to Diffusive Tides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27132)
*Shu Yan Lau,Ethan McKeever,Hang Yu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的重复性部分潮汐瓦解事件机制——扩散潮汐rpTDEs，即当双星系统的近心点距离为潮汐半径数倍时，通过多轨道累积的扩散过程触发非线性不稳定性，导致质量抛射和爆发，产生具有大时间间隔变化的重复性事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统rpTDE模型要求近心点距离接近或小于潮汐半径，但观测到的一些重复性事件（如J0456-20）具有大的复发时间变化，需要新的物理机制来解释这种随机性复发模式。

Method: 提出扩散潮汐机制：当白矮星或主序星围绕大质量黑洞运行时，若近心点距离为潮汐半径数倍且轨道周期短于潮汐能量耗散时间尺度，通过多轨道累积的扩散过程可触发非线性不稳定性，导致质量抛射。

Result: 扩散潮汐增长可在近心点距离为潮汐半径数倍时发生，产生具有大复发时间变化的重复性部分潮汐瓦解事件，这类扩散潮汐rpTDEs可能占所有rpTDEs的显著比例。

Conclusion: 扩散潮汐机制为解释具有大复发时间变化的重复性部分潮汐瓦解事件提供了新的理论框架，扩展了传统rpTDE模型，并可能解释观测到的随机性复发模式。

Abstract: A repeating partial tidal disruption event (rpTDE) is typically modelled as a star in a bounded orbit getting disrupted by a massive black hole at each pericenter passage. For the disruption to occur, the pericenter distance should be close to or within the characteristic tidal radius, such that the tidal field can overcome the star's binding force to trigger mass loss. However, a binary with a pericenter distance several times the tidal radius can build up its tidal perturbation over multiple orbits via a diffusive process, eventually triggering a nonlinear instability that may also eject mass and power an eruption. This leads to repeated disruptions that recur stochastically. In this letter, we propose that such a mechanism can produce a subclass of rpTDEs with large variations in the recurrence time (e.g., J0456-20), which we dub ``diffusive-tide rpTDEs''. We show that diffusive tidal growth can occur for a white dwarf or main-sequence star orbiting a massive black hole when the pericenter distance is a few times the tidal radius, provided the orbital period is shorter than the tidal energy dissipation timescale. These diffusive-tide rpTDEs may account for a significant fraction of all rpTDEs.

</details>


### [140] [Systematic Study of Forward and Reverse Shock Afterglow Emission from Two-Component Jets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27225)
*Olzhas Mukazhanov,Ernazar Abdikamalov,Paz Beniamini*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文系统分析了双成分喷流在伽马暴余辉中的特征，通过数值模拟揭示了喷流能量分布、相对论聚束效应和反向激波贡献对观测特征的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然双成分喷流模型常被用来解释伽马暴余辉中的复杂特征（如晚期再增亮和色散断点），但现有研究多针对单个事件进行拟合，缺乏对参数空间的系统性探索，特别是反向激波贡献的研究不足。

Method: 使用VegasAfterglow代码进行数值模拟，系统分析双成分喷流的观测特征，重点关注喷流能量分布、相对论聚束效应和反向激波贡献等参数空间。

Result: 1. 前向激波产生可观测再增亮需要翼部携带显著更多能量，而反向激波中能量可比；2. 高速翼部初始因相对论聚束对轴上观测者不可见，减速后去聚束产生陡峭上升（斜率约4.5）；3. 高速翼部会掩盖核心喷流断点，只显示单一晚期断点；4. 慢速翼部不掩盖核心，可观测两个喷流断点；5. 反向激波仅在强磁化和长引擎持续时间条件下有显著贡献。

Conclusion: 双成分喷流的观测特征高度依赖于参数选择：能量分布、相对论聚束效应和反向激波条件共同决定了余辉光变曲线的复杂行为，为解释观测到的再增亮和断点特征提供了系统框架。

Abstract: Two-component jets are frequently invoked to explain complex features in gamma-ray burst (GRB) afterglows, such as late-time rebrightening and chromatic breaks. While many studies fit these models to individual events, a systematic exploration mapping the broader parameter space, particularly the reverse shock contribution, is currently lacking. To address this, we present a comprehensive systematic analysis of two-component jet signatures using numerical modeling with the VegasAfterglow code. Our modeling shows that observable rebrightenings in the forward shock require the wing to carry substantially more energy, while for the reverse shock the energies can be comparable. Because the two components can occupy different spectral regimes, spectral breaks may arise when the wing emission overtakes the core. When the wing's initial velocity is high, relativistic beaming can render its emission invisible to the on-axis observer. As the flow decelerates, the resulting debeaming produces a steeper rise in the observed emission, reaching temporal slopes as steep as about $4.5$ and peaking shortly after the core jet break. In this case, the wing masks the core's break, leaving only a single late-time break. Slower wings that are not initially beamed away do not obscure the core, allowing the observer to see two distinct jet breaks. At late times, the decaying post-jet-break slopes are unaffected and limited to temporal slopes of about $-p$. Additionally, the forward shock dominates the emission across most of the parameter space, while the reverse shock contributes noticeably only under conditions of high magnetization and long engine durations.

</details>


### [141] [A Tale of Two Jets: Double Relativistic Outflows from Close Binary GRB Progenitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27660)
*He Gao*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 论文提出双喷流模型解释长伽马暴，认为部分长伽马暴可能源于含致密伴星的双星系统，主星超新星抛射物被伴星吸积后产生延迟的第二喷流，产生多波段观测特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统坍缩星模型难以解释长伽马暴所需的核心快速旋转，而双星相互作用通过潮汐自旋加速提供了自然解决方案。近期种群合成研究表明，相当比例的长伽马暴可能源自含致密伴星的密近双星系统。

Method: 建立双喷流系统的综合模型，分析第二喷流的动力学及其与第一喷流的相互作用。考虑洛伦兹因子比、对齐角度和时间延迟等关键参数，研究不同配置下的观测特征。

Result: 发现对齐喷流存在两种机制：快速第二喷流产生具有不同光谱/偏振演化的多重伽马射线触发；慢速第二喷流表现为X射线耀斑和能量注入导致的余辉平台。未对齐喷流则产生从正常伽马暴到晚射电结构，或快速X射线暂现源伴随离轴再增亮等信号。

Conclusion: 双喷流模型的预测特征与现有伽马暴数据存在对应关系。SKA的高分辨率射电干涉、eXTP的时间分辨偏振测量、爱因斯坦探针和SVOM的多波段巡天将验证这些预测，为密近大质量双星作为伽马暴和引力波源前身星的演化提供约束。

Abstract: Gravitational wave astronomy has revealed that close binaries with compact companions are widespread. Long GRBs (LGRBs) from massive star collapse face persistent challenges in achieving the rapid core rotation required by the collapsar model. Binary interaction via tidal spin-up offers a natural solution; recent population synthesis studies suggest a substantial fraction of LGRBs may originate from close binaries with a compact companion. In this scenario, supernova ejecta from the primary can be accreted by the companion, potentially launching a second relativistic jet after a delay set by the binary separation. We develop a comprehensive model for these double-jet systems, analyzing the dynamics of the second jet and its interaction with the first. The resulting observational signatures depend critically on the Lorentz factor ratio, the alignment angle, and the time delay. For aligned jets, two regimes arise: a fast second jet producing multiple gamma-ray triggers with distinct spectral/polarization evolution, and a slow second jet where its emission appears as an X-ray flare followed by an afterglow plateau from energy injection. For misaligned jets, the observed signal ranges from normal GRBs with late-time radio structures to fast X-ray transients followed by off-axis rebrightening. These features have observational parallels in existing GRB data. High-resolution radio interferometry with SKA, time-resolved polarimetry with eXTP, and multi-wavelength surveys with Einstein Probe and SVOM will test these predictions, providing constraints on the evolution of close massive binaries as progenitors of GRBs and gravitational wave sources.

</details>


### [142] [Toward More Realistic Machine-Learning Inference of the Dense-Matter Equation of State from Supernova Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27680)
*Almat Akhmetali,Y. Sultan Abylkairov,Marat Zaidyn,Aknur Sakan,Alisher Zhunuskanov,Nurzhan Ussipov,José Antonio Font,Alejandro Torres-Forné,Ernazar Abdikamalov*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究通过放松三个关键假设（包含真实探测器噪声、扩展前身星模型范围、引入核心反弹时间不确定性），发现这些更现实的条件不会显著降低核坍缩超新星引力波信号的核物质状态方程分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于快速旋转恒星引力波信号的机器学习研究在状态方程分类方面取得了有希望的准确性，但这些分析依赖于多个简化假设。本研究旨在放松这些假设，将更现实的条件纳入核坍缩超新星引力波推断中。

Method: 研究放松了三个关键假设：1) 包含真实探测器噪声；2) 将分析从单一前身星模型扩展到四个模型（12-40太阳质量），每个质量考虑从慢到快的多种旋转配置；3) 引入高达20毫秒的核心反弹时间不确定性，而非假设精确已知。使用更大的数据集进行训练和分类。

Result: 研究发现这些更现实的条件都不会显著降低状态方程分类性能。相反，与多个前身星模型和噪声实现相关的更大数据集改善了训练和分类准确性。

Conclusion: 这项研究是在核坍缩超新星引力波推断中逐步纳入更现实条件的重要一步，表明即使在更复杂的现实条件下，机器学习方法仍能有效进行状态方程分类。

Abstract: Gravitational waves from core-collapse supernovae offer a unique probe of the equation of state (EOS) of dense nuclear matter. For rapidly rotating stars, previous machine-learning studies demonstrated promising EOS classification accuracy. However, these analyses relied on several simplifying assumptions. In this work, we relax three key assumptions. First, we include real detector noise. Second, we expand the analysis from a single progenitor model to four models spanning 12 to 40 solar masses, and for each mass we consider multiple rotational configurations, from slow to rapid. Third, we introduce uncertainty in the core bounce time of up to 20 ms, rather than assuming it is known precisely. We find that none of these effects significantly degrades EOS classification performance. Instead, the larger dataset associated with multiple progenitor models and noise realizations improves training and classification accuracy. This study is a step in a broader effort to progressively incorporate more realistic conditions into gravitational-wave inference for core-collapse supernovae.

</details>


### [143] [Self-Consistent Modelling of Neutrino Production in Turbulent Black Hole Coronae](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27749)
*Le Bihan Sébastien,Lemoine Martin,Rieger Frank*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: Turb-AM3：用于模拟致密天体环境中湍流驱动粒子加速的混合数值代码，成功解释了NGC 1068黑洞冕区的IceCube中微子信号


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁化湍流等离子体中的随机粒子加速是解释致密天体环境多信使信号的关键机制，但自洽建模面临挑战，需要同时处理多个非线性动力学过程

Method: 开发混合数值代码Turb-AM3，将先进的时变轻子-强子辐射求解器AM3与随机加速模块耦合，包含湍流加速最新理论进展并考虑加速粒子对湍流的动态阻尼

Result: 使用该代码对NGC 1068黑洞冕区质子加速进行自洽时变建模，发现标准物理参数能很好再现IceCube中微子信号，同一模板模型也能满意解释其他活动星系核的观测

Conclusion: Turb-AM3框架为模拟多种致密天体环境的多信使辐射提供了强大工具，参数空间探索可预测TeV中微子谱的详细模板形状，有助于未来观测约束湍流AGN冕区特性

Abstract: Stochastic particle acceleration in magnetized turbulent plasmas has emerged as a key mechanism to explain multi-messenger signals from compact astrophysical environments. Self-consistent modelling remains challenging because it requires to treat simultaneously several non-linear kinetic processes, especially turbulence-driven acceleration and its feedback on the turbulent cascade, as well as the radiative and hadronic losses, including the reprocessing of electromagnetic radiation in radiatively dense environments. The present paper introduces the hybrid numerical code Turb-AM3 designed to this effect. This hybrid numerical code couples the state-of-the-art time-dependent lepto-hadronic radiative solver AM3 with a stochastic acceleration module that incorporates recent theoretical advances in turbulent acceleration and accounts for the dynamical damping of turbulence by accelerated particles. In a second part, we use this code to provide self-consistent time-dependent models of proton acceleration in the turbulent black hole corona of NGC~1068. We find that the IceCube neutrino signal is well reproduced for a standard set of physical parameters describing the black hole corona. The same template model accounts in a satisfactory way for IceCube observations of other active galactic nuclei. Furthermore, our exploration of parameter space allows us to predict detailed template spectral shapes for the TeV neutrino spectrum, which in turn help understand how future neutrino observations can constrain the properties of turbulent AGN coronae and the underlying acceleration mechanism. This Turb-AM3 framework provides a powerful tool to model multi-messenger emission in a broad variety of compact astrophysical environments.

</details>


### [144] [The counterjet dominates the production of PeV photons from Cyg X-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27805)
*Andrzej A. Zdziarski,Anton Dmytriiev,Karri I. I. Koljonen*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了Cyg X-3系统中PeV光子的轨道调制产生机制：氦核在喷流强磁场区加速后，移动到弱磁场区扩散出喷流，与恒星光子和恒星风发生强子碰撞产生π介子，最终衰变为PeV光子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHAASO合作组最近在Cyg X-3中发现了轨道调制的PeV光子，需要解释其产生机制以及为何其相位分布与GeV光子相反。

Method: 通过分析氦核在喷流中的加速过程，考虑其在强磁场区加速后移动到弱磁场区扩散出喷流，然后与恒星光子和Wolf-Rayet伴星的恒星风发生强子碰撞产生π介子，最终衰变为PeV光子。同时考虑了光学深度和柱密度的影响。

Result: 发现PeV光子主要由反向喷流中的相对论性强子产生，因为其朝向观测者的柱密度比正向喷流更长。这解释了PeV光子通量峰值位于上合相位的相反侧，而GeV光子由正向喷流中的相对论性电子与恒星光子在光学厚区域碰撞产生。

Conclusion: Cyg X-3中PeV光子的轨道调制机制与GeV光子不同：PeV光子来自反向喷流的强子过程，GeV光子来自正向喷流的轻子过程，两者相位分布相反是由不同的产生机制和观测几何共同决定的。

Abstract: We study the physical mechanisms underlying the production of orbitally-modulated PeV photons from Cyg X-3, recently discovered by the LHAASO collaboration. Our key findings are as follows. Helium nuclei are accelerated in a compact and strongly magnetized region within the jet, but they then quickly advect downstream to regions with a weaker field, allowing them to diffuse out of the jet, where they produce pions in hadronic collisions with both the stellar photons and the stellar wind of the Wolf-Rayet donor. The optical depths across the binary are $\lesssim$1 for both types of interactions, implying that their rates are proportional to the column densities along the particle paths. Given the low viewing angle of Cyg X-3 ($i\approx26^\circ$--$28^\circ$), most of the observed photons are produced by the relativistic hadrons accelerated in the counterjet (for which the column densities toward the observer are much longer than for the jet). This also explains the peak of the phase-folded PeV photon flux to be on the opposite side of the superior conjunction than that for the (also orbitally-modulated) GeV photons, which are produced by collisions of relativistic electrons with stellar photons in the optically thick regime. This then implies that the GeV emission is produced in the approaching jet.

</details>


### [145] [Similar ratios of rise timescale to decline timescale of optical light curves in common tidal disruption events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27897)
*Zhang XueGuang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文提出用光变曲线上升与下降时标比值R_{1/2,rd}作为区分潮汐瓦解事件与其他瞬变事件的新分类参数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潮汐瓦解事件具有相似的物理过程，需要找到能够区分TDE与其他瞬变事件的关键参数。目前缺乏有效的分类参数来区分具有不同物理过程的瞬变事件。

Method: 基于34个光学TDE报告的上升时标t_{1/2,r}（半峰到峰值）和下降时标t_{1/2,d}（峰值到半峰），计算时标比值R_{1/2,rd}。对异常值AT2020wey使用更灵活的模型函数重新确定参数，并检验AT2019avd、PS1-10adi等独特光学瞬变事件的R_{1/2,rd}值。

Result: 34个光学TDE中，除AT2020wey外平均R_{1/2,rd}≈0.6。AT2020wey初始值为2.7（异常值），但经模型修正后降至0.9。AT2019avd的第二次爆发R_{1/2,rd}≈11，明显不同于其他TDE，而PS1-10adi、SDSS J0946+3512等事件的R_{1/2,rd}值与TDE相似。

Conclusion: R_{1/2,rd}≈0.6可能成为光学TDE的潜在分类参数，能够有效区分TDE与其他瞬变事件。未来需要更大样本验证该参数作为分类标准的可靠性。

Abstract: Totally similar physical process in tidal disruption events (TDEs) basically indicates that there should be potential parameter to distinguish variability properties of TDEs from the other transient events having different physical processes. Here, we try to report such a parameter, the timescale ratio $R_{2/1,rd}$ of rise timescale $t_{1/2,r}$ (from half-max to maximum) to decline timescale $t_{1/2,d}$ (from maximum to half-max), especially based on the 34 optical TDEs with reported $t_{1/2,r}$ and $t_{1/2,d}$. Among the 34 optical TDEs, AT2020wey is an outlier with $R_{2/1,rd}\sim2.7$ which is 4.5 times larger than the mean value 0.6 of the other optical TDEs. However, after considering similar but more flexible model functions, the re-determined $R_{2/1,rd}$ is $\sim$0.9 in AT2020wey, totally similar as the values of the other optical TDEs. Therefore, the parameter $R_{1/2,rd}\sim0.6$ could be a potential classification parameter for optical TDEs. Furthermore, $R_{1/2,rd}$ have been checked in the unique optical transients of AT2019avd, PS1-10adi, SDSS J0946+3512 and J2334+1457. We can find that the second flare with $R_{1/2,rd}\sim11$ in AT2019avd should be very different from the other optical TDEs, but PS1-10adi, SDSS J0946+3512, J2334+1457 and the first flare in AT2019avd should be similar as the other optical TDEs. In the near future, properties of $R_{1/2,rd}$ through large sample of optical transients could provide further clues to support whether $R_{1/2,rd}$ could be a better classification parameter to distinguish TDEs and the other transient events.

</details>


### [146] [The Environmental Effects on Inspiraling Binary Black Hole Systems in the Centers of the LMC and M31](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27951)
*Meng Xu,Zhijin Li,Xiao Guo,Yun-Long Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 研究星系中心双黑洞系统在暗物质尖峰和吸积盘环境中的动力学演化与引力波信号特征，预测未来脉冲星计时阵列可探测这些环境效应


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系中心双黑洞系统演化于复杂的暗物质晕和重子吸积盘环境中，这些环境会显著改变双星轨道动力学和引力波辐射，需要系统研究这些环境效应及其可探测性

Method: 构建综合分析框架，联合考虑引力波辐射反应、暗物质尖峰效应（包括动力学摩擦和吸积，基于NFW剖面）和吸积盘扰动，追踪双星半正焦弦和轨道偏心率的长期演化

Result: 暗物质尖峰与吸积盘共存显著加速了旋进过程；特定参数空间下系统可达到稳健探测阈值（SNR≥8）；波形失配可达~0.7（LMC场景20年观测），表明环境效应引起的相位偏差与真空模板高度可区分

Conclusion: 未来引力波探测可用于探测复杂星系环境，暗物质尖峰和吸积盘的共同作用对双黑洞演化产生显著影响，这些环境效应可通过脉冲星计时阵列观测到

Abstract: Binary black hole (BBH) systems residing in the centers of galaxies evolve within complex astrophysical environments. These environments, comprising dark matter (DM) halos and baryonic accretion disks, can significantly alter the orbital dynamics of the binaries and their resulting gravitational wave (GW) emission. In this study, we investigate the dynamical evolution and GW waveforms of BBH systems embedded in the centers of the Large Magellanic Cloud (LMC) and the Andromeda Galaxy (M31). We construct a comprehensive analytical framework that jointly incorporates GW radiation reaction, DM spike effects (including dynamical friction and accretion, derived from the Navarro-Frenk-White profile), and accretion disk perturbations. Using this framework, we track the long-term evolution of the binary's semi-latus rectum $p$ and orbital eccentricity $e$. Our simulations reveal that the coexistence of a DM spike and an accretion disk significantly accelerates the inspiral process compared to pure DM or vacuum scenarios. Crucially, to assess the observability of these environmental effects, we calculate the Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR) and waveform Mismatch for future Pulsar Timing Arrays (PTAs). Our analysis demonstrates that these systems can achieve robust detectability thresholds ($\text{SNR} \ge 8$) within specific parameter spaces. Furthermore, the substantial Mismatch (reaching $\sim 0.7$ over a 20-year observation in the LMC scenario) indicates that the phase deviations induced by these environmental effects are highly distinguishable from vacuum templates. These findings predict the prospect of using future GW detections to probe complex galactic environments.

</details>


### [147] [Probing burst-disc interaction and disc reflection in SAX J1808.4$-$3658 with NICER, XMM-Newton, and NuSTAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28189)
*Manoj Mandal,Sachindra Naik,Birendra Chhotaray*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用XMM-Newton和NICER对毫秒X射线脉冲星SAX J1808.4-3658的热核暴进行综合研究，通过盘反射模型分析揭示了光球半径膨胀、1 keV发射线等特征，并估算了中子星磁场强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究毫秒X射线脉冲星SAX J1808.4-3658的热核暴与吸积盘的相互作用，通过盘反射模型方法深入了解中子星光球半径膨胀、辐射再处理过程以及系统物理参数。

Method: 采用自洽且物理驱动的盘反射建模方法分析NICER观测的强烈暴发；使用可变持续辐射方法和盘反射模型分析XMM-Newton EPIC-PN观测的热核暴；利用NuSTAR观测通过盘反射特征研究持续辐射。

Result: 发现光球半径膨胀证据，最大膨胀至14.8±0.7 km，对应黑体温度降至1.9 keV；观测到1 keV发射线，可能来自Ne或Fe L带跃迁；该发射线通量与反射成分通量强相关；NuSTAR观测得到内盘半径14_{-5.9}^{+9.7} R_g，倾角38°-60°；估算中子星极区磁场强度约3.7×10^8 G。

Conclusion: 盘反射模型为研究热核暴与吸积盘相互作用提供了有效工具，揭示了光球半径膨胀、辐射再处理等物理过程，并成功估算了中子星系统的重要物理参数。

Abstract: We performed a comprehensive study of thermonuclear bursts from the millisecond X-ray pulsar SAX J1808.4$-$3658 with XMM-Newton and NICER. We report the results from the analysis of an intense burst with NICER using a self-consistent and physically motivated disc reflection modeling approach and investigate the burst-disc interaction. The dynamic evolution of the spectral parameters suggested evidence of photospheric radius expansion (PRE) of the neutron star using the disc reflection modeling approach, which indicates a maximum expansion of the photosphere up to 14.8$\pm$0.7 km. The corresponding blackbody temperature drops to a minimum of 1.9 keV. In addition, an emission line at 1 keV is observed, possibly originating from the Ne or Fe L-band transition as a result of the reprocessing of burst photons by cold gas in the accretion disc. The 1 keV emission line flux is found to be strongly correlated with the flux of the reflection component. We also investigated a thermonuclear burst observed with XMM-Newton EPIC-PN from SAX J1808.4$-$3658 using the variable persistent emission method and the disc reflection modeling approach. The X-ray reflection feature is also investigated in persistent emission using a NuSTAR observation. The best-fitting results provide an inner disc radius of $14_{-5.9}^{+9.7}$ $R_g$ and an inclination of $ 38^\circ-60^\circ$ during the NuSTAR observation. The magnetic field is estimated to be $\simeq$3.7 $\times$10$^8$ G at the poles of the neutron star.

</details>


### [148] [Search for TeV emission from spider millisecond pulsars with HAWC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28528)
*R. Alfaro,E. Anita-Rangel,M. Araya,J. C. Arteaga-Velázquez,D. Avila Rojas,H. A. Ayala Solares,R. Babu,P. Bangale,E. Belmont-Moreno,A. Bernal,F. Calore,T. Capistrán,A. Carramiñana,S. Casanova,A. L. Colmenero-Cesar,U. Cotti,J. Cotzomi,S. Coutiño de León,E. De la Fuente,P. Desiati,N. Di Lalla,R. Diaz Hernandez,M. A. DuVernois,J. C. Díaz-Vélez,K. Engel,T. Ergin,C. Espinoza,K. Fang,N. Fraija,S. Herzog,D. Huang,F. Hueyotl-Zahuantitla,P. Hüntemeyer,A. Iriarte,S. Kaufmann,D. Kieda,K. Leavitt,W. H. Lee,H. León Vargas,A. L. Longinotti,G. Luis-Raya,K. Malone,S. Manconi,O. Martinez,J. Martínez-Castro,J. A. Matthews,P. Miranda-Romagnoli,J. A. Morales-Soto,M. Mostafá,M. Najafi,L. Nellen,R. Noriega-Papaqui,N. Omodei,M. Osorio-Archila,E. Ponce,Y. Pérez Araujo,C. D. Rho,A. Rodriguez Parra,D. Rosa-González,M. Roth,H. Salazar,A. Sandoval,M. Schneider,J. Serna-Franco,M. Shin,Y. Son,R. W. Springer,O. Tibolla,K. Tollefson,I. Torres,F. Ureña-Mena,E. Varela,X. Wang,Z. Wang,H. Wu,S. Yu,X. Zhang,H. Zhou,C. de León*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 使用HAWC望远镜数据研究蜘蛛毫秒脉冲星在甚高能波段（0.1-100 TeV）的辐射，通过个体源上限和叠加分析约束其TeV发射特性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜘蛛毫秒脉冲星（红背和黑寡妇系统）可能通过双星内部激波加速电子/正电子对，产生甚高能光子。研究这些系统的TeV辐射特性有助于理解其粒子加速机制和对银河系弥漫辐射的贡献。

Method: 使用HAWC望远镜Pass 5数据（2565天观测），对蜘蛛毫秒脉冲星进行甚高能辐射搜索：1）计算个体源的流量上限；2）对红背和黑寡妇两类系统分别进行叠加分析，比较其光谱特性。

Result: 1）获得个体蜘蛛毫秒脉冲星的甚高能辐射上限；2）叠加分析显示两类系统光谱特性无显著差异；3）研究表明毫秒脉冲星不太可能是TeV及以上能量银河系弥漫辐射的主要贡献者。

Conclusion: 蜘蛛毫秒脉冲星在甚高能波段的辐射受到严格约束，它们对银河系TeV弥漫辐射的贡献有限。研究为理解这类双星系统的粒子加速机制提供了重要观测限制。

Abstract: Millisecond pulsars (MSPs) are observed to emit multi-wavelength radiation, from radio to GeV. Spider MSPs, which interact with their low-mass companion in close orbit (orbital periods $< 1$ day), may lead to strong intrabinary shocks that can further accelerate electron and positron pairs produced in the magnetosphere, possibly emitting very-high-energy (0.1--100 TeV; VHE) photons through inverse Compton scattering. Using 2565 days of HAWC Pass 5 data, we search for VHE emission from spider MSPs and present upper limits on individual sources. We also perform a stacking analysis to examine whether the two sets of spider systems, classified as redbacks and black widows depending on the companion mass, exhibit different spectral properties. Our study places constraints on TeV emission from MSPs and suggests that they are unlikely to contribute significantly to the Galactic diffuse emission at TeV and higher energies.

</details>


### [149] [Comprehensive Measurement of Spectral Evolution in a GRB Flare: High Time-Resolution Insights into the "Double-Tracking" Phenomenon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28570)
*Zheng-Hang Yu,Wen-Jun Tan,Chen-Wei Wang,Shao-Lin Xiong,Chao Zheng,Peng Zhang,Hao-Xuan Guo,Zheng-Hua An,Ce Cai,Min Gao,Ke Gong,Dong-Ya Guo,Yue Huang,Bing Li,Cheng-Kui Li,Xiao-Bo Li,Xin-Qiao Li,Jia-Cong Liu,Ya-Qing Liu,Xiao-Jing Liu,Xiang Ma,Wen-Xi Peng,Rui Qiao,Yang-Zhao Ren,Li-Ming Song,Jin Wang,Jin-Zhou Wang,Ping Wang,Yue Wang,Xiang-Yang Wen,Shuo Xiao,Sheng-Lun Xie,Wang-Chen Xue,Sheng Yang,Shu-Xu Yi,Da-Li Zhang,Fan Zhang,Zhen Zhang,Xiao-Yun Zhao,Jin-Peng Zhang,Wen-Long Zhang,Yan-Qiu Zhang,Shuang-Nan Zhang,Shi-Jie Zheng*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 首次对GRB 221009A中最亮耀斑进行高时间分辨率光谱拟合，发现耀斑整体和上升阶段α-流量、Ep-流量、Ep-α关系呈正相关幂律关系，表现出"双追踪"行为，可用磁耗散同步辐射模型解释；下降阶段相关性变平，可能是下一个耀斑开始爆发导致的混合效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然伽马射线暴(GRB)瞬时辐射的光谱演化特征已被广泛研究，但对GRB耀斑中光谱演化的详细研究仍然缺乏。本文旨在通过对GRB 221009A中最亮耀斑进行高时间分辨率光谱拟合，首次分析GRB耀斑中的光谱参数演化。

Method: 对GRB 221009A中最亮耀斑进行高时间分辨率光谱拟合，分析光谱参数α(低能谱指数)、Ep(峰值能量)和流量之间的演化关系。研究耀斑整体阶段、上升阶段和下降阶段中α-流量、Ep-流量、Ep-α的相关性，并使用幂律模型进行拟合。

Result: 发现耀斑整体阶段和上升阶段中，α-流量、Ep-流量、Ep-α关系都能很好地用简单幂律模型描述，呈现正相关，表明最亮耀斑表现出"双追踪"行为。α值未超过同步辐射"死亡线"(-2/3)，支持磁耗散同步辐射模型解释。在下降阶段，Ep-流量和Ep-α相关性明显变平，幂律指数显著下降，可能是下一个耀斑开始爆发导致两个耀斑效应叠加的结果。

Conclusion: 对最亮耀斑光谱参数关系的研究为GRB瞬时辐射和耀斑的辐射机制提供了新的见解。耀斑的光谱演化特征支持磁耗散同步辐射模型，而下降阶段相关性的变化表明可能存在多个耀斑的叠加效应，这对理解GRB中耀斑的产生机制具有重要意义。

Abstract: The spectral evolution characteristics of the prompt emission in gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) have been extensively studied, but detailed investigations of spectral evolution in a GRB flare remain lacking. In this work, we present the first analysis of spectral parameter evolution in a GRB flare through high time-resolved spectral fitting of the Brightest Flare in GRB 221009A. We find that the $α$-Flux, $E_p$-Flux, and $E_p$-$α$ relationships during both the overall phase and the rise phase of flare can be well described by simple power-law model, showing positive correlations. Therefore, we conclude that Brightest Flare exhibits "Double-tracking" behavior. Since values of $α$ do not exceed the synchrotron "death line" (-2/3), we explain this phenomenon using a magnetic dissipation synchrotron radiation model. In the decay phase of flare, the $E_p$-Flux and $E_p$-$α$ correlations become notably flatter, with their power-law indices decreasing significantly compared to those in the rise phase. This may be due to the fact that the next flare begins to erupt before the Brightest Flare has completely ended, resulting in the combined effects of both two flares. Our study of spectral parameter relations of the Brightest Flare provides new insights into the radiation mechanisms of both GRB prompt emission and flares.

</details>


### [150] [Variable ADAF disk as the origin of Changing-Look AGN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28666)
*Chun Xu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文提出变化外观AGN的转变源于内层ADAF盘的大小变化，认为ADAF尺寸变化导致吸积率和外流变化，从而解释观测现象，建议AGN统一模型应同时考虑取向和ADAF尺寸


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释变化外观活动星系核（changing-look AGN）的观测现象，特别是状态变化和观测变化AGN之间的关联，为AGN统一模型提供更全面的理论框架

Method: 提出理论模型：AGN吸积盘由外层薄盘和内层厚ADAF组成，ADAF尺寸具有内在不稳定性并随时间演化；ADAF尺寸变化伴随吸积率和外流量的变化，后者控制视线吸收

Result: 从可变ADAF视角统一解释变化状态和变化观测AGN，认为它们是同一现象的两个方面；提出千兆赫峰值和致密陡谱射电源可能是中小尺度ADAF的表现形式

Conclusion: AGN统一模型应同时纳入取向和ADAF尺寸作为关键参数，为理解AGN多样性和变化现象提供更完整的理论框架

Abstract: We propose that changing-look AGN transitions arise from size variability of the inner ADAF disk. The AGN accretion disk comprises an outer thin disk and an inner thick ADAF component whose size is intrinsically unstable and evolves with time. The size variations of ADAF are accompanied by changes in accretion rate and outflow quantity, with the latter governing line of-sight absorption. From this variable ADAF perspective, changing-state and changing-observation AGN represent two faces of the same coin. We further discuss gigahertz-peaked and compact steep-spectrum radio sources as potential manifestations of small-to-intermediate scale ADAFs. Finally, we propose that AGN unification models should incorporate both orientation and ADAFsize as key parameters.

</details>


### [151] [An Intertwined Short and Long GRB with 4-minute Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28699)
*Liang Li,Yu Wang,Bing Zhang,Ye Li,Shu-Rui Zhang,Jochen Greiner,Zhi-Ping Jin,Jin-Jun Geng,Hou-Jun Lv,Asaf Peer,Maria Dainotti,Tong Liu,Yi-Zhong Fan,Yong-Feng Huang,Zi-Gao Dai,Melin Kole,Wei-Hua Lei,Ye-Fei Yuan,Shuang-Nan Zhang,Felix Ryde,She-Sheng Xue,Rong-Gen Cai*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: GRB 160425A是一个特殊的伽马射线暴，包含一个短而尖锐的爆发(G₁)和一个长而宽的爆发(G₂)，两者仅相隔4分钟。所有标准观测诊断都一致地将G₁归类为短暴(合并起源)，G₂归类为长暴(塌缩星起源)，这挑战了现有的GRB分类框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上伽马射线暴(GRB)被分为长暴(>2秒，与大质量恒星核心塌缩相关)和短暴(<2秒，与致密双星合并相关)。两类具有明显不同的观测特征。本文旨在报告一个特殊的GRB事件，它同时表现出两种类型的特征，从而挑战现有的分类框架和起源模型。

Method: 研究分析了GRB 160425A的观测数据，该暴包含两个部分：G₁(短而尖锐)和G₂(长而宽)，间隔4分钟。使用了多种标准诊断方法进行分析：脉冲形态、持续时间、硬度比、最小变时标、光谱特性、光谱延迟，以及经验关系(如Amati关系和Norris关系)。

Result: 所有诊断结果一致显示：G₁具有短暴(合并起源)的特征，而G₂具有长暴(塌缩星起源)的特征。具体来说，G₁的持续时间短、硬度高、变时标短、光谱延迟小，符合短暴特征；G₂则相反，符合长暴特征。

Conclusion: GRB 160425A在同一事件中同时表现出合并起源和塌缩星起源的特征，这挑战了现有的GRB分类方案和起源模型。需要重新评估GRB的分类框架和可能的物理起源机制。

Abstract: Gamma-ray bursts (GRBs), among the most energetic transients in the Universe, are traditionally classified into long-duration GRBs (lasting more than two seconds) and short-duration GRBs (lasting less than two seconds)\cite{Kouveliotou1993}. Long-duration GRBs are typically associated with the core collapse of massive stars (Type II), whereas short-duration GRBs originate from the merger of compact binary systems (Type I)\cite{Woosley2006, Zhang2006Natur, Zhang2009b, Berger2014}. Owing to their distinct physical origins, the two classes exhibit markedly different observational properties, which serve as key diagnostic criteria for GRB classification\cite{Norris2000, Zhang2009b, Lv2010, Lv2014, Qin2013, Li2016, Minaev2020}. Here we report a peculiar gamma-ray burst, GRB 160425A, comprising a short-sharp duration burst ($G_1$) followed by a long-broad duration burst ($G_2$), separated by only four minutes. Strikingly, nearly all standard prompt-emission observational diagnostics, including pulse morphology\cite{Norris2005}, duration\cite{Kouveliotou1993}, hardness ratio \cite{Horvath2010, Goldstein2017}, minimum variability timescale\cite{Golkhou2014, Golkhou2015}, spectral properties \cite{Dezalay1992}, spectral lag\cite{Norris2000,Norris2006, Yi2006, Bernardini2015}, and established empirical correlations (the Amati and Norris relations \cite{Amati2002, Norris2000}), consistently categorize $G_1$ as a short-like (Type-I, merger-origin) GRB and $G_2$ as a long-like (Type-II, collapsar-origin) GRB. The coexistence of merger and collapsar signatures within a single event challenges existing progenitor frameworks, calling for a fundamental re-evaluation of GRB classification schemes and progenitor scenarios.

</details>


### [152] [Can a single supernova remnant account for the gamma-ray emission of G106.3+2.7?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28701)
*P. Cristofari,G. Emery,T. Lubrano di Vavaria,H. Costantini,F. Cassol,M. -S. Carrasco,B. Le Nagat Neher*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究探讨SNR G106.3+2.7作为潜在PeVatron的物理机制，通过分析其在团块介质中演化的激波动力学和粒子加速过程，解释TeV伽马射线观测，但发现宽带伽马射线谱与标准超新星遗迹不符，更可能与脉冲星相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNR G106.3+2.7是一个复杂的TeV伽马射线源，其发射机制尚不清楚。该源因其伽马射线谱在高达~600 TeV范围内未显示显著抑制而被认为是潜在的PeVatron（PeV粒子加速器），这引发了对其物理起源的广泛讨论。研究旨在检验该源是否由在团块或云状环境中演化的超新星遗迹驱动，并解释观测到的TeV伽马射线发射特征。

Method: 采用基于物理的简单解析模型，分析激波在团块介质中的动力学演化过程以及加速粒子含量。模型考虑激波在非均匀介质中的传播特性，并将模拟结果与现有的伽马射线观测数据（特别是"头部"和"尾部"两个区域的TeV发射）进行对比验证。

Result: 研究发现，单个活跃的超新星遗迹可以解释当前观测到的头部和尾部区域的TeV伽马射线发射。在团块介质中膨胀或逃逸到尾部致密区域会导致谱的自然硬化。然而，从GeV到≳100 TeV的宽带伽马射线发射难以与标准超新星遗迹（无论是热核型还是核心坍缩型）相协调，反而更倾向于与脉冲星相关联。

Conclusion: 虽然SNR G106.3+2.7的TeV伽马射线发射可以用在团块环境中演化的超新星遗迹模型解释，但其宽带伽马射线谱特征与标准超新星遗迹模型存在矛盾。观测数据更支持该源的伽马射线发射主要与脉冲星相关的物理机制，而非传统的超新星遗迹加速过程。这一结论对理解该源作为PeVatron候选体的性质具有重要意义。

Abstract: SNR G106.3+2.7 is a complex TeV emitting source whose emission is still poorly understood. It has especially been at the center of numerous discussions on its potential for being a supernova remnant (SNR) PeVatron, since its gamma-ray spectra seems not to exhibit any significant suppression in the multi--TeV range, up to $\sim 600$ TeV, thereby indicating the presence of $\sim$ PeV particles.
  We study the hypothesis in which a SNR evolving in a clumpy or cloudy environment is powering the TeV gamma-ray emission, detected mainly from two regions, the "head" and the "tail". We discuss the implications of such an hypothesis. We rely on a simple physically motivated analytical modeling of the shock dynamics and of the content of accelerated particles and confront it to available gamma-ray observations. We find that the current observed TeV gamma-ray emission in the head and tail regions can be accounted for by an active single SNR, with a natural hardening of the spectrum due to the expansion in a clumpy medium or escaping to a dense region in the tail. However, in all scenarios, the broadband gamma-ray emission from the GeV range to the $\gtrsim 100$ TeV range is difficult to reconcile with a standard SNR - whether originating from a thermonuclear or a core-collapse supernova - and instead points toward an association with the pulsar.

</details>


### [153] [The average X-ray spectrum of the volume-complete M-, F-, G-, and K-type star sample within 10 pc of the Sun](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28751)
*Xueying Zheng,Gabriele Ponti,Nicola Locatelli,Beate Stelzer,Enza Magaudda,Konrad Dennerl,Michael Freyberg,Jeremy Sanders,Marilena Caramazza,Manami Sasaki,Andrea Merloni,Jan Robrade,Teng Liu,He-shou Zhang,Martin G. F. Mayer,Yi Zhang,Michael C. H. Yeung,Werner Becker*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用eROSITA巡天数据，通过距离归一化和光谱叠加方法，首次系统测量了10pc内M型和FGK型恒星的集体X射线性质，为理解银河系软X射线背景的恒星贡献提供了关键约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: F、G、K和M型恒星是银河系中最丰富的恒星种群，预计会对银河系的弥散X射线发射有贡献。然而，由于它们微弱的X射线光度，其内在的平均X射线光谱仍缺乏良好约束，导致它们在银河系X射线背景中的集体作用不确定。

Method: 使用eROSITA全天巡天（eRASS:4）数据，分析了距离太阳10pc内的体积完整样本，包括M0-M6型恒星和FGK型恒星。将个体光谱按曝光时间和距离归一化后叠加，生成代表性平均光谱。通过距离归一化的发射测量计算平均X射线光度。

Result: M型恒星的平均X射线光度为(2.6±0.1)×10²⁷ erg/s，FGK型恒星为(15±3)×10²⁷ erg/s（0.2-2.0 keV）。平均光谱可用三组分和两组分热模型良好拟合。拟合的温度和丰度在M型恒星亚群中保持一致，但早期M型恒星的平均光度意外低于中晚期M型恒星。

Conclusion: 该研究为邻近恒星的集体X射线性质提供了新见解，并为探索其与银河系未解析软X射线背景的联系提供了动力。结果表明恒星集体发射对银河系X射线背景有重要贡献，需要进一步研究其与未解析背景的关系。

Abstract: F, G, K and M type stars are the most abundant stellar population in the Milky Way and are expected to contribute to its diffuse X-ray emission. Yet their intrinsic average X-ray spectrum remains poorly constrained due to their faint X-ray luminosities, leaving their collective role in the X-ray background of the Milky Way uncertain. We analysed the volume-complete sample of M- (M0--M6) and FGK-type stars within 10 pc of the Sun using data from eROSITA all-sky survey aboard the Spectrum-Roentgen-Gamma (SRG) mission (eRASS:4). Individual spectra were normalized by exposure and distance and stacked to produce representative averages. The distance-normalized emission measures yield an average X-ray luminosity of $(2.6 \pm0.1)\times 10^{27}$ erg/s for M-type stars, and $(15\pm3)\times 10^{27}$ erg/s for F, G and K-type stars in 0.2--2.0 keV. The average spectra could be well described by a sum of three and two thermal models. Fitted temperatures and abundances remain consistent across M-star subgroups, while early-M stars are surprisingly on average less luminous than mid/late-M types. These results offer new insights into the collective X-ray properties of nearby stars, and provide motivation to explore the link with the unresolved soft X-ray background of the Galaxy.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Symmetry analysis and exact solutions of multi-layer quasi-geostrophic problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26959)
*Serhii D. Koval,Alex Bihlo,Roman O. Popovych*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 对多层准地转问题进行扩展对称性分析，首次正确描述了一般情况下的守恒律和哈密顿结构，计算了最大李不变代数，研究了Lie约化，得到了多种线性方程的精确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层准地转模型由任意数量的耦合正压涡度方程组成，是地球物理流体动力学中的重要模型。需要对其对称性进行全面分析，以更好地理解模型结构和获得精确解。

Method: 使用原始方法计算模型的最大李不变代数和完整点对称伪群；对李不变代数的一维和二维子代数进行分类；详尽研究余维数为1、2、3的Lie约化；分析模型垂直耦合矩阵的谱性质。

Result: 首次正确描述了一般情况下的守恒律和哈密顿结构；获得了包括Helmholtz、修正Helmholtz、Laplace、Klein-Gordon、Whittaker、Bessel和线性化Benjamin-Bona-Mahony方程在内的解耦线性系统；构建了广泛的精确解族，包括重新发现的定常和传播斜压Rossby波、相干斜压涡旋、热涡和局域偶极涡的表示。

Conclusion: 通过扩展对称性分析，深入理解了多层准地转模型的结构特性，获得了丰富的精确解，并使用三层海洋模型的真实地球物理数据验证了所得解的物理相关性。

Abstract: We carry out an extended symmetry analysis of the multi-layer quasi-geostrophic problem. This model is given by a system of an arbitrary number of coupled barotropic vorticity equations. Conservation laws and a Hamiltonian structure for the general case of the model are correctly described for the first time. Using original methods, we compute the maximal Lie invariance algebra and the complete point-symmetry pseudogroup of the model. After classifying one- and two-dimensional subalgebras of the Lie invariance algebra, we exhaustively study codimension-one, -two and -three Lie reductions. Notably, among invariant submodels of the original nonlinear model, we obtain uncoupled systems of well-known linear equations, including the Helmholtz, modified Helmholtz, Laplace, Klein-Gordon, Whittaker, Bessel and linearized Benjamin-Bona-Mahony equations. Integration of these systems significantly depends on spectral properties of the model's vertical coupling matrix, which we also revisit in detail. As a result, we construct wide families of exact solutions, including rediscovered representations of stationary and travelling baroclinic Rossby waves, coherent baroclinic eddies, hetons and localized dipolar vortices. We illustrate the physical relevance of obtained solutions using real-world geophysical data for a three-layer ocean model.

</details>


### [155] [Semiclassical shape resonances for magnetic Stark Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27196)
*Kentaro Kameoka,Naoya Yoshida*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究二维磁斯塔克哈密顿量在准经典极限下的形状共振，证明共振与参考算子离散特征值的一一对应关系，推导共振的韦尔定律和渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究磁斯塔克哈密顿量在准经典极限下的形状共振问题，旨在理解磁场和线性势扰动下量子系统的共振现象，特别是势阱附近共振的分布规律。

Method: 采用复扭曲哈密顿量方法，通过复平移在紧集外定义复扭曲哈密顿量，将共振视为该复算子的复特征值。假设磁场恒定，标量势为线性势的扰动且具有势阱，建立哈密顿量形状共振与参考算子离散特征值的一一对应。

Result: 证明了形状共振与参考算子离散特征值存在一一对应关系，由此推导出共振数量的韦尔定律，并得到了势阱底部附近共振实部的渐近行为。

Conclusion: 在准经典极限下，二维磁斯塔克哈密顿量的形状共振可以通过参考算子的离散谱来完全描述，这为理解此类系统的共振结构提供了严格的数学框架。

Abstract: We study shape resonances of two-dimensional magnetic Stark Hamiltonians in the semiclassical limit. The magnetic field is assumed to be constant and the scalar potential is a perturbation of a linear potential. Under the assumption that the scalar potential has potential wells, the existence of a one-to-one correspondence between shape resonances of the Hamiltonian and discrete eigenvalues of a certain reference operator is proved. This implies the Weyl law for the number of resonances and the asymptotic behavior of the real parts of resonances near the bottom of a potential well. Resonances are studied as complex eigenvalues of complex distorted Hamiltonians, which is defined by the complex translation outside a compact set.

</details>


### [156] [Quantum Hall States response to toroidal geometry deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27319)
*Bruno Mera,José M. Mourão,João P. Nunes,Carolina Paiva*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 应用几何量子化技术研究整数和分数量子霍尔效应对环面几何形变的响应，通过复时间哈密顿演化和广义相干态变换分析Laughlin态演化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子霍尔系统在几何形变下的响应特性，特别是环面几何变化对Laughlin态的影响，探索几何量子化方法在非平坦Kähler形变中的应用

Method: 使用复时间哈密顿演化诱导几何变化，结合广义相干态变换(gCST)追踪Laughlin态演化；考虑两类形变：平坦环面形变（使用平移生成元的二次哈密顿量）和非平坦Kähler环面形变（使用双周期哈密顿量）

Result: 平坦形变中gCST方法提供了新颖的研究视角；非平坦形变中发现了τ保持的Mabuchi测地线，在有限虚时间内达到曲率奇点；通过限制S^1不变形变，获得了到奇点为止的几何和Laughlin态演化的显式解析表达式

Conclusion: 几何量子化方法能有效研究量子霍尔系统对几何形变的响应，gCST为分析Laughlin态演化提供了新工具，非平坦Kähler形变揭示了曲率奇点现象，为理解几何变化对拓扑量子态的影响提供了理论基础

Abstract: In this paper, we apply techniques of geometric quantization to study the response of the integer and fractional quantum Hall effects to toroidal geometry deformation. The main method is that of using complex time Hamiltonian evolution to induce the geometry change and then the associated generalized coherent state transforms (gCST) to find the evolution of the Laughlin states. We consider two kinds of deformations. The first are flat toroidal deformations. Although Laughlin states for all flat toroidal geometries have been thoroughly studied before, we believe that our approach via the gCST is novel. It also serves as a testing ground to study the non-flat Kähler deformations. The Hamiltonians used in the flat deformations are quadratic in the generators of translations and therefore non periodic. The second kind of deformations involve nonflat Kähler toroidal deformations, generated by global, thus bi-periodic, Hamiltonians on the torus. The corresponding imaginary time flows are (elliptic curve modulus) $τ$-preserving Mabuchi geodesics in the space of Kähler metrics on the torus, hitting a curvature singularity in finite imaginary time. By restricting to $S^1$-invariant deformations we find explicit analytic expressions for the evolution of the toroidal geometry and of the Laughlin states all the way to the singularity.

</details>


### [157] [The Full Set of KMS-States for Abelian Kitaev Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27436)
*Danilo Polo Ojito,Emil Prodan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 证明阿贝尔Kitaev模型的顶点和面算子生成的子代数C是拟局域可观测量UHF代数A的C*-对角，利用相关Weyl群胚将A表示为群胚C*-代数，并完整分类了模型的KMS态


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究阿贝尔Kitaev模型的平衡态统计力学，特别是KMS态的分类和性质，以及它们与基态的关系

Method: 首先证明顶点和面算子生成的子代数C是UHF代数A的C*-对角，利用C*-包含C↪A的Weyl群胚将A表示为群胚C*-代数，其中动力学由群胚1-上循环c_H生成，然后使用(c_H,β)-KMS测度的概念

Result: 完整识别了模型的所有KMS态，证明了在β∈[0,∞)范围内的唯一性，并证明了当β→∞时极限存在且与模型的唯一无阻挫基态一致

Conclusion: 通过C*-对角和群胚代数的方法，成功建立了阿贝尔Kitaev模型KMS态的完整分类，揭示了平衡态与基态之间的连续过渡关系

Abstract: We first prove that the subalgebra $\mathcal{C}$ generated by the vertex and face operators of an abelian Kitaev model is a $C^\ast$-diagonal of the UHF algebra $\mathcal{A}$ of quasilocal observables. This gives us access to the Weyl groupoid $\mathcal{G}_\mathcal{C}$ associated with the $C^\ast$-inclusion $\mathcal{C} \hookrightarrow \mathcal{A}$, which supplies a valuable presentation of $\mathcal{A}$ as a groupoid $C^\ast$-algebra where the dynamics of the model are generated by a groupoid 1-cocycle $c_H$. Making appeal to the notion of $(c_H,β)$-KMS measures for this groupoid, we identify the full set of KMS states of the model and prove its uniqueness for $β\in [0,\infty)$. Furthermore, we show that its limit at $β\rightarrow \infty$ exists and coincides with the unique frustration-free ground state of the model.

</details>


### [158] [Pseudo-magnetism in a strained discrete honeycomb lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27503)
*Xuenan Li,Michael I. Weinstein*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 非均匀应变诱导伪磁场导致狄拉克波包局域化，在扶手椅方向保持平移不变性的单向变形产生类朗道能级的局域态，而锯齿方向变形则无此效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非均匀应变如何诱导伪磁场，以及这种伪磁场如何影响具有蜂窝对称性的非磁性波传播介质中的波包动力学，特别是探索在特定变形条件下能否产生类似朗道能级的局域态和高态密度。

Method: 从离散最近邻紧束缚模型出发，推导连续有效磁狄拉克哈密顿量；针对扶手椅方向保持平移不变性的单向有界梯度变形，证明存在沿扶手椅方向类平面波、横向指数局域的时谐态；通过多尺度分析获得小变形梯度下的主导结构；进行数值模拟验证。

Result: 扶手椅方向平移不变的变形诱导近似垂直恒定伪磁场（朗道规范），产生具有近乎平坦能带（朗道能级）谱的局域态，导致极高态密度；横向局域化由一维有效狄拉克哈密顿量的本征态决定；锯齿方向平移不变的变形不诱导类似局域化。

Conclusion: 非均匀应变在蜂窝介质中诱导的伪磁场能够产生类似朗道能级的局域态，但仅当变形保持扶手椅方向平移不变性时才有效，这为通过几何变形调控波传播和态密度提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Slowly varying nonuniform strains of non-magnetic wave propagating media with honeycomb symmetry induce an effective- or pseudo-magnetic field, a phenomenon observed first in graphene, and later in photonic crystals and other physical settings. Starting with a discrete nearest-neighbor tight-binding model of a non-uniformly strained honeycomb medium, we derive the continuum effective magnetic Dirac Hamiltonian governing the envelope dynamics of wave packets, which are spectrally localized near a Dirac point (conical band degeneracy) of the unperturbed honeycomb. For unidirectional deformations of bounded gradient, which preserve translation invariance along the ''armchair'' direction, we prove the existence of time-harmonic states which are plane-wave like (pseudo-periodic) along the armchair direction and exponentially localized transverse to it. We also obtain the leading order multi-scale structure of such modes for small deformation gradients. Their transverse localization are determined by the eigenstates of a one dimensional effective Dirac Hamiltonian. Our rigorous results apply to deformations which induce an approximate perpendicular constant pseudo-magnetic field (Landau gauge), and yield states with nearly flat band (Landau level) spectrum and hence very high density of states. In contrast, the analogous deformation which preserves translations in the zigzag direction induces no such localization. Corroborating numerical simulations for the different deformation types are presented.

</details>


### [159] [Homothetic Hodge$-$de Rham Theory and a Geometric Regularization of Elliptic Boundary Value Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27564)
*Fereidoun Sabetghadam*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种经典Weyl可积几何的同位扩展，通过将线性规范变换推广到以调和尺度不变形式α_d为中心的仿射同位变换，建立了扭曲外微积分和同位Hodge理论，并应用于椭圆边值问题的扩散界面表示和点源非奇异建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展经典Weyl可积几何，将线性规范变换推广到仿射同位变换，以建立更一般的几何框架。该框架旨在为椭圆边值问题提供严格的扩散界面（体积惩罚）表示，并构建点源的非奇异模型。

Method: 1. 引入以调和尺度不变形式α_d为中心的仿射同位变换扩展Weyl几何；2. 通过变量平移重新线性化仿射规范变换，获得与Witten变形结构等价的扭曲外微积分；3. 发展同位Hodge理论，定义扭曲伴随算子和同位Laplacian，证明紧致黎曼流形上的同位Hodge分解定理；4. 将Weyl尺度场建模为超曲面附近的固定分布，几何低阶项形成惩罚层以强制边界条件；5. 构建椭圆场方程中点源的非奇异模型。

Result: 1. 建立了与Witten变形等价的扭曲外微积分结构；2. 证明了紧致黎曼流形上的同位Hodge分解定理；3. 标量同位Laplacian为椭圆边值问题提供了严格的扩散界面表示；4. 惩罚层方法能在单个几何方程中强制Dirichlet、Neumann或Cauchy边界条件；5. 即使在经典不相容Cauchy数据情况下也能产生一致的弱解；6. 构建了保持正确Coulomb远场、消除核心奇异性且具有有限场能的点源非奇异模型。

Conclusion: 论文成功将经典Weyl几何扩展到仿射同位变换框架，建立了严格的同位Hodge理论。该理论为椭圆边值问题提供了统一的扩散界面表示方法，能够处理经典不相容的边界条件。构建的点源非奇异模型在保持物理正确性的同时消除了奇异性，具有重要的理论和应用价值。

Abstract: We introduce a homothetic extension of classical Weyl integrable geometry by generalizing the usual linear gauge transformations to affine homothetic transformations centered at a distinguished harmonic, scale-invariant form $α_d$. After relinearizing these affine gauge transformations via a suitable shift of variables, we obtain a twisted exterior calculus that is structurally equivalent to the Witten deformation of the de Rham complex. On this basis, we develop a corresponding homothetic Hodge theory: we define a twisted adjoint and homothetic Laplacian, and prove a homothetic Hodge decomposition theorem on compact Riemannian manifolds. In the context of partial differential equations, we show that the scalar homothetic Laplacian provides a rigorous diffuse interface (volume penalization) representation of elliptic boundary value problems. Modeling the Weyl scale field as a fixed distribution localized near a hypersurface, the resulting lower order geometric terms form a penalty layer that enforces Dirichlet, Neumann, or Cauchy data within a single geometric equation. This formulation yields consistent weak solutions even in the presence of classically incompatible Cauchy data. As an application, we construct a nonsingular model for point sources in elliptic field equations, which preserves the correct Coulombian far field while removing the core singularity and yielding finite field energy.

</details>


### [160] [A Concentration of Measure Phenomenon in the Principal Chiral Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27700)
*Tamer Tlas*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 利用测度集中现象研究O(N)主手征模型的大N极限，证明该极限下的配分函数对应自由有质量理论


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究O(N)主手征模型在大N极限下的行为，理解该量子场论模型在极限情况下的物理性质

Method: 应用测度集中现象（concentration of measure phenomenon）来分析O(N)主手征模型的大N极限

Result: 证明在大N极限下，配分函数对应一个自由有质量理论的配分函数

Conclusion: O(N)主手征模型在大N极限下简化为自由有质量理论，揭示了该模型在极限情况下的简化性质

Abstract: We utilize the concentration of measure phenomenon to study the large $N$ limit of the $O(N)$ principal chiral model. The partition function in this limit is demonstrated to be that of a free massive theory.

</details>


### [161] [Resonances in a Dirichlet quantum waveguide coupled to a cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27825)
*Sylwia Kondej,Nikoloz Kurtskhalia*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究Dirichlet波导中带腔体结构的共振特性，分析小间隙尺寸对共振极虚部的影响，发现共振时间尺度与间隙体积平方成反比


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究波导中嵌入特征值在腔体存在小间隙时如何转变为共振，探索间隙尺寸对共振特性的影响，特别是共振极的虚部行为

Method: 考虑二维和三维Dirichlet波导，分析带小间隙腔体结构，通过数学分析确定间隙尺寸与共振极虚部之间的标度关系

Result: 二维波导中，间隙尺寸为ε时，共振极虚部主项为O(ε²)；三维波导中，矩形开口体积为O(ε²)时，共振极虚部为O(ε⁴)；共振时间尺度与间隙体积平方成反比

Conclusion: 在分析的模型类中，共振特性强烈依赖于间隙尺寸，特征时间尺度与诱导共振的开口体积平方成反比，这为波导-腔体系统的共振行为提供了定量理解

Abstract: We consider a Dirichlet waveguide in $\mathbb{R}^n$ ($n = 2,3$) with an attached cavity. We show that if the cavity admits a small gap, then the original embedded eigenvalues turn into resonances. The main question we address is how the size of the gap affects the resonant properties, in particular the imaginary part of the resonant pole. For example, in the case of a two dimensional waveguide with a gap of size $\varepsilon$, we show that the leading order term of the resonance behaves as $\mathcal O (\varepsilon^2)$. In the three-dimensional case, if the aperture is defined by a rectangular opening with volume proportional to $\varepsilon^2$, the resonant component behaves as $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^4)$. This shows that, in the analyzed class of models, the characteristic time scale associated with the resonances is generically of order $\mathcal{O}((\mathrm{vol}_\varepsilon)^{-2})$, where $\mathrm{vol}_\varepsilon$ denotes the volume of the aperture inducing the resonance.

</details>


### [162] [A symmetry formula for correlation functions in the superintegrable chiral Potts spin chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27930)
*Haoran Zhu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 证明了N态超可积手性Potts自旋链中两点关联函数的精确有限体积对称公式，解决了Fabricius和McCoy的猜想


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究周期性N态超可积手性Potts自旋链中关联函数的对称性质，特别是验证Fabricius和McCoy关于中点关联函数实性的猜想

Method: 通过数学证明建立精确的有限体积对称公式，分析哈密顿量和单点平移算符共同本征态下的关联函数，并将三态链的结果推广到任意N和所有平移本征扇区

Result: 证明了对于任意链长L和所有共同本征态，关联函数满足对称关系⟨Z₀ʳZᴿ†ʳ⟩* = ⟨Z₀ʳZʟ₋ᴿ†ʳ⟩，当L为偶数时中点关联⟨Z₀ʳZʟ/₂†ʳ⟩为实数

Conclusion: 成功建立了N态超可积手性Potts自旋链的精确对称公式，完全解决了Fabricius和McCoy的猜想，为理解该模型的相关函数性质提供了理论基础

Abstract: We prove an exact finite-volume symmetry formula for two-point functions in the periodic $N$-state superintegrable chiral Potts spin chain. We show that, for every chain length $L$ and every simultaneous eigenvector of the Hamiltonian and the one-site translation operator, the correlations satisfy $\langle Z_0^r Z_R^{\dagger r}\rangle^*=\langle Z_0^r Z_{L-R}^{\dagger r}\rangle$ for $1\leqslant r\leqslant N-1$. Hence, whenever $L$ is even, the midpoint correlation $\langle Z_0^r Z_{L/2}^{\dagger r}\rangle$ is real. Then we generalise the three-state chain case to arbitrary $N$ and to every translation eigensector. This resolves a conjecture of Fabricius and McCoy.

</details>


### [163] [A Damage-Driven Model for Duchenne Muscular Dystrophy: Early-Stage Dynamics and Invasion Thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28147)
*Gaetana Gambino,Francesco Gargano,Alessandra Rizzo,Vincenzo Sciacca*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个基于损伤驱动范式的杜氏肌营养不良症空间扩展数学模型，研究免疫招募如何由组织损伤触发，分析显示疾病进展通过入侵过程而非图灵不稳定性发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为杜氏肌营养不良症建立一个空间扩展的数学模型，研究损伤驱动的免疫招募机制如何影响疾病进展，理解空间异质性在疾病发展中的作用。

Method: 构建反应-扩散-趋化系统描述健康组织、受损纤维、免疫细胞和炎症信号的相互作用；通过线性化分析早期动力学；推导入侵条件和最小传播速度；进行数值模拟验证。

Result: 扩散不诱导图灵不稳定性，空间异质性源于局部损伤扩张而非内在模式形成机制；建立了有效损伤繁殖阈值条件；病理前沿遵循拉前机制，确定了最小传播速度。

Conclusion: 该模型为早期疾病进展提供了数学框架，表明空间模式源于局部损伤的扩张而非扩散驱动的模式形成机制，疾病进展主要通过入侵过程发生。

Abstract: We introduce a spatially extended mathematical model for Duchenne muscular dystrophy based on a damage-driven paradigm, in which immune recruitment is triggered by tissue injury. The model is formulated as a reaction--diffusion--chemotaxis system describing the interaction between healthy tissue, damaged fibers, immune cells and inflammatory signals. We establish the global well-posedness of the system and investigate the early-stage dynamics through linearization around the healthy equilibrium. Our analysis shows that diffusion does not induce Turing instabilities, so that spatial heterogeneity cannot arise from diffusion-driven mechanisms. Instead, disease progression occurs through invasion processes. We derive explicit conditions for the onset of invasion, interpreted as an effective damage reproduction threshold and characterize the minimal propagation speed of pathological fronts, showing that the dynamics is governed by a pulled-front mechanism. Numerical simulations support the analytical results and confirm the transition between decay and invasion. These results provide a mathematical framework for early-stage disease progression and indicate that spatial patterns arise from the expansion of localized damage rather than from intrinsic pattern-forming mechanisms.

</details>


### [164] [Quasi-local probability averaging in the context of cutoff regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28235)
*A. V. Ivanov,I. V. Korenev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究任意维欧几里得空间中拉普拉斯算子特殊类型平均基本解的性质，提出适用于概率平均的核类，给出变形基本解及其零值的新表示，并提供与量子场模型相关的示例


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究拉普拉斯算子平均基本解的性质，特别是适用于概率平均的核类，为量子场论中的重整化问题提供数学工具

Method: 考虑一类适用于概率平均的核，提出变形基本解及其在零点值的新表示方法，通过具体量子场模型示例进行验证

Result: 建立了平均基本解的新表示形式，获得了变形基本解在零点的具体表达式，为量子场论重整化研究提供了数学框架

Conclusion: 该工作为任意维欧几里得空间中拉普拉斯算子的平均基本解研究提供了系统方法，所提出的表示形式在量子场论重整化问题中具有应用价值

Abstract: In this paper, we study the properties of averaged fundamental solutions of a special type for Laplace operators in the Euclidean space of an arbitrary dimension. We consider a class of kernels suitable for probabilistic averaging, and propose new representations for the deformed fundamental solutions and their values at zero. In addition, we give examples related to specific quantum field models in the context of studying renormalization properties.

</details>


### [165] [Three non-Hermitian random matrix universality classes of complex edge statistics: Spacing ratios and distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28457)
*Gernot Akemann,Georg Angermann,Noah Aygün,Adam Mielke,Patricia Päßler,Christoph Raitzig,Tobias Winkler*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究非厄米随机矩阵三种对称类的局部统计特性，包括复间隔比和最近邻间距分布，分析其在体区和边缘的行为，并与二维泊松过程对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非厄米随机矩阵三种通用对称类的局部统计特性，特别是复间隔比和最近邻间距分布，以理解这些系统在体区和边缘的普适行为。

Method: 采用解析和数值相结合的方法：1）对A类引入条件点过程简化表达式，研究椭圆Ginibre系综的参数相关N=3推测；2）数值比较三种对称类的复间隔比、其矩和最近邻间距分布，与二维泊松过程在体区和边缘的对比。

Result: 1）证明了早期近似在大N极限下在体区收敛良好；2）提出了椭圆Ginibre系综的参数相关N=3推测；3）发现不同边缘普适类的排斥程度可通过有效二维库仑气体描述理解；4）复间隔比在边缘不能完全展开局部统计；5）三种对称类在体区和边缘的小参数最近邻间距分布均符合立方排斥规律。

Conclusion: 非厄米随机矩阵的三种对称类在体区和边缘展现出不同的局部统计特性，复间隔比和最近邻间距分布能有效表征这些差异，但复间隔比在边缘的展开能力有限，而立方排斥规律在所有情况下均成立。

Abstract: The conjectured three generic local bulk statistics amongst all non-Hermitian random matrix symmetry classes have recently been extended to three generic local edge statistics. We study analytically and numerically complex spacing ratios and nearest-neighbour (NN) spacing distributions that characterise such local statistics. We choose the three simplest representatives of these universality classes, given by the Gaussian ensembles of complex Ginibre, complex symmetric and complex self-dual matrices, denoted by class A, AI$^†$ and AII$^†$. In the first part, we analytically study the complex spacing ratio in class A, at finite matrix size $N$. Introducing a conditional point process, we simplify existing expressions and show why an uncontrolled approximation introduced earlier converges well in the large-$N$ limit in the bulk. When specifying to the elliptic Ginibre ensemble, we present a parameter-dependent $N=3$ surmise for the complex spacing ratio, interpolating to that of the Gaussian unitary ensemble (GUE), where such a surmise is very accurate. In the second numerical part, we compare complex spacing ratios, its moments, and NN spacing distributions for all three ensembles with that of uncorrelated points, the two-dimensional (2D) Poisson process, both in the bulk and at the edge. The varying degree of repulsion within these different edge universality classes can be well understood in terms of an effective 2D Coulomb gas description, at different values of inverse temperature $β$. We find indications that the complex spacing ratio does not fully unfold the local statistics at the edge. Finally we verify that for small argument, in all three symmetry classes the NN spacing distributions in the bulk and at the edge are consistent with the universal cubic repulsion.

</details>


### [166] [Geometry of the Ising persistence problem and the universal Bonnet-Manin Painlevé VI distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28632)
*Ivan Dornic,Robert Conte*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文确定了非马尔可夫随机过程的完全持续概率分布，揭示了其由Painlevé VI系统控制，并与Bonnet曲面的平均曲率存在几何联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于相互作用自旋系统及相关系统中的首次通过问题，需要理解非马尔可夫随机过程的持续概率分布。

Method: 利用精确的Fredholm Pfaffian结构与可积sech核，将持续概率分解为偶数和奇数Fredholm行列式，通过求解二阶非线性常微分方程（Painlevé VI方程）来控制这些行列式。

Result: 证明了持续概率分布由Painlevé VI系统控制，该系统的解与Bonnet曲面的平均曲率一致；恢复了Derrida-Hakim-Pasquier的普遍持续指数，并给出了几何解释。

Conclusion: 非马尔可夫随机过程的持续概率分布具有精确的Painlevé VI结构，并与微分几何中的Bonnet曲面存在深刻联系，持续指数对应于相关曲面的渐近平均曲率。

Abstract: We determine the full persistence probability distribution for a non-Markovian stochastic process, motivated by first-passage questions arising in interacting spin systems and allied systems. We show that this distribution is governed by a distinguished Painlevé VI system arising from an exact Fredholm Pfaffian structure associated with the integrable sech kernel, $K_{\mathrm{sech}}=1/(2 π\cosh[(x-y)/2])$. The universal persistence exponent originally obtained by Derrida, Hakim and Pasquier is recovered as an asymptotic observable and acquires a natural geometric interpretation. In the stationary scaling regime, the persistence probability admits an exact Pfaffian decomposition into even and odd Fredholm determinants of the integrable \emph{sech} kernel. These determinants are controlled by a unique global solution of a second-order nonlinear ordinary differential equation, which is identified as a particular Painlevé VI equation. The corresponding Painlevé VI connection problem determines the persistence exponent as a limiting value at infinity.
  We further show that the Painlevé VI system governing persistence admits a direct geometric interpretation: the relevant solution coincides with the mean curvature of a one-parameter family of Bonnet surfaces immersed in $\mathbb R^3$. A folding transformation between such surfaces singles out the Painlevé VI equation with Manin coefficients $[0,0,0,0]$, which in particular governs the universal persistence distribution in the symmetric Ising case. In this framework, the persistence exponent is identified with the asymptotic mean curvature of the associated surface.

</details>


### [167] [Invariant measures of randomized quantum trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28664)
*Tristan Benoist,Sascha Lill,Cornelia Vogel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究随机化探针可观测量对量子轨迹的影响，证明非奇异随机化确保量子轨迹纯化并存在唯一不变概率测度，引入乘性本原性新概念，计算典型量子通道的不变测度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子轨迹是描述量子系统经受重复间接测量的马尔可夫链。其稳态特性取决于探针上测量的可观测量。本文旨在探索当探针可观测量选择被随机化时量子轨迹的性质，研究随机化如何正则化量子轨迹并影响其不变测度。

Method: 采用随机化探针可观测量的方法，引入非奇异随机化条件，证明量子轨迹的纯化性质。提出量子通道的"乘性本原性"新概念，研究不变测度的正则性。通过计算典型量子通道的不变测度，并用多个示例验证假设的边界。

Result: 证明非奇异随机化确保量子轨迹纯化，从而存在唯一不变概率测度。引入的乘性本原性概念强于本原性但弱于正性改善。计算了典型量子通道的不变测度，并通过示例展示了假设的适用范围和限制。

Conclusion: 随机化探针可观测量能正则化量子轨迹，非奇异随机化保证轨迹纯化和唯一不变测度存在。乘性本原性为量子通道的遍历性提供了新视角，典型量子通道的不变测度计算验证了理论框架的有效性，但某些假设存在边界情况。

Abstract: Quantum trajectories are Markov chains modeling quantum systems subjected to repeated indirect measurements. Their stationary regime depends on what observables are measured on the probes used to indirectly measure the system. In this article we explore the properties of quantum trajectories when the choice of probe observable is randomized. The randomization induces some regularization of the quantum trajectories. We show that non-singular randomization ensures that quantum trajectories purify and therefore accept a unique invariant probability measure. We furthermore study the regularity of that invariant measure. In that endeavour, we introduce a new notion of ergodicity for quantum channels, which we call multiplicative primitivity. It is a priory stronger than primitivity but weaker than positivity improving. Finally, we compute some invariant measures for canonical quantum channels and explore the limits of our assumptions with several examples.

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [Statistical modeling of breast cancer radiomic features and hazard using image registration-aided longitudinal CT data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26888)
*Subrata Mukherjee,Qian Cao,Thibaud Coroller,Ravi K. Samala,Nicholas Petrick,Berkman Sahiner*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该研究开发了RAMAC算法用于转移性乳腺癌患者CT扫描中病灶的纵向跟踪，并建立了结合基线影像组学特征、治疗后变化和人口统计学变量的可解释生存预测模型，提高了预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转移性乳腺癌患者在治疗过程中需要重复进行CT成像监测疾病进展。准确跟踪不同扫描时间点和不同放射科医生标注的病灶对于可靠的影像组学分析和临床决策至关重要。现有方法在跨时间点和不同标注者之间建立病灶对应关系方面存在挑战。

Method: 1. 开发了基于配准的自动匹配和对应算法（RAMAC），使用匈牙利算法建立不同放射科医生和成像时间点标注之间的病灶对应关系。2. 使用RAMAC处理的数据集，结合基线影像组学特征、第8、16、24周的治疗后变化以及人口统计学变量，开发可解释的影像组学生存模型。3. 针对纵向影像组学数据的高维特性，采用L1惩罚加性Cox比例风险模型和最佳子集选择进行特征降维，然后进行Cox建模。

Result: 1. RAMAC算法成功建立了跨时间点和不同标注者的病灶对应关系。2. 纳入更多成像时间点提高了预测性能，平均C-index从基线时的0.58提高到0.64。3. 联合建模显示纵向影像组学特征与生存结果之间存在显著的时间依赖性关联。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的RAMAC算法能够有效解决转移性乳腺癌患者CT扫描中病灶纵向跟踪的挑战。结合纵向影像组学特征的生存模型显著提高了无进展生存期的预测性能，为临床决策提供了有价值的工具。纵向影像组学特征与生存结果之间的显著关联表明这些特征在监测治疗反应和预测预后方面具有重要价值。

Abstract: Patients with metastatic breast cancer (mBC) undergo repeated computed tomography (CT) imaging during treatment to monitor disease progression. Accurate longitudinal tracking of individual lesions across scans from multiple radiologists is essential for reliable radiomic analysis and clinical decision-making. We conducted a retrospective study using serial chest CT scans from the Phase III MONALEESA-3 and MONALEESA-7 trials and developed statistical models for multi-source data integration and survival analysis. First, we introduced a Registration-based Automated Matching and Correspondence (RAMAC) algorithm to establish lesion correspondence across annotations from different radiologists and imaging time points using the Hungarian algorithm. Second, using the RAMAC-processed dataset, we developed interpretable radiomic survival models for progression-free survival prediction by combining baseline radiomic features, post-treatment changes at Weeks 8, 16, and 24, and demographic variables. To address the high dimensionality of longitudinal radiomic data, feature reduction was performed using an L1-penalized additive Cox proportional hazards model and best subset selection followed by Cox modeling. Model performance was evaluated using the concordance index (C-index). Incorporating additional imaging time points improved predictive performance, increasing the mean C-index from 0.58 at baseline to 0.64. Joint modeling further showed significant associations between longitudinal radiomic features and survival outcomes over time.

</details>


### [169] [Biased Mean Quadrangle and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26901)
*Anton Malandii,Stan Uryasev*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出"偏置均值回归"方法，用于估计偏置均值E[Y]+x，通过最小化超期望误差实现，可转化为线性规划问题，与分位数回归和CVaR优化等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一个基础统计问题，涵盖多种应用场景：如估计导致投资组合损失超过预期损失特定金额的因素，或估计影响环境中特定过量辐射释放的因素，其中核安全法规规定了不同的严重级别。

Method: 基于风险四边形框架，通过最小化超期望误差进行偏置均值估计。该方法可转化为线性规划问题，计算上具有吸引力。建立了与分位数回归和CVaR优化的等价关系。

Result: 建立了两个等价结果：(1)偏置均值回归在适当参数化下等价于分位数回归，当x=0时等价于普通最小二乘回归；(2)在投资组合优化中，最小化超期望风险等价于CVaR优化。数值实验验证了理论陈述。

Conclusion: 偏置均值回归提供了一种计算高效的方法，可作为普通最小二乘回归的良好替代方案，在风险四边形框架下具有理论保证，并在金融和核安全等领域有实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper introduces \emph{biased mean regression}, estimating the \emph{biased mean}, i.e., $\mathbb{E}[Y] + x$, where $x \in \mathbb{R}$. The approach addresses a fundamental statistical problem that covers numerous applications. For instance, it can be used to estimate factors driving portfolio loss exceeding the expected loss by a specified amount (e.g., $ x=\$10 billion$) or to estimate factors impacting a specific excess release of radiation in the environment, where nuclear safety regulations specify different severity levels.
  The estimation is performed by minimizing the so-called \emph{superexpectation error}. We establish two equivalence results that connect the method to popular paradigms: (i) biased mean regression is equivalent to quantile regression for an appropriate parameterization and is equivalent to ordinary least squares when $x=0$; (ii) in portfolio optimization, minimizing \emph{superexpectation risk}, associated with the superexpectation error, is equivalent to CVaR optimization. The approach is computationally attractive, as minimizing the superexpectation error reduces to linear programming (LP), thereby offering algorithmic and modeling advantages. It is also a good alternative to ordinary least squares (OLS) regression.
  The approach is based on the \emph{Risk Quadrangle} (RQ) framework, which links four stochastic functionals -- error, regret, risk, and deviation -- through a statistic. For the biased mean quadrangle, the statistic is the biased mean. We study properties of the new quadrangle, such as \emph{subregularity}, and establish its relationship to the quantile quadrangle. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical statements and illustrate the practical implications.

</details>


### [170] [The Load Management Paradox: Correcting the Healthy-Worker Survivor Effect in NBA Injury Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26935)
*Yue Yu,Guanyu Hu*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了NBA数据分析中"健康工人幸存者效应"导致的悖论：传统生存模型显示高负荷球员受伤风险更低，实际是选择偏倚造成的假象。作者开发了MS-PEM模型来校正这一结构混杂，发现调整选择偏倚后负荷与受伤风险呈正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在职业体育分析中，评估累积负荷与受伤风险的关系是核心目标。然而，应用于NBA比赛数据的传统生存模型产生了一个悖论：近期比赛时间长的球员似乎受伤可能性更低。研究者发现这种反直觉结果是"健康工人幸存者效应"的产物，即基于比赛参与的条件化导致了由未观察到的潜在体能驱动的严重碰撞偏倚。

Method: 为了解决这种结构混杂，研究者开发了边际结构分段指数模型（MS-PEM），该模型将逆概率处理加权（IPTW）与灵活的分段指数加性模型和加权累积暴露（WCE）相结合。通过模拟研究验证了选择机制在数学上足以完全逆转负荷与受伤之间的真实关联方向。

Result: 将MS-PEM应用于三个NBA赛季的78,594个球员-比赛观察数据（涵盖771名球员和2,439次受伤事件），发现调整观察到的选择偏倚可靠地将风险函数移回潜在的生理关系方向。校正的确切幅度对结果模型的正则化敏感：在保守交叉验证下衰减了1%-2%，在较轻惩罚下衰减了63%-78%。但因果校正的正方向在多个倾向得分规范和双重稳健性检验中高度稳健。

Conclusion: 这些结果为偏倚感知的体育损伤建模提供了方法学模板，同时警告仅依赖观察性比赛数据的模型会系统性地低估高负荷的真实风险，除非有更丰富的生理数据用于完全因果识别。研究强调了在体育分析中考虑选择偏倚的重要性。

Abstract: In professional sports analytics, evaluating the relationship between accumulated workload and injury risk is a central objective.
