However, naive survival models applied to NBA game-log data consistently yield a paradox: players who recently logged heavy minutes appear less likely to sustain an injury. We demonstrate that this counterintuitive result is an artifact of the healthy-worker survivor effect, wherein conditioning on game participation induces severe collider bias driven by unobserved latent fitness. To address this structural confounding, we develop a Marginal Structural Piecewise Exponential Model (MS-PEM) that unifies inverse probability of treatment weighting (IPTW) with flexible piecewise-exponential additive models and weighted cumulative exposure (WCE). A simulation study confirms that this selection mechanism is mathematically sufficient to entirely reverse the sign of the true association between workload and injury. Applying the MS-PEM to 78,594 player-game observations across three NBA seasons (encompassing 771 players and 2,439 injury events), we find that adjusting for observed selection reliably shifts the hazard back toward the underlying physiological relationship. While the exact magnitude of the correction is sensitive to outcome-model regularization (attenuating the paradoxical weight function by 1% to 2% under conservative cross-validation and up to 63% to 78% under lighter penalization), the positive direction of the causal correction is highly robust across multiple propensity specifications and doubly robust checks. Ultimately, these results provide a methodological template for bias-aware sports injury modeling, while cautioning that models relying strictly on observational game logs will systematically underestimate the true risk of heavy workloads without richer physiological data for full causal identification.

</details>


### [171] [Forecastability as an Information-Theoretic Limit on Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27074)
*Peter Maurice Catt*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个基于互信息的预测性理论框架，用于评估在给定信息集下每个预测时间窗口可获得的最大预测信息量，而不依赖于具体模型选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测通常关注模型选择，但本文从更基础的角度出发：在给定信息集下，每个预测时间窗口到底有多少预测信息可用？这为评估预测任务的可行性提供了理论基础，避免在信息不足的情况下盲目建模。

Method: 基于对数损失函数，建立未来观测值与信息集之间的互信息与最大可达到的预期损失减少之间的精确等式。通过这一等式推导出预测性剖面、信息压缩效应、截断误差预算、周期性继承等结构性质，并定义利用比率作为方法充分性的标准化诊断指标。

Result: 预测性剖面反映了过程的依赖结构，不一定是单调的；信息压缩只能降低预测性；有限滞后窗口与完整历史之间的差距给出了精确的截断误差预算；具有周期性依赖的过程，其剖面继承周期性；预测损失可分解为信息结构决定的不可约分量和方法决定的近似分量。

Conclusion: 该框架为评估预测任务的可行性提供了理论基础，可在建模前判断信息集是否包含足够的预测信息。虽然精确等式仅适用于对数损失，但当预测性接近零时，任何方法在任何损失函数下都无法显著改善无条件基线预测。

Abstract: Forecasting is usually framed as a problem of model choice. This paper starts earlier, asking how much predictive information is available at each horizon. Under logarithmic loss, the answer is exact: the mutual information between the future observation and the declared information set equals the maximum achievable reduction in expected loss. This paper develops the consequences of that identity. Forecastability, defined as this mutual information evaluated across horizons, forms a profile whose shape reflects the dependence structure of the process and need not be monotone. Three structural properties are derived: compression of the information set can only reduce forecastability; the gap between the profile under a finite lag window and the full history gives an exact truncation error budget; and for processes with periodic dependence, the profile inherits the periodicity. Predictive loss decomposes into an irreducible component fixed by the information structure and an approximation component attributable to the method; their ratio defines the exploitation ratio, a normalised diagnostic for method adequacy. The exact equality is specific to log loss, but when forecastability is near zero, classical inequalities imply that no method under any loss can materially improve on the unconditional baseline. The framework provides a theoretical foundation for assessing, prior to any modelling, whether the declared information set contains sufficient predictive information at the horizon of interest.

</details>


### [172] [Socioeconomic Drivers of Physical Morbidity Across U.S. Counties: A Spatial Causal Inference Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27095)
*Ranadeep Daw,Hunter Evans,Indrabati Bhattacharya*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种结合谱基函数和双重稳健广义倾向得分估计器的方法，用于分析社会经济因素对人口健康的因果效应，解决了空间自相关、混杂偏倚和地理逻辑推断等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别社会经济决定因素对人口健康的因果效应在统计方法发展和公共卫生实践及政策制定中具有重要意义。需要解决空间自相关（暴露和结果中均存在）、处理与协变量之间的混杂偏倚，以及地理逻辑推断需求等统计问题。

Method: 使用谱基函数（莫兰特征向量映射和ICAR精度矩阵特征向量）结合双重稳健广义倾向得分估计器处理连续处理变量。该方法同时处理空间自相关、混杂偏倚和地理逻辑推断问题。

Result: 将该框架应用于2022年美国县级健康数据，识别了六个选定预测因子对每月平均身体不健康天数的影响。研究展示了方法在实际公共卫生数据中的应用效果。

Conclusion: 该方法为社会经济因素对人口健康的因果推断提供了有效的统计框架，能够同时处理空间自相关和混杂偏倚问题。研究还讨论了进一步的应用和方法扩展作为未来研究方向。

Abstract: Identifying the causal effects of socioeconomic determinants on population health is of many great interests - from statistical methodology development to public health practitioners and policy developments. The statistical side of the problem needs to address several questions: spatial autocorrelation in both exposures and outcomes, confounding between treatments and covariates, and the need for geographically logical inference. We address these jointly by using spectral basis functions - Moran Eigenvector Maps and ICAR precision matrix eigenvectors - within a doubly robust generalized propensity score estimator for continuous treatments. Applied to 2022 county health data across the U.S. counties, the framework identifies the effect of six chosen predictors on the average physically unhealthy days per month. Possible further applications and methodological extensions are also discussed as future directions from this research.

</details>


### [173] [PyINLA: Fast Bayesian Inference for Latent Gaussian Models in Python](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27276)
*Esmail Abdul Fattah,Elias Krainski,Havard Rue*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: PyINLA是一个Python包，为INLA（集成嵌套拉普拉斯近似）方法提供Python接口，使Python用户能够使用这种确定性贝叶斯推断方法处理潜高斯模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的MCMC方法在处理复杂分层模型时计算成本高，特别是在需要重复模型拟合或硬件资源有限的情况下。INLA作为一种确定性替代方法，在潜高斯模型中能提供准确的后验边缘近似，但之前主要在R中通过INLA包使用，缺乏Python原生支持。

Method: 开发PyINLA包，提供Pythonic接口直接连接到现有的C语言实现的inla程序。该包允许在Python中指定潜高斯模型、运行基于INLA的推断，并直接访问后验摘要，同时利用成熟的INLA实现。

Result: PyINLA成功实现了在Python环境中使用INLA方法，能够处理包括广义线性混合模型、时间序列预测、疾病映射和地质统计预测在内的代表性模型。该包与常见的科学计算工作流程自然集成。

Conclusion: PyINLA为Python用户提供了使用INLA进行确定性贝叶斯推断的能力，填补了Python生态系统中高效潜高斯模型推断工具的空白，使研究人员能够在Python工作流程中利用INLA的计算优势。

Abstract: Bayesian inference often relies on Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods, particularly required for non-Gaussian data families. When dealing with complex hierarchical models, the MCMC approach can be computationally demanding in workflows that require repeated model fitting or when working with models of large dimensions with limited hardware resources. The Integrated Nested Laplace Approximations (INLA) is a deterministic alternative for models with non-Gaussian data that belong to the class of latent Gaussian models (LGMs), yielding accurate approximations to posterior marginals in many applied settings. The INLA method was implemented in C as a standalone program, inla, that is widely used in R through the INLA package. This paper introduces PyINLA, a dedicated Python package that provides a Pythonic interface directly to the inla program. Therefore, PyINLA enables specifying LGMs, running INLA-based inference, and accessing posterior summaries directly from Python while leveraging the established INLA implementation. We describe the package design and illustrate its use on representative models, including generalized linear mixed models, time series forecasting, disease mapping, and geostatistical prediction, demonstrating how deterministic Bayesian inference can be performed in Python using INLA in a way that integrates naturally with common scientific computing workflows.

</details>


### [174] [Cristiano Ronaldo or Lionel Messi, who is more consistent in scoring goals? The evidence from CFM exploratory analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27502)
*Samsul Anwar,Siti Munawarah,Radhiah Radhiah*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 使用竞争失效模式分析比较C罗和梅西在六种进球方式上的得分一致性，发现两人在17年职业生涯中具有相同的得分稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足球巨星C罗和梅西的竞争一直是广泛讨论的话题，本研究旨在通过多种进球方式比较两人的得分一致性水平。

Method: 使用竞争失效模式分析，基于Transfermarkt.com的数据，分析两人在六种进球方式（右脚射门、左脚射门、点球、直接任意球、远射、头球）上每次进球所需的时间间隔。

Result: 竞争失效模式探索性分析结果显示，C罗和梅西在超过17年的职业足球生涯中具有相同的得分一致性水平。

Conclusion: 两人都是现代足球时代最具天赋的球员，个人和团队成就远超世界其他足球运动员，且在得分稳定性方面表现相当。

Abstract: The rivalry between two football superstars Cristiano Ronaldo and Lionel Messi has always been a subject of extensive discussion. This study aimed to compare the level of consistency between the two players in scoring goals through 6 ways: right-footed kicks, left-footed kicks, penalty kicks, direct free kicks, long-range kicks, and headers. The data analyzed was the duration of time (minutes) each player took to score a goal in every match they played. The data was obtained from a football website called Transfermarkt.com. Competing Failure Modes (CFM) was used to measure the reliability of the two players in scoring goals based on those various ways. The results of CFM exploratory analysis showed that Ronaldo and Messi had the same level of consistency in scoring goals for more than 17 years of their professional football career. Both have been among most talented players in the modern football era with individual and team achievements that are far above other footballers around the world.

</details>


### [175] [A statistical perspective on higher-order interactions modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28273)
*Catherine Matias*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提供了超图建模的导览，超图作为表示高阶交互的框架，涵盖了从基础概念到统计模型和节点聚类的全面介绍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界系统中普遍存在高阶交互（涉及多个实体的交互），而传统的成对关系模型无法完全捕捉这些复杂现象，因此需要超图作为更强大的建模框架。

Method: 采用统计和概率视角，系统性地介绍超图建模：从高阶交互的普遍性说明开始，介绍超图的基础概念和符号，讨论描述性统计和图表示，探索各种统计模型，并解决节点聚类的关键任务。

Result: 提供了超图建模领域的全面导览，涵盖了从基础理论到实际应用的各个方面，为研究者理解高阶交互建模提供了系统框架。

Conclusion: 超图为建模高阶交互提供了强大框架，本文系统梳理了该领域的关键概念、方法和挑战，并指出了未来研究的开放性问题。

Abstract: Modeling higher-order interactions (HOI) has emerged as a crucial challenge in complex systems analysis, as many phenomena cannot be fully captured by pairwise relationships alone. Hypergraphs, which generalize graphs by allowing interactions among more than two entities, provide a powerful framework for representing such intricate dependencies. Adopting a statistical and probabilistic perspective on hypergraph modeling, we propose a guided tour through this emerging research area. We begin by illustrating the ubiquity of HOI in real-world systems, where interactions often involve groups of entities rather than isolated pairs. We then introduce the foundational concepts and notations of hypergraphs, discussing their descriptive statistics, graph-based representations, and the challenges associated with their complexity. We further explore a variety of statistical models for hypergraphs and address the critical task of node clustering. We conclude by outlining some open challenges in the field.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [A categorical p-adic Langlands correspondence for GL_2(Q_p)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26887)
*Andrea Dotto,Matthew Emerton,Toby Gee*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 将GL₂(Qₚ)的p进表示范畴嵌入到(φ,Γ)-模叠的导出范畴


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究p进表示与(φ,Γ)-模之间的深层联系，建立范畴层面的对应关系

Method: 构造一个完全忠实的函子，从GL₂(Qₚ)的p进表示导出范畴到(φ,Γ)-模叠的Ind-凝聚层导出范畴

Result: 成功建立了两个范畴之间的嵌入关系，为p进表示理论提供了新的几何视角

Conclusion: 该函子建立了p进表示与(φ,Γ)-模几何之间的紧密联系，为后续研究提供了重要工具

Abstract: Let p at least 5 be prime. We construct a fully faithful functor from the derived category of all smooth p-adic representations of GL_2(Q_p) (with a fixed central character) to a derived category of Ind-coherent sheaves on a stack of (phi,Gamma)-modules.

</details>


### [177] [Tempered vs generic automorphic functions and the canonical filtration on automorphic functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26925)
*Dennis Gaitsgory,Vincent Lafforgue,Sam Raskin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文引入并研究了自守函数空间上的一个滤过结构，该滤过是通过将经典朗兰兹猜想谱侧的滤过（由相干奇异支撑诱导）转移而得到的，并提出了连接该滤过与由Hecke算子解析谱产生的滤过的若干猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自守函数空间上的滤过结构，旨在建立经典朗兰兹猜想谱侧的相干奇异支撑诱导的滤过与Hecke算子解析谱产生的滤过之间的联系，深化对自守表示谱理论的理解。

Method: 在处处非分歧的函数域情形下，将经典朗兰兹猜想谱侧的滤过（由相干奇异支撑诱导）转移到自守函数空间上，并研究这一滤过结构。同时考虑由Hecke算子的解析谱产生的滤过，提出连接这两种滤过的猜想。

Result: 论文引入了一个新的滤过结构，该滤过源于相干奇异支撑概念，并提出了若干猜想，这些猜想旨在建立该滤过与由Hecke算子解析谱产生的滤过之间的联系。

Conclusion: 通过引入和研究自守函数空间上的滤过结构，论文为理解朗兰兹猜想谱侧结构与Hecke算子解析谱之间的关系提供了新的视角和猜想框架，为进一步研究自守表示的谱理论奠定了基础。

Abstract: We introduce and study the filtration on the space of automorphic functions (in the everywhere unramified situation for the function field case) obtained by transferring the filtration on the spectral side of the classical Langlands conjecture, induced by coherent singular support.
  We propose a number of conjectures that tie this filtration (which, by design, arises from the notion of cohomological support) to a filtration on the space of C-valued automorphic functions that arises by considering the analytic spectrum of Hecke operators.

</details>


### [178] [Asymptotic Universal Koszulity in Galois Cohomology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27032)
*Marina Palaisti*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文引入了渐近通用Koszul性的概念，研究无限维代数如何通过有限型通用Koszul二次子代数的滤过系统来逼近，并建立了该类的结构性质及其在伽罗瓦上同调中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无限维代数（特别是伽罗瓦上同调环）的Koszul性质，建立一种能够通过有限维逼近来刻画无限维代数同调性质的框架，连接同调代数、二次代数和伽罗瓦上同调理论。

Method: 引入渐近通用Koszul性的概念，建立其基本结构性质（滤过余极限、直积、基变换的稳定性），提出有限型局部准则，在伽罗瓦上同调中证明pro-p群的余极限定理，分析有限生成商代数，并建立条件性的局部-全局和拼接原理。

Result: 建立了渐近通用Koszul代数的结构性质，证明了伽罗瓦上同调环在温和条件下可作为有限商滤过余极限出现，给出了证明群上同调代数是渐近通用Koszul的一般准则，获得了有限生成商代数的有限型捕获结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为连接同调代数、二次代数和伽罗瓦上同调提供了灵活的结构框架，建立了渐近通用Koszul性的系统理论，并指出了在算术背景下进一步研究的方向，特别是局部-全局和拼接原理的应用前景。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of asymptotic universal Koszulity for graded-commutative algebras generated in degree~$1$, capturing the idea that an infinite-dimensional algebra can be approximated by a filtered system of finite-type universally Koszul quadratic subalgebras. We establish basic structural properties of this class, including stability under filtered colimits, direct products, and base change, as well as a local finite-type criterion expressed in terms of finite-dimensional subspaces of the degree-one component. In the context of Galois cohomology, we prove a colimit theorem for pro-$p$ groups under mild assumptions, showing that cohomology rings arise as filtered colimits of finite quotients. This yields a general criterion under which the cohomology algebra of a profinite group is asymptotically universally Koszul. We further analyze finitely generated quotients via a finite-type capture result, identifying their cohomology with canonical quadratic subalgebras of the ambient algebra. Finally, we formulate conditional local--global and patching principles that isolate the mechanisms by which asymptotic universal Koszulity may arise in arithmetic settings. These results provide a flexible structural framework linking homological algebra, quadratic algebras, and Galois cohomology, and suggest several directions for further investigation.

</details>


### [179] [Improved Bounds for 3-Progressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27045)
*Rushil Raghavan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了无三阶等差数列的子集大小上界：|A| ≤ exp(-c log(N)^{1/6} log log(N)^{-1}) N


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究集合中无三阶等差数列（即不存在三个不同元素满足 a+b=2c）的子集大小上界问题，这是组合数论和加性组合学中的经典问题

Method: 1. 使用Kelley和Meka筛选论证的迭代变体；2. 采用Bloom和Sisask改进的Croot-Sisask几乎周期性自举论证

Result: 证明了如果A⊂{1,...,N}不含非平凡三阶等差数列，则|A| ≤ exp(-c log(N)^{1/6} log log(N)^{-1}) N，其中c>0为绝对常数

Conclusion: 该结果改进了无三阶等差数列子集大小的上界，展示了迭代筛选和改进的几乎周期性论证在加性组合学中的有效性

Abstract: We prove that if $A\subset \{1,\dots,N\}$ has no nontrivial three-term arithmetic progressions, then $|A|\leq \exp(-c\log(N)^{1/6}\log\log(N)^{-1})N$ for some absolute constant $c>0$. To obtain this bound, we use an iterated variant of the sifting argument of Kelley and Meka, as well as an improved bootstrapping argument for Croot-Sisask almost-periodicity due to Bloom and Sisask.

</details>


### [180] [A note on the multiple zeta functions and their variants at identical arguments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27266)
*Pawan Singh Mehta*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多重zeta函数及其变体在相同参数下的性质，利用调和乘积将其表示为黎曼zeta函数的多项式，并建立了多重zeta函数、多重t函数及其星型变体之间的函数关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多重zeta函数及其变体在相同参数下的性质，探索它们与黎曼zeta函数之间的关系，并建立不同函数之间的函数关系。

Method: 使用调和乘积将多重zeta函数及其变体表示为黎曼zeta函数的多项式，利用完全贝尔多项式描述这些多项式的系数，从而分析函数的奇异性并建立函数关系。

Result: 给出了多重zeta函数多项式系数的显式描述（通过完全贝尔多项式），完整描述了这些函数的奇异性，并建立了多重zeta函数、多重t函数及其星型变体在相同参数下的函数关系。

Conclusion: 通过调和乘积和完全贝尔多项式，成功建立了多重zeta函数及其变体之间的函数关系，为研究这些特殊函数在相同参数下的性质提供了系统的理论框架。

Abstract: In this article, we study the multiple zeta functions (MZF) and some of its variants at identical arguments. Using the harmonic product, these functions can be expressed as polynomials in the Riemann zeta function. Firstly, we note that an explicit description of the coefficients of these polynomials can be given in terms of the values of the complete Bell polynomials. This for instance, easily leads to the complete description of the singularities of these functions. But more importantly, this enables us to establish a functional relation between MZF, Multiple $t$-functions (M$t$F) and their star variants at identical arguments.

</details>


### [181] [NP-hardness of SVP in Euclidean Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27398)
*Daqing Wan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了van Emde Boas关于格中最短非零向量计算是NP难问题的猜想，从而去随机化了Ajtai的经典随机性结果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决van Emde Boas在1981年提出的猜想：在欧几里得空间中计算格的最短非零向量是NP难问题，并以此去随机化Ajtai在1998年的经典随机性结果

Method: 基于Bennet-Peifert（2023）通过Reed-Solomon码构造局部稠密格的方法，并关键性地依赖于Deligne关于有限域上高维簇的Weil猜想的工作

Result: 成功证明了van Emde Boas猜想为真，即计算格的最短非零向量确实是NP难问题，从而实现了对Ajtai随机性结果的去随机化

Conclusion: 该研究解决了计算几何和密码学中长期存在的开放问题，为格问题的复杂性提供了确定性证明，具有重要的理论和应用意义

Abstract: van Emde Boas (1981) conjectured that computing a shortest non-zero vector of a lattice in an Euclidean space is NP-hard. In this paper, we prove that this conjecture is true and hence de-randomize the classical randomness result of Ajtai (1998). Our proof builds on the construction of Bennet-Peifert (2023) on locally dense lattices via Reed-Solomon codes, and depends crucially on the work of Deligne on the Weil conjectures for higher dimensional varieties over finite fields.

</details>


### [182] [The congruence subgroup property for $S$-arithmetic subgroups of simple algebraic groups when $S$ has positive Dirichlet density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27472)
*Andrei S. Rapinchuk*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在满足Margulis-Platonov猜想的前提下，对于某些特定条件的赋值集S，绝对几乎单连通代数群的同余核C^S(G)是平凡的，这为Serre同余子群猜想提供了新证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究代数群的同余子群问题，特别是Serre同余子群猜想，该猜想涉及代数群的算术子群与同余子群之间的关系。论文旨在在更一般的条件下证明同余核的平凡性，为这一重要猜想提供新的证据。

Method: 采用非分情况讨论的统一方法，结合关于同余核的先前结果和最近关于几乎强逼近的新结果。关键技巧是构造适当的赋值集S，使其满足特定条件：包含所有阿基米德赋值，但不包含任何使得G在完备化K_v上各向同性的非阿基米德赋值v，且S与在M/K中完全分裂的赋值集的交集具有正Dirichlet密度。

Result: 证明了在上述条件下，同余核C^S(G)是平凡的。这一结果为Serre同余子群猜想提供了新的支持证据，表明在相当一般的条件下，代数群的算术子群与同余子群具有相同的结构。

Conclusion: 论文建立了关于绝对几乎单连通代数群同余核平凡性的新定理，避免了传统的分情况讨论方法，为Serre同余子群猜想的最终解决提供了重要进展。证明方法具有一般性，可应用于更广泛的代数群理论问题。

Abstract: Let $G$ be an absolutely almost simple simply connected algebraic group defined over a number field $K$, and let $M/K$ be the minimal Galois extension over which $G$ becomes an inner form of a split group. Assume that $G$ satisfies the Margulis-Platonov conjecture over $K$. We prove that if $S$ is a set of valuations of $K$ that contains all archimedean ones but does not contain any nonarchimedean valuations $v$ for which $G$ is anisotropic over the completion $K_v$ such that its intersection $S \cap \mathrm{Spl}(M/K)$ with the set $\mathrm{Spl}(M/K)$ of nonarchimedean valuations of $K$ that split completely in $M$ has positive Dirichlet density, then the congruence kernel $C^S(G)$ is trivial. This result provides additional evidence for Serre's Congruence Subgroup Conjecture. The proof does not involve any case-by-case considerations and relies on previous results concerning the congruence kernel and recent results on almost strong approximation.

</details>


### [183] [Monodromy groups of polynomials of composition length 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27609)
*Angelot Behajaina,Joachim König,Danny Neftin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究两个不可分解多项式复合的monodromy群，证明它们要么满足"大性"性质，要么属于明确的例外列表


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多项式复合的monodromy群性质对于处理多个多项式的复合至关重要，这些结果在多项式算术问题中有广泛应用预期，特别是为解决Davenport、Lewis和Schinzel提出的长期开放问题提供关键工具

Method: 研究两个不可分解多项式的复合的monodromy群，分析其代数性质，建立"大性"性质与例外情况的分类

Result: 证明两个不可分解多项式复合的monodromy群要么满足特定的"大性"性质，要么属于一个明确的例外列表

Conclusion: 该结果为处理多个多项式复合提供了关键理论工具，是解决Davenport-Lewis-Schinzel长期开放问题的核心组成部分，在多项式算术领域具有广泛应用前景

Abstract: We study the monodromy groups of compositions of two indecomposable polynomials. In particular, we show that such monodromy groups either fulfill a certain "largeness" property, or are in an explicit list of exceptions. Such largeness results are crucial for dealing with compositions of more than two polynomials, and consequently are expected to have a wide range of applications to problems concerning the arithmetic of polynomials. Concretely, our main result is a key ingredient in the solution of a long-standing open problem due to Davenport, Lewis and Schinzel, achieved in a companion paper.

</details>


### [184] [Sharp Hausdorff Dimension Bounds for Sets with Bounded and Growing Digits in $N$-expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27635)
*Andreea Catalina Chitu,Gabriela Ileana Sebe,Dan Lascu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了N-展开中数字有界或趋于无穷的无理数集合的Hausdorff维数，获得了精确的上下界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究N-展开中数字有界或趋于无穷的无理数集合的Hausdorff维数，将经典连分数结果推广到N-展开，探索参数N对维数性质的影响。

Method: 结合基本区间长度的精细估计与优化覆盖论证（上界），以及通过质量分布原理的康托集构造（下界）。

Result: 1) 对数字有界于M≥N的集合，获得改进的Jarník型界，推广并精化了经典连分数结果；2) 对数字趋于无穷的集合，获得精确渐近，证明数字趋于无穷的集合维数恰好为1/2，且数字一致大的集合维数随下界增加以对数衰减方式趋近1/2。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了参数N如何影响这些例外集合的维数性质，将Good定理推广到N-展开，建立了精确的维数估计。

Abstract: We establish sharp bounds for the Hausdorff dimension of sets of irrational numbers in $(0,1)$ whose digits in the $N$-expansion are either uniformly bounded or tend to infinity. For sets with digits bounded by an integer $M \ge N$, we obtain improved Jarník-type bounds that generalize and refine classical results for regular continued fractions, with explicit dependence on $N$ (Theorem~1.1). For sets with digits that grow without bound, we obtain precise asymptotics that extend Good's theorems to $N$-expansions, proving in particular that the set of numbers whose digits tend to infinity has Hausdorff dimension exactly $1/2$, and that the dimension of sets with uniformly large digits approaches $1/2$ as the lower bound increases, with explicit logarithmic decay (Theorem~1.2). The results reveal how the parameter $N$ influences the dimensional properties of these exceptional sets. Our methods combine careful estimates of fundamental interval lengths with optimized covering arguments for upper bounds and Cantor set constructions via mass distribution principles for lower bounds.

</details>


### [185] [Galois representation of the product of two Drinfeld modules of generic characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27710)
*Lian Duan,Jiangxue Fang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了Drinfeld模乘积的Galois表示，证明了对于任意有限素数集，相应乘积表示的像足够大，与由自然行列式条件定义的子群可公度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Drinfeld模乘积的Galois表示，作为Serre关于椭圆曲线乘积Galois表示像的经典结果在函数域情形的类比。

Method: 结合Pink关于局部域上线性群紧子群的最小模型理论，以及全局函数域的显式互反律。

Result: 证明了对于任意有限素数集，Drinfeld模乘积Galois表示的像足够大，与由自然行列式条件定义的子群可公度。

Conclusion: 建立了Drinfeld模乘积Galois表示像的大小性结果，这是Serre经典定理在函数域情形的类比，为函数域算术几何提供了新工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the Galois representations attached to products of Drinfeld modules. As an analogue of Serre's classical result on the images of Galois representations associated with products of elliptic curves, we prove that for any finite set of primes, the image of the corresponding product representation is sufficiently large, in the sense that it is commensurable with a subgroup defined by a natural determinant condition. Our approach combines Pink's minimal model theory for compact subgroups of linear groups over local fields with explicit reciprocity laws for global function fields.

</details>


### [186] [The Davenport-Lewis-Schinzel problem on the reducibility of $f(X)-g(Y)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27728)
*Angelot Behajaina,Joachim König,Danny Neftin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文解决了Davenport-Lewis-Schinzel问题，给出了多项式f(X)-g(Y)在复数域上可约性的完整描述，并应用于Hilbert-Siegel问题、算术动力学和函数方程求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决起源于1950年代的Davenport-Lewis-Schinzel问题，该问题涉及复数域上多项式f(X)-g(Y)的可约性。这一问题与Hilbert-Siegel问题密切相关，后者关注多项式映射纤维的可约性条件。

Method: 作者通过分析多项式f(X)-g(Y)的可约性条件，建立了完整的理论框架。特别关注那些合成因子避开特定低次多项式的多项式映射f，并明确描述了在哪些整数上f的纤维是可约的。

Result: 完全解决了Davenport-Lewis-Schinzel问题，为Hilbert-Siegel问题提供了几乎完整的解决方案。建立了多项式纤维可约性的明确判据，并将结果应用于算术动力学中迭代的稳定性分析，以及函数方程f(X)=g(Y)在复数有理函数域中的求解。

Conclusion: 该研究彻底解决了长期存在的Davenport-Lewis-Schinzel问题，为多项式可约性理论提供了重要工具，并在Hilbert-Siegel问题、算术动力学和函数方程求解等多个领域获得了重要应用。

Abstract: We solve the problem of Davenport--Lewis--Schinzel (DLS), originating in the 1950s, regarding the reducibility of $f(X)-g(Y)\in\mathbb C[X,Y]$. This yields an almost-complete solution to the Hilbert--Siegel problem: For a polynomial map $f$ whose composition factors avoid only very specific low-degree polynomials, we explicitly describe over which integers the fibers of $f$ are reducible. We further apply the solution to stability of iterates of $f$ in arithmetic dynamics, and to solving the functional equation $f(X)=g(Y)$ in $X,Y\in\mathbb{C}(z)$.

</details>


### [187] [Some vanishing results for the rational completed cohomology of Shimura varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27932)
*Kai-Wen Lan,Lue Pan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 基于Bhatt的混合特征近Kodaira型消失结果，证明在一般Shimura簇的局部解析完备上同调中，足够正则的无穷小权仅出现在中间度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Shimura簇的局部解析完备上同调中无穷小权的分布规律，特别是正则权在度数上的限制

Method: 利用Bhatt在混合特征下的近Kodaira型消失定理，结合局部解析完备上同调理论，分析无穷小权的度数分布

Result: 证明在一般Shimura簇的局部解析完备上同调中，足够正则的无穷小权仅出现在中间度，其他度数不包含此类权

Conclusion: 该结果为Shimura簇的局部解析完备上同调中无穷小权的分布提供了重要限制，揭示了正则权在度数上的特殊性质

Abstract: Based on an almost Kodaira-type vanishing result in mixed characteristics of Bhatt, we show that, in the locally analytic completed cohomology of a general Shimura variety, sufficiently regular infinitesimal weights can only show up in the middle degree.

</details>


### [188] [On the locally analytic completed cohomology of modular curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27937)
*Lue Pan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 关于模曲线完备上同调局部解析向量的研究综述


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究模曲线完备上同调的局部解析向量，旨在理解p-adic自守形式与Galois表示之间的深刻联系，特别是通过局部解析向量这一概念来探索p-adic Langlands对应关系

Method: 采用p-adic解析方法，研究完备上同调模的局部解析向量，结合模曲线的几何性质与p-adic表示理论，分析局部解析向量的结构与性质

Result: 建立了完备上同调局部解析向量的系统理论，揭示了其与p-adic自守形式和Galois表示之间的对应关系，为p-adic Langlands纲领提供了新的视角和工具

Conclusion: 局部解析向量为研究模曲线完备上同调提供了强有力的p-adic解析框架，深化了对p-adic自守形式与Galois表示之间联系的理解，推动了p-adic Langlands纲领的发展

Abstract: We survey our works on the locally analytic vectors of completed cohomology of modular curves.

</details>


### [189] [Products of consecutive integers with unusual anatomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27990)
*Terence Tao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了与阶乘方程 $a_1! a_2! a_3! = m^2$ 相关的三种区间类型：bad（坏区间）、very bad（非常坏区间）和 type $F_3$ 区间，回答了 Erdős 和 Graham 提出的若干问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究这些区间概念的动机源于分析阶乘方程 $a_1! a_2! a_3! = m^2$（其中 $a_1<a_2<a_3$）。Erdős 和 Graham 提出了关于这些区间数量的问题，需要建立相应的渐近公式

Method: 通过数论和组合分析方法，建立三种区间（bad、very bad、type $F_3$）的计数渐近公式。对于 bad 和 very bad 区间，获得精确的渐近结果；对于 type $F_3$ 区间，获得接近渐近的结果

Result: 获得了 bad 区间和 very bad 区间中包含的整数数量的渐近公式，以及 type $F_3$ 区间右端点数量的近渐近公式。这些结果直接应用于阶乘方程 $a_1! a_2! a_3! = m^2$ 的解的数量估计

Conclusion: 成功回答了 Erdős 和 Graham 提出的关于三种特殊区间计数的问题，为阶乘方程 $a_1! a_2! a_3! = m^2$ 的研究提供了重要的理论工具和渐近结果

Abstract: Call an interval $\{N+1,\dots,N+H\}$ of consecutive natural numbers \emph{bad} if the product $(N+1) \dots (N+H)$ is divisible by the square of its largest prime factor; \emph{very bad} if this product is powerful, and \emph{type $F_3$} if it has the same squarefree component as a factorial. Such concepts arose in the analysis of the factorial equation $a_1! a_2! a_3! = m^2$ with $a_1<a_2<a_3$. Answering several questions of Erdős and Graham, we obtain asymptotics for the number of integers contained in bad or very bad intervals, and to get near-asymptotics for the number of right endpoints of a type $F_3$ interval, or on the number of solutions to $a_1! a_2! a_3! = m^2$.

</details>


### [190] [Zeta Zeros in a Narrow Vertical Box](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28104)
*Daniel A. Goldston,Ade Irma Suriajaya*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文提供了一个简化证明，表明在假设黎曼ζ函数零点位于临界线附近一个宽度为b/logT的窄垂直盒子中时，当b→0时，渐近地至少有2/3的零点既是简单的又位于临界线上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Montgomery在1973年证明了在黎曼假设(RH)下，黎曼ζ函数至少有2/3的零点是简单零点。本文旨在提供一个更简单的证明，将RH条件替换为更一般的零点分布假设，从而得到零点既是简单的又位于临界线上的结果。

Method: 采用Montgomery证明的直接推广方法，假设所有零点位于高度T到2T之间、宽度为b/logT、以临界线为中心的窄垂直盒子中。当b=b(T)→0时，通过分析零点分布来证明简单零点在临界线上的比例。

Result: 在给定假设条件下，渐近地至少有2/3的黎曼ζ函数零点既是简单的又位于临界线上。这扩展了Montgomery的原始结果，将RH条件替换为更具体的零点位置约束。

Conclusion: 通过简化证明方法，本文展示了在零点分布满足特定几何约束的条件下，可以获得比Montgomery原始定理更强的结果——零点不仅是简单的，而且位于临界线上。这为研究黎曼ζ函数零点的多重性和位置提供了新的视角。

Abstract: In 1973 Montgomery proved, assuming the Riemann Hypothesis (RH), that asymptotically at least 2/3 of zeros of the Riemann zeta-function are simple zeros. In a previous note (arXiv:2511.20059 [math.NT]) we showed how RH can be replaced with a general estimate for a double sum over zeros, and this allows one to then obtain results on zeros that are both simple and on the critical line. Here we give a simple proof based on a direct generalization of Montgomery's proof that on assuming all the zeros are in a narrow vertical box between height $T$ and $2T$ of width $b/\log T$ and centered on the critical line, then, if $b=b(T)\to 0$ as $T\to \infty$, we have asymptotically at least 2/3 of the zeros are simple and on the critical line.

</details>


### [191] [Plectic Heegner classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28327)
*Michele Fornea*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 引入新的部分全局伽罗瓦上同调类集合，包含丛状赫格纳点和模拟丛状不变量，为高秩椭圆曲线算术提供更精细控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服先前构造的限制，为高秩椭圆曲线的算术研究提供更精细的控制工具，并与Tate猜想的丛状细化兼容

Method: 系统使用系数为p-adic测度（取值于伽罗瓦上同调）的自守函数，通过志村曲线的单值化而非高维四元数志村簇来构造

Result: 构建了丛状赫格纳类，前者作为丛状赫格纳点的局部化恢复，后者作为"部分弗罗贝尼乌斯"作用的本征空间投影出现

Conclusion: 丛状赫格纳类为高秩椭圆曲线算术提供更精细控制，其构造与Tate猜想的丛状细化兼容，通过志村曲线单值化实现

Abstract: We introduce a new collection of partially global Galois cohomology classes subsuming both plectic Heegner points and mock plectic invariants. The former are recovered as localizations of plectic Heegner classes, while the latter arise as eigenspace projections with respect to a "partial Frobenius"-action. By overcoming some limitations of previous constructions, plectic Heegner classes are expected to provide finer control over the arithmetic of higher rank elliptic curves. We are able to perform our construction via a systematic use of certain automorphic functions whose coefficients are p-adic measures valued in Galois cohomology. As we produce these functions through the uniformization of Shimura curves -- rather than higher dimensional quaternionic Shimura varieties -- our results are compatible with a plectic refinement of Tate's conjectures.

</details>


### [192] [Special geodesics and atypical intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28465)
*Matteo Tamiozzo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了非模多项式定义的复不可约平面曲线仅包含有限多条实代数曲线，这些曲线的坐标轴投影是特殊测地线的并集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究复平面曲线中具有特殊几何性质的实代数曲线的存在性问题，特别是那些坐标投影为特殊测地线并集的曲线。

Method: 采用代数几何和复分析的方法，结合模函数理论和测地线几何，分析非模多项式定义的复不可约平面曲线的性质。

Result: 证明了对于非模多项式定义的复不可约平面曲线，其中坐标轴投影为特殊测地线并集的实代数曲线只有有限多条。

Conclusion: 非模多项式定义的复不可约平面曲线中具有特殊测地线投影性质的实代数曲线是有限的，这一结果揭示了此类曲线的几何约束特性。

Abstract: Let $C$ be a complex irreducible plane curve that is not the vanishing locus of a modular polynomial. We show that $C$ contains finitely many real algebraic curves whose projection on each coordinate axis is a union of special geodesics.

</details>


### [193] [Farey graphs and geodesic expansions of complex continued fractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28468)
*Hitoshi Nakada,Rie Natsui,Jörg Thuswaldner*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究欧几里得虚二次域Q(√-d)的复Farey图，构建三维双曲空间中的Farey多边形，恢复Hecke群作用的双曲平面镶嵌，并通过Bianchi群作用定义三维双曲空间的Farey镶嵌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究欧几里得虚二次数域Q(√-d)（d∈{1,2,3,7,11}）的复Farey图结构，扩展A. Schmidt的Farey多边形到三维双曲空间，探索双曲几何中的Farey镶嵌和复连分数展开。

Method: 使用复Farey图构建三维双曲空间H³中的双曲Farey多边形；通过Hecke群H₄和H₆作用恢复双曲平面H²的镶嵌；利用双曲Farey多边形定义多面体，通过特定Bianchi群作用诱导H³的Farey镶嵌；采用复连分数展开的测地线方法。

Result: 恢复了I. Short和M. Walker研究的由Hecke群H₄和H₆作用定义的双曲平面镶嵌；定义了诱导三维双曲空间Farey镶嵌的多面体；提供了比M. Hockman讨论更一般化的复连分数展开方法。

Conclusion: 复Farey图和双曲Farey多边形为研究欧几里得虚二次域的几何结构提供了统一框架，将二维Farey镶嵌推广到三维，并为复连分数展开提供了更一般的方法。

Abstract: We discuss complex Farey graphs for the Euclidean imaginary quadratic number fields $\mathbb Q(\sqrt{-d})$, $d\in\{1, 2, 3, 7, 11\}$. We study hyperbolic versions of A. Schmidt's Farey polygons living in $3$-dimensional hyperbolic space $\mathbb{H}^3$. Using these Farey polygons we recover tessellations of the hyperbolic plane $\mathbb{H}^2$ that are defined by the action of the Hecke groups $H_4$ and $H_6$ and have been studied earlier by I. Short and M. Walker. Moreover, hyperbolic Farey polygons allow us to define polyhedra that induce Farey tessellations of $\mathbb{H}^3$ by the action of certain Bianchi groups. Using complex Farey graphs we consider geodesic complex continued fraction expansions. Our method provides a different and more general approach as the one from the discussion by M. Hockman.

</details>


### [194] [Complete Walsh spectra for a permutation-inverse family of Boolean functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28491)
*Kaimin Cheng*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了有限域上的一类布尔函数的Walsh谱，证明了该函数是bent函数当且仅当参数α是域中的非立方元，验证了一个已知猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限域上特定构造的布尔函数的Walsh谱特性，特别是验证Li等人关于该函数bent性质的猜想。

Method: 通过有限域论方法处理β∈𝔽_q部分，将β∈𝔽_{q^2}\𝔽_q部分转化为迹零空间上的Hadamard问题，并与已知的Kerdock和Delsarte-Goethals壳结构建立联系。

Result: 完全确定了f_α的所有Walsh值及其重数，证明了f_α是bent函数当且仅当α是𝔽_q中的非立方元，验证了Li等人的猜想。

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了关于特定布尔函数Walsh谱的完整刻画问题，验证了相关猜想，并将结果与已知的编码理论结构联系起来，展示了不同坐标表示下的等价性。

Abstract: Let $q=2^e$ with $e$ even, and let $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$ be the finite field of order $q^2$. Put $d=(q^2+q+1)/3$, and consider the permutation polynomial $$σ(X)=X+X^d+X^{dq}\in\mathbb{F}_{q^2}[X].$$ For $α\in\mathbb{F}_q^*$, define the Boolean function $$ f_α(x)=\text{Tr}_{q^2}\bigl(α(σ^{-1}(x))^3\bigr),\qquad x\in\mathbb{F}_{q^2},$$ where $\text{Tr}_{q^2}$ denotes the absolute trace from $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$ to $\mathbb{F}_{2}$. In this paper, we determine all Walsh values of $f_α$ and their multiplicities. In particular, $f_α$ is bent if and only if $α$ is a noncube in $\mathbb{F}_q$, proving a conjecture of Li, Li, Helleseth, and Qu. The $β\in\mathbb{F}_q$ part of the spectrum is handled by an elementary finite-field argument, whereas the $β\in\mathbb{F}_{q^2}\setminus\mathbb{F}_q$ part is reduced to a Hadamard problem on the trace-zero space. We then identify the resulting word with a normalized odd-dimensional shortened Kerdock shell in the noncube case and with a normalized first shortened Delsarte--Goethals shell in the cube case. To make the external shell input independently checkable, we separate the finite-field dictionary from the cited shell theorem and verify directly the degenerate case $e=2$. As an application, we show that a recent cyclotomic family of Xie, Li, Wang, and Zeng is the same construction in different coordinates.

</details>


### [195] [The conjecture of Colmez and reciprocity laws for modular forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28536)
*Vincent Maillot,Damian Rössler*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出并证明了Colmez猜想的一个细化版本，该猜想将CM阿贝尔簇的Faltings高度与Artin L-函数的对数导数联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Colmez在1993年提出的猜想指出CM阿贝尔簇的Faltings高度可以表示为Artin L-函数对数导数的线性组合。由于Faltings高度是定义数域上嵌入的超越量平均值，作者希望细化这一猜想，识别每个单独的超越量。

Method: 提出了Colmez猜想的细化版本，将每个嵌入对应的超越量具体识别。通过展示该猜想如何蕴含Siegel模形式在CM点取值的精细互反律，并利用Siegel和Hasse关于椭圆单位的经典结果证明了椭圆曲线情形。

Result: 成功证明了细化猜想在椭圆曲线情形成立，建立了椭圆曲线Faltings高度与Artin L-函数对数导数之间的精确对应关系。

Conclusion: 本文提出的细化猜想不仅深化了Colmez原始猜想，还为Siegel模形式在CM点的取值提供了精细互反律的理论基础，并在椭圆曲线情形得到了完全证明。

Abstract: In an article published in 1993, P. Colmez formulated a remarkable conjecture, which asserts that the Faltings height of a CM abelian variety can be computed as a linear combination of logarithmic derivatives of Artin $L$-functions. Noting that the Faltings height is an average of transcendental quantities summed over the embeddings of a number field of definition of the abelian variety, we propose a refinement of this conjecture, which identifies each of these transcendental quantities. We also show how our conjecture would imply the existence of fine reciprocity laws for Siegel modular forms with rational coefficients evaluated at CM points, and we prove our conjecture for elliptic curves, using old results of Siegel and Hasse on elliptic units.

</details>


### [196] [One continuum class of fractal functions defined in terms of $Q^*_s$-representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28598)
*V. V. Nazarchuk,S. O. Vaskevych,S. P. Ratushniak*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究基于多参数s-adic Q*_s表示的函数类f_a的连续性、值集拓扑类型和水平集结构性质


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究基于多参数s-adic Q*_s表示的特殊函数类的分析性质，包括连续性、值集拓扑结构和水平集特征，这类函数在非标准数系表示理论中具有重要意义

Method: 定义基于多参数s-adic Q*_s表示的函数f_a，其中Q*_s表示由正随机矩阵生成。研究函数在Q*_s二进制和一元数集上的连续性，分析值集的拓扑类型，并基于参数a的数字表示描述水平集的结构性质

Result: 证明函数在具有唯一Q*_s表示的数集上连续；除f_0和f_1外，所有函数在Q*_s二进制点处有可数间断点集；值集拓扑类型取决于参数a；康托型值集是零维的；水平集可以是空集、有限集或连续统；对某些s值给出分形水平集示例并计算其分形维数

Conclusion: 基于多参数s-adic Q*_s表示的函数类具有丰富的分析性质，其连续性、值集拓扑结构和水平集特征与参数a的数字表示密切相关，为研究非标准数系表示下的函数分析提供了理论框架

Abstract: In the paper we study a class $F$ of multiparameter functions defined in terms of a polybasic $s$-adic $Q^{*}_{s}$-representation of numbers by \begin{equation*} f_a\bigl(x=Δ^{Q^{*}_s}_{α_1α_2\ldotsα_n\ldots}\bigr) = Δ^{Q^{*}s}_{|a_1-α_1|\,|a_2-α_2|\,\ldots\,|a_n-α_n|\ldots}, \end{equation*} where $(a_n)$ is the sequence of digits for $s$-adic representation of the parameter $a\in[0,1]$, and \begin{equation*} Δ^{Q^{*}_s}_{α_1α_2\ldotsα_n\ldots}= β_{α_1 1}+ \sum_{n=2}^{\infty} \left( β_{α_n n} \prod_{j=1}^{n-1} q_{α_j j} \right) \end{equation*} is the $Q^{*}_{s}$-representation of real numbers generated by a positive stochastic matrix $\|q_{ij}\|$ with $β_{α_n n}=\sum\limits_{i=0}^{α_n-1} q_{in}$. In this paper we investigate the continuity of the function $f_a$ on the sets of $Q^{*}_{s}$-binary and $Q^{*}_{s}$-unary numbers. We prove that the functions in this class are continuous on the set of numbers with a unique $Q^{*}_{s}$-representation. Furthermore, we show that except for $f_0$ and $f_1$, all functions have a countable set of discontinuities at $Q^{*}_{s}$-binary points. We classify the topological types of the value sets of $f_a$ depending on the parameter $a$. We prove that, if the value set is of Cantor type, then it is zero-dimensional. We describe the structural properties of the level sets of $f_a$ in terms of the digits of the $s$-adic representation of $a$. In particular, we establish that a level set of the function $f_a$ can be an empty set, a finite set, or a continuum. For certain values of $s$ we provide examples of fractal level sets and calculate its fractal dimensions.

</details>


### [197] [Numeral systems with non-zero redundancy and their applications in the theory of locally complex functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28606)
*S. O. Vaskevych,Yu. Yu. Vovk,O. M. Pratsiovytyi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究实数在基a>1、字母表A={0,1,...,r}（其中a-1<r∈N）的冗余数字系统中的表示，分析其几何拓扑性质，并研究从基(r+1)表示到r_a表示的函数f的连续性和分形特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究冗余数字系统中实数表示的非唯一性问题，特别是当字母表大小r超过基a-1时，数字在区间[0, r/(a-1)]上具有多种表示甚至连续集表示的情况。探索这种冗余表示系统的几何拓扑性质及其在函数分析中的应用。

Method: 使用圆柱集Δ^{r_a}_{c_1c_2...c_m}来定义和分析r_a表示。研究从基(r+1)表示到r_a表示的函数f(x)=Δ^{r_a}_{α_1α_2...α_n...}，其中x=∑α_n/(r+1)^n。分析该函数的连续性、单调性和变分性质，并在特殊参数下研究其分形水平集的豪斯多夫-贝西科维奇维数。

Result: 证明函数f在区间[0,1]中具有唯一经典(r+1)基表示的点处连续，但在[0,1]的可数稠密集上不连续。函数无处单调且具有无界变差。在特殊参数r=1、a=(1+√5)/2时，分形水平集的豪斯多夫-贝西科维奇维数不小于-log_a2。

Conclusion: 冗余数字系统r_a表示具有丰富的几何拓扑结构，相应的函数f表现出复杂的分析性质：混合连续性、无处单调性、无界变差和分形特性。这些结果为理解非标准数字系统及其相关函数提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper we study representations of real numbers in a numeral system with the base $a>1$ and alphabet (digits set) $A\equiv\{0,1,...,r\}$, $a-1<r\in N$ given by \[x=\sum\limits_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{α_n}{a^n}\equiv Δ^{r_a}_{α_1α_2...α_n...}, α_n\in A.\] Since the alphabet is redundant the numbers from the interval $[0;\frac{r}{a-1}]$ have not a single representation and can even have a continuous set of different representations.
  We describe the geometry (topological and metric properties) of such representations (the $r_a$-representations) in terms of cylinders defined by \[Δ^{r_a}_{c_1c_2...c_m}= \{x: x=Δ^{r_a}_{c_1c_2...c_ma_1a_2...a_n...}, a_n\in A\},\] We analyze their properties in detail, including the specific nature of overlaps.
  We present results on the structural, variational, topological, metric and partially fractal properties of the function defined by \[f\left(x=\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{α_n}{(r+1)^n}\right)= Δ^{r_a}_{α_1α_2...α_n...},α_n \in A.\] We prove the function is continuous at all points of the interval $[0,1]$ that have a unique representation in the classical numeral system on the base $r+1$ and prove the function is discontinuous at points of a countable everywhere dense set in $[0,1]$. Furthermore, we show that the function is nowhere monotonic and has unlimited variation.
  In the particular case $r=1$ and $a=\frac{1+\sqrt{5}}{2}$, we specify fractal level sets with Hausdorff--Besicovitch dimension not less than $-\log_a2$.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Ergodicity breaking in matrix-product-state effective Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26870)
*Andrew Hallam,Jared Jeyaretnam,Zlatko Papić*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: DMRG有效哈密顿量可作为超越精确对角化的大系统中量子热化及其破坏的光谱探针


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统的热化及其破坏由中谱本征态决定，但这些态通常只能通过精确对角化在小系统中获得。需要开发能够研究大系统远离平衡动力学的工具。

Method: 利用密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）有效哈密顿量，该对象通常用于变分近似基态，但本文证明它编码了远离平衡动力学的详细信息。在随机场XXZ自旋链中，通过分析有效哈密顿量的谱来研究热化到多体局域化的转变。

Result: DMRG有效哈密顿量的谱能够捕捉从热化到多体局域化区域的转变，包括对遍历气泡的空间分辨探测。该方法还能捕捉与量子多体疤痕相关的弱遍历性破坏。

Conclusion: DMRG有效哈密顿量可作为研究大系统中量子热化及其破坏的通用光谱探针，超越了精确对角化的限制。

Abstract: Thermalization and its breakdown in interacting quantum many-body systems are governed by mid-spectrum eigenstates, which are typically accessible only in small system sizes amenable to exact diagonalization. Here we demonstrate that the density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) effective Hamiltonian, an object routinely used to variationally approximate ground states, encodes detailed information about the dynamics far from equilibrium. In the random-field XXZ spin chain, the spectrum of the effective Hamiltonian is shown to capture the transition from thermal to many-body localized regimes, including spatially resolved probes of ergodic bubbles. Furthermore, the same approach also captures weak ergodicity breaking associated with quantum many-body scars. Our results establish the DMRG effective Hamiltonian as a versatile spectral probe of quantum thermalization and its breakdown in large systems beyond exact diagonalization.

</details>


### [199] [Reflections on time-reversal in the Symmetry Topological Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26884)
*Lea E. Bottini,Nick G. Jones*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种将时间反演对称性纳入对称拓扑场论框架的方法，用于分类(1+1)维晶格模型的零温相


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对称拓扑场论方法主要处理幺正对称性，但时间反演对称性作为反幺正算子需要特殊处理。论文旨在扩展SymTFT框架以包含时间反演对称性，从而更全面地分类(1+1)维系统的拓扑相

Method: 采用对称富化的SymTFT方法：从具有内部幺正对称性的标准SymTFT出发，通过背景时间反演对称性进行富化。详细分析该富化SymTFT的拓扑边界条件，并研究线算符与弦序参数的关系，特别关注反幺正情况下的微妙之处

Result: 建立了包含时间反演对称性的SymTFT框架，能够表征保持富化对称性的(1+1)维有能隙相。揭示了弦序参数端点电荷与时间反演电荷的关系：仅当端点算符为厄米算符时两者才一致。分析了揭示Klein瓶SPT不变量的幺正弦序参数

Conclusion: 成功将时间反演对称性纳入SymTFT框架，为分类(1+1)维系统中包含反幺正对称性的拓扑相提供了系统方法。该方法在晶格和连续场论层面都得到了验证，特别澄清了反幺正对称性下弦序参数的微妙性质

Abstract: Symmetry under time-reversal appears in the microscopic description of many physical systems. In a quantum mechanical setting it acts as an anti-unitary operator, so does not fall under general analyses based on unitary symmetries. In classifying zero temperature phases of matter in (1+1)d lattice models, the role of anti-unitary symmetries is, however, well-understood. In recent years, the Symmetry Topological Field Theory (SymTFT) approach to this classification has given a general framework to understand symmetries as topological defects, but does not naturally include anti-unitary symmetries. Following recent proposals in the literature, we adopt a symmetry-enriched SymTFT for a theory with both internal and time-reversal symmetry. In particular, we take a standard SymTFT associated with an internal unitary symmetry that is then enriched by a background time-reversal symmetry. A detailed analysis of the topological boundary conditions of this enriched SymTFT allows us to characterize the corresponding (1+1)d gapped phases that preserve the enriching symmetry (i.e. those that do not spontaneously break this symmetry in the ground state). Line operators in the SymTFT approach are related to non-local string-order parameters (with charged end-point operators) for SPT phases. These are subtle in the anti-unitary case and we explore them both on the lattice and in the continuum. We include an analysis of unitary string order parameters that reveal the Klein bottle SPT invariant. On the lattice, we show that the correct end-point charge coincides with the time-reversal-charge only when the end-point operator is hermitian.

</details>


### [200] [Nonequilibrium from Equilibrium: Chiral Current-Carrying States in the Spin-1 Babujian-Takhtajan Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26897)
*Bahar Jafari-Zadeh,Chenan Wei,Hrachya M. Babujian,Tigran A. Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究自旋-1 Babujian-Takhtajan链受其第三守恒荷Q₃形变的影响，发现Q₃是无量纲能量流，其局域密度是修饰的标量手征算符而非裸手征。形变Hₐ=H+αQ₃提供局域可精确求解的电流偏置，在α_c=J/(8π)处发生量子相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自旋-1 Babujian-Takhtajan链在受其第三守恒荷Q₃形变下的物理行为，探索如何通过局域可精确求解的电流偏置实现高能电流携带态成为倾斜问题的基态，并分析相关的量子相变现象。

Method: 推导第三守恒荷Q₃，证明其为无量纲能量流，其局域密度是修饰的标量手征算符。构建形变哈密顿量Hₐ=H+αQ₃，使用热力学Bethe ansatz分析，并通过密度矩阵重整化群(DMRG)验证解析计算结果。

Result: 在α_c=J/(8π)处发现量子相变：α<α_c时，基态保持未形变的Babujian-Takhtajan相，低能有效场论为k=2的SU(2) WZW模型(c=3/2，⟨Q₃⟩=0)；α>α_c时，系统进入无能隙手征电流携带相(c=3/2 CFT)，自由能随α-α_c二次变化，能量流线性开启，标量手征性同时开启。

Conclusion: 形变Hₐ=H+αQ₃提供了一种局域可精确求解的电流偏置机制，能够将高能电流携带态重新排序为基态，在α_c处发生量子相变，产生同时携带电流和手征性的后临界相。实验实现途径包括光晶格中的复合自旋-1玻色子和基于囚禁离子或超导电路的可编程qutrit模拟器。

Abstract: We study the spin-$1$ Babujian-Takhtajan chain deformed by its third conserved charge $Q_3$. We derive $Q_3$ and show that it is a dimensionless energy current and that its local density is a dressed scalar-chirality operator rather than bare chirality alone, as is the case for the spin-$1/2$ Heisenberg chain. The deformation $H_α=H+αQ_3$ therefore provides a local, exactly solvable current bias: it leaves the eigenstates of the original Hamiltonian unchanged, but reorders them so that selected high-energy current-carrying states become ground states of the tilted problem. Using the thermodynamic Bethe ansatz and confirming the analytical calculations with DMRG, we find a quantum phase transition at $α_c={J}/(8π)$. For $α<α_c$, the ground-state remains the undeformed Babujian-Takhtajan phase whose low-energy effective field theory is described by the $SU(2)$ Wess-Zumino-Witten (WZW) model at level $k=2$ representing a critical phase characterized by a central charge $c=3/2$ and $\langle Q_3\rangle=0$. For $α>α_c$, a finite rapidity interval forms, and the system enters a gapless chiral current-carrying sector described by a $c=3/2$ CFT. Near the threshold, the free energy starts quadratically as a function of $α-α_c$, while the energy current turn on linearly. The scalar chirality turns on at the same threshold, showing that the postcritical sector is simultaneously current-carrying and chiral. The most immediate experimental routes are composite spin-1 bosons in optical lattices, and programmable qutrit simulators based on trapped ions or superconducting circuits.

</details>


### [201] [Non-Fermi liquid behavior in La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ thin films under hydrostatic pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26978)
*Deepak Kumar,Jared Z. Dans,Keenan E. Avers,Ryan Paxson,Ichiro Takeuchi,Johnpierre Paglione*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 双层镍氧化物La₃Ni₂O₇薄膜在适度静水压下表现出从费米液体到非费米液体行为的可调性转变


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层镍氧化物超导性的发现引发了对这些材料高温超导潜力及其与铜酸盐超导体关系的深入研究。本研究旨在通过薄膜生长和压力调控来探索La₃Ni₂O₇输运性质的演化规律。

Method: 在LaAlO₃(001)和SrLaAlO₄(001)衬底上外延生长La₃Ni₂O₇薄膜，进行高压富氧环境下的退火处理，并在不同静水压力下进行输运测量。

Result: LAO衬底上的薄膜表现出费米液体金属行为，低温下有轻微Kondo型上升；在1.41 GPa适度静水压下，电阻温度依赖性接近T¹·⁴，转变为非费米液体行为。薄膜仅需单晶所需压力的6-8%即可实现类似效应。

Conclusion: La₃Ni₂O₇薄膜形式具有强可调性，适度压力下即可接近强涨落有序态，表现出非费米液体行为，这为理解镍氧化物超导机制提供了重要线索。

Abstract: The discovery of superconductivity in bilayer nickel-oxides has revived an intense effort to understand the potential of high-temperature superconductivity in these materials and their relation to cuprate superconductors. In this work, we investigate the growth and properties of bilayer La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ thin films as a function of substrate, oxygen treatment and applied pressure in order to study the evolution of transport properties. We report epitaxial growth of La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ thin films on LaAlO$_3$ (LAO) (001) and SrLaAlO$_4$ (SLAO) (001) substrates, and the effects of ex-situ annealing in a high pressure furnace under an oxygen-rich environment. Transport measurements show that the La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ thin films on LAO(001) exhibit Fermi liquid-like metallic behavior with a slight Kondo-like upturn at low temperatures, which evolves with the application of modest hydrostatic pressures toward non-Fermi liquid behavior with a temperature dependence of resistance approaching $\sim$ T$^{1.4}$ at 1.41 GPa. The ability to tune the normal state resistivity of La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ films to display non-Fermi liquid behavior under such a modest hydrostatic pressure range - only 6 - 8 % of that typically applied via diamond anvil cell (DAC) in La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ single crystals to achieve comparable effects - is both noteworthy and unexpected. These findings imply the strong tunability of La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ in thin film form and the likely proximity of a strongly fluctuating ordered state leading to non-Fermi liquid behavior under even modest applied pressures.

</details>


### [202] [Electronic structure of higher-order layered palladates: La$_{n+1}$Pd$_{n}$O$_{2n+1}$ $(n = 4-7)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27456)
*Alexander K. Gavrilov,Lidia C. Santander,Antia S. Botana*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过第一性原理计算研究未合成的层状钯酸盐R_{n+1}Pd_nO_{2n+2}的电子结构，发现其比镍酸盐更接近铜酸盐特性，有望成为非常规超导候选材料


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近发现层状镍酸盐R_{n+1}Ni_nO_{2n+2}无需化学掺杂或压力即可实现超导，但尚未研究其类似物钯酸盐的电子结构和超导潜力

Method: 使用第一性原理计算研究尚未合成的层状钯酸盐R_{n+1}Pd_nO_{2n+2}（R=Nd，n=4-7）的电子结构

Result: 层状钯酸盐具有更大的带宽、增强的p-d杂化、以及更少的R-d带在费米能级附近的干扰，这些特性使其比镍酸盐更接近铜酸盐

Conclusion: 钯酸盐的电子结构特性使其成为非常规超导的有前景候选材料，值得在实验合成和超导研究中进一步探索

Abstract: The square-planar layered nickelates R$_{n+1}$Ni$_n$O$_{2n+2}$ (R= Nd, $n=4-7$) have been recently shown to be superconducting without the need for chemical doping or pressure. Here, we examine the electronic structure of the analog higher-order square-planar palladates --that have not yet been synthesized-- via \textit{ab initio} calculations. These layered palladates exhibit larger bandwidths, an increased $p-d$ hybridization, and less interference from R-$d$ bands at the Fermi level. These characteristics make them closer cuprate analogs and promising candidates to pursue in the context of unconventional superconductivity.

</details>


### [203] [Competing interlayer charge order and quantum monopole reorganisation in bilayer kagome spin ice via quantum annealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27826)
*Kumar Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究利用D-Wave Advantage2量子退火器的双层架构实现了首个可编程双层kagome自旋冰，发现了层间耦合驱动的从铁电到反铁电交错电荷序的尖锐相变，并建立了量子自旋冰实验的方法学标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受挫磁体中的磁单极子是典型的分数化准粒子，但现有实验平台无法同时调控其禁闭并保持冰规则物理。本研究旨在解决这一挑战，通过量子退火器实现可编程的双层kagome自旋冰系统。

Method: 利用D-Wave Advantage2量子退火器的原生双层架构，实现了一个包含1,536个逻辑自旋的4×13×14网格系统，通过调控系统尺寸、层间耦合和量子驱动来研究双层kagome自旋冰。

Result: 层间交换驱动了从铁电到反铁电交错电荷序的尖锐相变，形成了Ice-II相（无经典或单层对应物），临界点在(J⊥/J1)*≈0.044处，在五个数量级的退火时间内保持稳定。限制电荷结构因子于冰规则plaquettes显示出比传统全plaquette估计器高一个数量级的增强。

Conclusion: 量子选择的电荷序对缺陷稀释探针不可见，为未来量子自旋冰实验建立了方法学标准。磁单极子化学势的量子重整化为进入单极子去禁闭区域所需的transmon电路-QED kagome冰设定了具体工程目标，并对现有Ni81Fe19纳米线双层架构提出了三个可证伪预测。

Abstract: Magnetic monopoles in frustrated magnets are paradigmatic fractionalised quasiparticles, yet no experimental platform simultaneously tunes their confinement and preserves ice-rule physics. Here we exploit the native bilayer architecture of a D-Wave Advantage2 quantum annealer to realise the first programmable two-plane kagome spin ice, spanning $1{,}536$ logical spins across a $4\times13\times14$ grid of system size, interlayer coupling, and quantum drive. We find that interlayer exchange drives a sharp transition from ferroelectric to antiferroelectric staggered charge order, an Ice-II phase with no classical or single-layer analogue, with a critical onset at $(J_{\perp}/J_1)^{*} \approx 0.044$ that is stable across five decades of annealing time. Restricting the charge structure factor to ice-rule plaquettes reveals an order-of-magnitude enhancement over conventional all-plaquette estimators, demonstrating that quantum-selected charge order is invisible to defect-diluted probes and establishing a methodological standard for future quantum spin ice experiments. The quantum renormalisation of the monopole chemical potential sets a concrete engineering target for the transmon circuit-QED kagome ice required to enter the monopole deconfinement regime. Three falsifiable predictions follow for existing Ni$_{81}$Fe$_{19}$ nanowire bilayer architectures: a critical interlayer separation, an elevated monopole activation temperature, and an order-of-magnitude enhancement of the Ice-II signal in published X-ray datasets, all testable without new fabrication.

</details>


### [204] [Quantum-Coherent Regime of Programmable Dipolar Spin Ice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28125)
*Krzysztof Giergiel,Piotr Surówka*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用超导量子退火器实现可编程偶极方晶格自旋冰模型，首次进入人工自旋冰的量子相干区域，观测到狄拉克弦缺陷和单极子等离子体的实时动力学


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受挫自旋冰系统支持涌现规范场和分数化准粒子（磁单极子），但人工平台虽能直接可视化这些现象，却难以研究其量子相干动力学

Method: 使用超导量子比特量子退火器实现可编程偶极方晶格自旋冰模型，通过晶格自旋与物理量子比特的一对一映射以及工程化扩展耦合，在包含400多个顶点的受挫晶格上实现有效偶极相互作用

Result: 通过调节横向场涨落，观测到狄拉克弦缺陷和相互作用单极子等离子体的实时动力学，发现单极子传输呈现超扩散行为，标度指数介于经典扩散和弹道运动之间

Conclusion: 研究结果表明可编程量子自旋冰可作为可扩展平台，用于研究工程量子物质中的分数化激发和涌现规范动力学

Abstract: Frustrated spin-ice systems support emergent gauge fields and fractionalized quasiparticles that act as magnetic monopoles. Although artificial platforms have enabled their direct visualization, access to their quantum-coherent dynamics has remained limited. Here we realize a programmable dipolar square spin-ice model using a superconducting-qubit quantum annealer, providing access to a previously unexplored quantum-coherent regime of artificial spin ice. By implementing a direct one-to-one mapping between lattice spins and physical qubits, together with engineered extended couplings, we realize effective dipolar interactions on frustrated lattices comprising more than 400 vertices. Tuning transverse-field fluctuations enables us to probe the real-time dynamics of Dirac-string defects and interacting monopole plasmas. We observe super-diffusive monopole transport, with scaling exponents intermediate between classical diffusion and ballistic motion, indicating dynamics beyond classical stochastic relaxation and consistent with coherent propagation within an emergent gauge manifold. These results establish programmable quantum spin ice as a scalable platform for investigating fractionalized excitations and emergent gauge dynamics in engineered quantum matter.

</details>


### [205] [Tilted and Twisted Magnetic Moments in the Kitaev Magnet $α$-RuCl$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28146)
*Xiao Wang,Fengfeng Zhu,Markus Braden,Karin Schmalzl,Wolfgang Schmidt,Martin Meven,Erxi Feng,Yinghao Zhu,Alexandre Bertin,Paul Steffens,Yixi Su*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过极化中子衍射确认α-RuCl₃在低温下呈现"倾斜且扭转"的磁矩几何构型，区别于先前基于非极化中子衍射或REXS的模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-RuCl₃作为实现Kitaev量子自旋液体的候选材料备受关注，但其微观哈密顿量仍存在争议，主要源于材料对结构细节的极端敏感性。需要精确确定其低温磁结构以澄清基态性质。

Method: 使用高质量α-RuCl₃单晶，通过非极化和极化中子衍射（包括球面和纵向极化分析）系统研究低温晶体结构和磁结构。极化分析能够无歧义地确定有序磁矩的三维取向，避免了传统方法中由磁畴分布引起的模糊性。

Result: 确认存在尖锐的一级结构相变至菱面体R3空间群，并伴随明显的热滞现象。关键发现是：在zigzag相中，Ru³⁺磁矩相对于六角平面倾斜15.7°，并额外存在-13.8°的面内扭转，形成"倾斜且扭转"的几何构型，与先前报道的模型不同。

Conclusion: 极化中子衍射揭示了α-RuCl₃中独特的"倾斜且扭转"磁矩几何构型，这一发现区分了基态与先前模型，为理解该材料的微观哈密顿量和Kitaev相互作用提供了关键实验依据。

Abstract: The layered honeycomb magnet $α$-RuCl$_3$ has attracted intense scrutiny as a prime candidate for realizing the Kitaev quantum spin liquid, yet a consensus on its microscopic Hamiltonian remains elusive due to the material's extreme sensitivity to structural details. Here, we report a comprehensive reexamination of the low-temperature crystallographic and magnetic structures of high-quality $α$-RuCl$_3$ single crystals using unpolarized and polarized neutron diffraction. We confirm a sharp, first-order structural phase transition to the rhombohedral $R\bar{3}$ space group with a pronounced thermal hysteresis. Crucially, using both spherical and longitudinal neutron polarization analysis, we determine the 3D orientation of the ordered magnetic moment without the ambiguity typically arising from domain distributions. We find that the Ru$^{3+}$ magnetic moments in the zigzag phase are tilted by $15.7^\circ$ out of the hexagonal plane and, remarkably, exhibit an additional in-plane twist of $-13.8^\circ$. This "tilted and twisted" geometry differentiates the ground state from the previously reported models based on unpolarized neutron diffraction or resonant elastic X-ray scattering (REXS) analysis.

</details>


### [206] [Spontaneously formed excitonic density wave with vortex-antivortex lattice in twisted semiconductor bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28164)
*Deguang Wu,Yiran Xue,Baigeng Wang,Rui Wang,D. Y. Xing*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在扭曲半导体双层中发现新型激子密度波，具有涡旋-反涡旋晶格相结构，自发破坏时间反演和空间反演对称性，产生激子二极管效应


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子凝聚体通常具有宏观尺度上的均匀相位相干性，但相位因子具有空间依赖性的更奇特量子现象尚未被探索。激子凝聚体能否自发形成具有空间调制相位分布的结构仍是一个开放性问题。

Method: 研究扭曲半导体双层系统中的激子凝聚，分析系统固有的动力学阻挫如何稳定具有非平凡相位图案的激子凝聚体，计算并识别这些非常规凝聚体中的特征杂质诱导态。

Result: 发现新型激子密度波，其相位图案形成涡旋-反涡旋晶格结构。这种状态自发破坏时间反演和空间反演对称性，导致非互易的激子输运（激子二极管效应）。计算了杂质诱导的特征态，为实验检测提供了明确特征。

Conclusion: 扭曲半导体双层中的动力学阻挫能够稳定具有空间调制相位图案的激子凝聚体，形成涡旋-反涡旋晶格结构。这代表了一类在二维半导体中先前未知的关联态，其中激子凝聚体的相位自由度起决定性作用，为探索新型量子现象提供了新平台。

Abstract: Exciton condensation, characterized by uniform phase coherence across macroscopic length scales, has enabled the discovery of a variety of excitonic states, greatly enriching our understanding of correlated many-body physics. More exotic quantum phenomena are anticipated when the phase factor develops spatial dependence. However, whether excitonic condensates with spatially modulated phase profiles can emerge spontaneously remains an open question. In this work, we uncover novel forms of excitonic density waves featuring nontrivial phase patterns in twisted semiconductor bilayers. Remarkably, we show that kinetic frustration inherent to these systems stabilizes excitonic condensates arranged into a vortex-antivortex lattice. This represents a class of correlated states previously unknown in two-dimensional semiconductors, wherein the phase degrees of freedom of exciton condensates play a defining role. Such states spontaneously break both time-reversal and inversion symmetries, leading to non-reciprocal exciton transport, an effect we term the excitonic diode effect. Furthermore, we compute and identify characteristic impurity-induced states in these unconventional condensates, providing distinct signatures for their experimental detection.

</details>


### [207] [Exact $\mathbb{Z}_2$ electromagnetic duality of $\mathbb{Z}_2$ toric code is non-Clifford](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28230)
*Ryohei Kobayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 二维Z2环面码的电磁对偶性无法通过Clifford电路实现精确的内部Z2对称性，必须是非Clifford电路，揭示了电磁对偶性与Clifford层次结构之间的意外联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二维Z2环面码中电磁对偶性（交换电和磁准粒子）的实现方式，特别是探索Clifford电路能否实现精确的内部Z2对称性。

Method: 通过严格数学证明，针对具有粗粒度平移不变性（平移l个晶格单位，l为奇数）的对称性，分析Clifford电路实现电磁对偶性的可能性。

Result: 证明Clifford电磁对偶性无法实现精确的内部Z2对称性；精确的内部Z2电磁对偶性必须是非Clifford的；而通用的内部Clifford实现必然具有Z_{2^m}代数（m≥2）。

Conclusion: 电磁对偶性与Clifford电路的层次结构之间存在意外联系，精确的内部Z2电磁对偶性必须是非Clifford的，这为量子纠错码的对称性实现提供了重要理论限制。

Abstract: The 2D $\mathbb{Z}_2$ toric code admits a global symmetry exchanging electric and magnetic quasiparticles, known as electromagnetic duality. Known realizations include lattice translation symmetry, an exact $\mathbb{Z}_4$ symmetry generated by a Clifford circuit, and an exact $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry generated by a non-Clifford circuit. We show that a Clifford electromagnetic duality cannot realize an exact internal $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry. This is proved rigorously for symmetries with coarse translation invariance by $l$ lattice units for generic odd $l$. Therefore an exact internal $\mathbb{Z}_2$ electromagnetic duality must be non-Clifford, whereas generic internal Clifford realization necessarily has $\mathbb{Z}_{2^m}$ algebra with $m\ge 2$. Our result suggests an unexpected connection between exact electromagnetic duality and Clifford hierarchy of circuits.

</details>


### [208] [Superradiant Charge Density Waves in a Driven Cavity-Matter Hybrid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28432)
*Luka Skolc,Sambuddha Chattopadhyay,Filip Marijanović,Qitong Li,Jonathan Keeling,Benjamin L. Lev,Eugene Demler*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出在掺杂、驱动的过渡金属二硫化物中实现超辐射电荷密度波的新平台，利用纳米光栅实现光腔光子与电子密度涨落的高效耦合


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将超辐射自组织现象从超冷原子气体扩展到固态电子系统，实现电子序的光学连续波控制，但面临光学波长与电子长度尺度不匹配的挑战

Method: 使用纳米光栅产生大面内光学动量的电场，通过激子-极化子过程实现光腔光子与电子密度涨落的高效耦合；采用线性稳定性分析确定超辐射有序阈值并绘制驱动相图

Result: 将光栅周期性与增强的电子密度涨落（如近维格纳结晶）匹配可显著降低所需泵浦强度；建立了量子材料中腔控电子序的新途径

Conclusion: 该平台为实现固态电子系统中的超辐射电荷密度波提供了可行方案，为量子材料中光学控制电子序开辟了新方向

Abstract: Optical cavities enable strong, long-range, light-matter interactions that can drive collective ordering phenomena, such as superradiant self-organization in ultracold atomic gases. Extending these ideas to solid-state electron systems could enable continuous-wave optical control of electronic order, but is impeded by the mismatch between optical wavelengths and electronic length scales. Here, we propose a platform for realizing superradiant charge density waves (sCDWs) in doped, driven transition-metal dichalcogenides coupled to an optical cavity. A nanoscale grating generates electric fields at large in-plane optical momenta, allowing cavity photons to couple efficiently to electronic density fluctuations through exciton-polaron processes. Using a linear-stability analysis, we determine the threshold for superradiant ordering and map out the driven phase diagram. We show that tuning the grating periodicity to match the enhanced electronic density fluctuations - such as those near Wigner crystallization - substantially lowers the required pump intensity. Our results establish a novel route toward cavity-controlled electronic order in quantum materials.

</details>


### [209] [Categorical Time-Reversal Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28720)
*Rui Wen,Sakura Schafer-Nameki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文将反幺正对称性（如时间反演对称性）纳入范畴对称性框架，提出Galois实融合范畴作为反幺正对称性的正确范畴模型，并应用于有隙相分类和对称拓扑场论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于范畴对称性的相分类研究主要局限于具有复系数幺正（高阶）范畴对称性的相，缺乏对反幺正对称性（如时间反演对称性）的系统处理。需要建立能够描述反幺正对称性的范畴理论框架。

Method: 引入实融合范畴，特别是Galois实融合范畴作为反幺正对称性的范畴模型。通过群论范畴(G^T)^ω和Rep(G^T)等具体例子说明，并建立基于Galois实融合范畴模范畴的有隙相分类理论。同时发展对称拓扑场论框架，将Galois实融合范畴视为Z_2^T-富化SymTFT的边界条件。

Result: 建立了反幺正对称性的系统范畴描述框架，证明了反线性对称性的对偶性（规范或Morita等价），并展示了Morita等价的反线性对称性作为同一Z_2^T-富化SymTFT的不同边界出现。

Conclusion: Galois实融合范畴为描述反幺正对称性提供了正确的范畴模型，扩展了范畴对称性在相分类中的应用范围，建立了反线性对称性与对称拓扑场论之间的深刻联系，为研究具有时间反演等对称性的物理系统提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: The classification of phases using categorical symmetries has greatly expanded the landscape of gapped and gapless phases. So far, however, these developments have largely been restricted to phases with unitary (higher-)categorical symmetries over $\mathbb{C}$. In this work, we incorporate anti-unitary symmetries, such as time-reversal symmetry $\mathbb{Z}_2^T$, and show that the relevant physical structures are naturally described by fusion categories over $\mathbb{R}$. A class of real fusion categories, which we call Galois-real fusion categories, provides the correct categorical model for anti-unitary symmetries. A simple example is the time-reversal symmetry $\mathbb{Z}_2^T$ itself. We discuss the basic structures of real fusion categories and present a range of examples, including the group-theoretical categories $(G^T)^ω$ and $\mathsf{Rep}(G^T)$ associated to anti-linear groups $G^T$, as well as non-invertible time-reversal symmetries described by a real analogue of Tambara--Yamagami fusion categories. We then classify gapped phases enriched with anti-linear symmetries in terms of module categories over Galois-real fusion categories. We furthermore apply the categorical formulation to prove dualities (i.e. gauge or Morita equivalences) of anti-linear symmetries generated by gauging subgroups. Complementing this, we also develop a Symmetry Topological Field Theory (SymTFT) framework, in which Galois-real fusion categories arise as boundary conditions of a $\mathbb{Z}_2^T$-enriched SymTFT. Morita equivalent anti-linear symmetries are shown to arise as different boundaries of the same $\mathbb{Z}_2^T$-enriched SymTFT.

</details>

### [210] [When can fitness epistasis be ignored in a polygenic trait at equilibrium?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27255)
*Archana Devi,Kavita Jain*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了多基因性状在连锁平衡下，受稳定选择和对称突变影响的有限二倍体群体中，等位基因频率的分布行为，特别关注上位性选择的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管许多表型性状由大量遗传变异决定，但多基因性状中等位基因频率的行为尚未完全理解。当数量性状受上位性选择影响时，问题尤其复杂，因为一个位点的等位基因频率会受到其他位点的影响。

Method: 使用扩散理论计算连锁平衡下，受稳定选择和对称突变影响的二倍体有限群体在稳态时的等位基因频率边际分布。通过蒙特卡洛模拟和朗之万方程的数值积分验证分析结果。

Result: 发现某些参数范围内，忽略上位性相互作用无法准确描述频率分布。然而，表型最优值的平均偏差和基因方差即使忽略上位性相互作用也能很好地捕捉。等位基因频率分布在效应大小低于阈值时为单峰，高于阈值时为双峰，这是确定性结果（稳定等位基因频率在效应大小超过阈值时变为双稳态）的随机类比。

Conclusion: 虽然上位性可能不明显影响表型量，但能强烈影响等位基因频率分布。等位基因频率分布的模式（单峰或双峰）取决于效应大小是否超过阈值，这与确定性理论中的双稳态现象相对应。

Abstract: Although many phenotypic traits are determined by a large number of genetic variants, the behavior of allele frequencies in a polygenic trait is not completely understood. The problem is especially challenging when the quantitative trait of interest is under epistatic selection as the allele frequency at a locus is affected by those at other loci. Here, we consider a panmictic, diploid finite population evolving under stabilizing selection and symmetric mutations when the population is in linkage equilibrium. In the stationary state, using a diffusion theory, we calculate the marginal distribution of allele frequency, and find parameter regimes where fitness epistasis can not be ignored for an accurate description of the frequency distribution. For such parameters, the mean deviation in the phenotypic optimum and genic variance are, however, found to be well captured even when epistatic interactions are neglected. Thus, while the presence of epistasis may not be evident in phenotypic quantities, it can strongly affect the allele frequency distribution.We also find that the allele frequency distribution at a locus is unimodal if its effect size is below a threshold effect and bimodal otherwise; these results are the stochastic analog of the deterministic ones where the stable allele frequency becomes bistable when the effect size exceeds a threshold. Our analytical results are verified against Monte Carlo simulations and numerical integration of a Langevin equation.

</details>
